# Steps M24-M26: the Glassway II, Glassway stories and Moonmere, Kettle Isle

Issue-ready specs for steps S-206 to S-235 of `docs/roadmap/INDEX.md`. The plan job pastes one `### S-…` block
per `agent:improvement` issue. Order of authority: `docs/roadmap/CANON-NOW.md` LOCKED > `docs/roadmap/CANON.md` > the reconciliation
rows in `docs/roadmap/steps/*.md` (for the topics they name, until the named close-out edits the losing file) > the
bible (`docs/roadmap/bible/*.md`) > the INDEX row > the issue. Where two bible files disagreed, the choice made here is listed in
[Bible reconciliations](#bible-reconciliations) at the end, so a bible writer can fold it back in. Splits are listed
in [Index changes](#index-changes).

Bible links below are relative to `docs/roadmap/` (the repo path of these files once S-001 copies them).

---

## M24 · The Glassway II: fishing, the Starsong and the Sunken Bell

- **Vision Roadmap item:** **M24 · The Glassway II: fishing, the Starsong and the Sunken Bell** `[agent-feasible]`.
- **Goal.** Fishing with Nell across every shipped land, the Starsong (the seventh tool), the Sunken Bell (dungeon 7),
  Pelagia's bell-order duel, and the Stair Stone count beat that raises the first Starstair flight and opens memories
  4-6.
- **Entry.** M23 closed: the Glassway (`glassway`, seed 40253) is unhidden (S-203) with free sailing or the chart-stop
  fallback (S-198/S-199, save v10); its finds are placed (S-205), including the inert Spiral Skerry star-door chest
  `glassway:c08`, the `bell_door` anchor at the spire foot and the two Hollow door anchors; `ch14` is set by
  `mq-ch14` and its chapter test is green. Bubble Puffer and Gullgrab ship (S-204).
- **Exit (what the player can do).** Pip gets Nell's spare rod and fishes 18 ripple spots in 7 lands (24 species in
  the Fish Log; 21 catchable now, 3 wait for Kettle, Saltglass and Driftholm); plays the Starsong's three tunes (Dawn
  and Dusk turn the clock at a bench, Calling brings Nell or the *Dandelion*); opens star doors at night on home, the
  four isles and Spiral Skerry; follows Pell's puffins to the chapel key, opens the Sunken Bell at low tide, solves its
  11 rooms, wins Pelagia's duel, carries the first Stair Stone home and sets it at the Summit Crystal Cairn: a floating
  flight rises from the needle, crown lantern 5 lights, and memories 4-6 open on the Taproot.
- **Steps (12 issues).** S-206, S-207, S-208, S-209, S-210, S-211, S-212a, S-212b, S-212c, S-212d, S-212e, S-213 (S-212d and S-212e are
  added by this file; any other lettered additions to M24 are listed in [Index changes](#index-changes)).
- **Play added.** +5 h (cumulative 53.5 h): fishing and the Fish Log about 1.5 h, star doors and their finds 0.5 h,
  the Bell's people steps 0.4 h, the Sunken Bell 1.75 h, the duel 0.2 h, memories 4-6 and their walk 0.6 h.
- **Save plan.** No bump; v10 stays. New state goes in `flags` (`stone:bell`, `stoneHeld:bell`, `bellDepths`,
  `gate:<realm>:stardoor-*`, `act.fish.n`, `fish.best:<slug>`, `mem:4`-`mem:6`), `items` (`rod`, `rod_charm`, `starsong`,
  `chapel_key`, `stone_bell`), `quests` (`act-fishing`, `mq-bell`, `mq-remember`), `collect.fish`, `collect.petal`,
  `chests`, `bosses` (`sunken_bell:pelagia`), `dungeons.sunken_bell`.

## M25 · Glassway stories, the Merry Mabel and Moonmere

- **Vision Roadmap item:** **M25 · Glassway stories, the Merry Mabel and Moonmere** `[agent-feasible]`.
- **Goal.** The optional wreck dungeon and its sleeping bosun, four Glassway side stories, Old Friends, the Bell Duel,
  the Glassway's two Hollows and Dash course, and Moonmere, Mote's mirror-home, with his twelve unsent letters.
- **Entry.** M24 closed: the Starsong, the stone framework (S-212a) and `mq-bell` ship; `stone:bell` can be set.
- **Exit (what the player can do).** Pip delivers the Bottle Chain letters to their letterboxes for Pell, trades six
  rare shells and Captain Pinch's childhood shell to Mr Whelk, boards the *Merry Mabel*, calms Bosun Snore and brings
  her bell home to Pinch, moves three gull nests so Hummock can roll over, plays the Bell Duel, sails Shellby out to
  race Pelagia on a sandbar, solves the Puffin Hollow and the Drip Hollow, runs the Glassway Dash at low tide, and at
  night plays *Calling* at the end of Nell's Jetty to walk the moonpath into Moonmere, where he lights twelve
  moon-lanterns, reads Mote's letters, wakes the Mirror Garden and finds bottle 11 on islet 2's beach.
- **Steps (11 issues).** S-214a, S-214b, S-215, S-216a, S-216b, S-217, S-218a, S-218b, S-219, S-220, S-221.
- **Play added.** +4 h (cumulative 57.5 h): the *Merry Mabel* 1.0 h, ss-34/ss-35 0.75 h, ss-36/ss-37 and the Bell Duel
  0.75 h, ss-09 0.4 h, Moonmere and ss-47 0.7 h, two Hollows and the Dash 0.4 h.
- **Save plan.** No bump; v10 stays. Flags (`ss34.*` … `ss37.*`, `ss09.*`, `ss47.*`, `moteLetter:<n>`,
  `gate:moonmere:lantern<n>`), items (`letter_copy_<n>`, `pinch_old_shell`, `ships_bell`, `gull_nest`), quests
  (`ss-09`, `ss-34`-`ss-37`, `ss-47`, `act-bell-duel`), `collect` (`hollow`, `petal`, `dash`, `bottle`), `bosses`
  (`merry_mabel:bosun`), `rematches`, `dungeons.merry_mabel`.

## M26 · Kettle Isle: the forge, the Belly and Starsteel

- **Vision Roadmap item:** **M26 · Kettle Isle: the forge, the Belly and Starsteel** `[agent-feasible]`.
- **Goal.** The forge isle (`kettle`, seed 40261), the Ember Sling (the eighth tool), the Kettle's Belly (dungeon 8),
  Lady Fumarole, a Stair Stone (twist T5 when it is the second), and the Starsteel Sword that cuts thornglass in every
  land.
- **Entry.** M25 closed. The Glassway's Kettle buoy exists as an anchor (S-200) and leads nowhere yet.
- **Exit (what the player can do).** Pip sails through the Glassway's Kettle buoy to Kettle Isle; meets Master Scoria,
  Pitch and Soakley; wins the Ember Sling in the Kettle's Belly and rings the four home landmark bells to open the Bell
  Hollow, and melts the Snowglobe's ice wall (the dungeon behind it is M27); calms Lady Fumarole; sets the Kettle's
  Stair Stone (memories 7-9 and twist T5 when it is the second stone); pays Scoria 600 gems for the Starsteel Sword
  and cuts thornglass thickets on home, the four isles, the Rootdeep, the Glassway and Kettle Isle; solves the Cinder
  and Kettle-Steam Hollows, runs the Kettle Dash and Rook's fourth race.
- **Steps (19 issues).** S-222, S-223a, S-223b, S-224, S-225, S-226, S-227a, S-227b, S-228, S-229, S-230, S-231,
  S-232a, S-232b, S-232c, S-233a, S-233b, S-234, S-235.
- **Play added.** +5 h (cumulative 62.5 h): Kettle Isle's land and people 1.2 h, the Belly 1.75 h, Fumarole 0.2 h,
  memories 7-9 0.4 h, Bell Hollow and home bells 0.3 h, Starsteel and the thornglass round-trip 0.7 h, Hollows, Dash
  and Rook 0.45 h.
- **Save plan.** **v10 → v11 in S-233a only** (`MIGRATIONS[10]`, identity on data; `normalize` clamps `swordLevel` to
  1-3). Everything else in flags, items, quests, collect and the per-id maps.

## Conventions every step below uses

These are the names the steps rely on. Each was introduced by an earlier step; if the shipped code chose a different
name, use the shipped one and say so in the PR body (never rename shipped code to match this file).

| Thing | Name used here | Introduced by |
|---|---|---|
| Realm registry, generator | `js/realms/index.js` (`REALMS`, `generateRealm`), `js/realms/recipes/<recipe>.js`, `RealmWorld` | S-018 |
| Realm swap | `js/realms/manager.js` `RealmManager.enter(id, spawn, how)`, `EV.REALM` | S-021 |
| Post-pass helpers | `postReach()` (S-016), `js/world-post.js` `attachPost(W)` (S-004), `W.portals` records (S-026) | as listed |
| Conditions / effects | `js/cond.js` `test(S, cond)`, `apply(S, effects)` | S-025 |
| Flags registry | `js/content/flags.js` `FLAGS` (key regex `/^[A-Za-z0-9:_.-]{1,48}$/`) | S-003, S-025 |
| Text and dialogue | `js/text/en/<short>.js` (`gw` Glassway, `ke` Kettle, `mm` Moonmere, `home`), `t(id, vars)`; `js/content/dialogue/<realm>.js` entries `{id, npc, when, lines, set, once, priority}` | S-032 |
| Scenes | `js/scenes.js` runner, `js/content/scenes/<realm>.js` data, seven step kinds of `story.md#scene-format` | S-034 |
| Items, abilities, gates | `js/items.js` `ITEMS`; `js/abilities.js` `ABILITIES`; `js/gates.js` gate registry; `js/progress-graph.js` solver | S-035, S-048 |
| Quests and journal | `js/quests.js`, `js/content/quests/<realm>.js`, `js/hud/journal.js` (Field Notes) | S-036, S-037 |
| Dungeons | `js/realms/dungeon-compile.js` (compiler + solver), `js/realms/recipes/dungeon.js`, `js/dungeon-mod.js`, `js/props-interior.js`, data in `js/realms/dungeons/<id>.js`, Hollows in `js/realms/hollows/<id>.js` | S-049-S-053, S-073 |
| Bosses | `js/boss/defs.js` + `js/boss/<id>.js`, move runner, `hp(def, S)`, `def.handover/plushie/rematch` | S-043-S-045 |
| Species | `ENEMY[id]` entries, `js/ai/<archetype>.js`, `enemyDef('<id>')` rig in `js/models.js` | S-040-S-042 |
| NPC looks | `js/content/looks.js` `NPC_LOOKS[id] = { rig, h, pal, acc }` read by one `npcDef` case per rig family | S-088 (first family) |
| Kits | `js/props-lamplighter.js` (bench, beacon, ruins, log niche, Hollow door, star door, root-gate) | S-082 |
| Benches, chart | `js/travel.js` | S-080 |
| Boat | the boat module of S-198/S-199 (`REALMS.glassway.sail = 'free' \| 'chart'`, `S.boat`) | S-198, S-199 |
| Tide | flag `tide:glassway` (0 low, 1 high, absent = high), flipped by the Tide Bell at tide stones (S-122, S-200) | S-200 |
| Coverage test | `js/realms/coverage.js` + `tests/coverage.test.js` | S-086 |
| Completion totals | `js/realms/stats.js` generated by `tools/realm-stats.mjs`; `tests/stats.test.js` compares | S-074 |
| Economy | `js/content/economy.js`, `tests/economy.test.js` | S-078 |
| Chapter tests | `node tests/chapter.mjs <id>`, specs `tests/fixtures/chapters/<id>.json`, starts `tests/fixtures/saves/<id>-start.json` | S-030 |

**In-page suites named below** (each a file `js/debug/tests/<suite>.js` registered in `js/debug/tests.js` and listed in
`.github/workflows/ci.yml`'s in-page matrix, like `wp1`-`wp9`): `realm` (S-022), `tools` (tool and gate tests,
S-048), `dungeons` (S-052), `bosses` (S-043), `species` (S-041), `activities` (S-112), `side` (side stories, S-077).
A step that adds a test to one of them uses the suite as the earlier steps named it.

**Debug hooks** used below: `G.debug.loadSave`, `flags.get/set`, `scene.skip/play`, `realm(id, spawn)`,
`warp`/`warpTo(anchor)`, `give(item, n)`, `ability(id)`, `quest.set/advance`, `killBoss(key)`, `actors.spawn/boss`,
`tod(h)`, `freezeTime`, `schedules(false)`, `hud.audit()`, `perf.views(realm)` (systems.md#test-harness). New hooks
are named in the step that adds them.

**The invariants block.** Every step's acceptance criteria paste `charter.md#invariants-block` with the lines that
apply (each step names which ones to keep). It is not repeated in full below.

**Machine rule for every verification plan.** Run `free -h` first; run one heavy job at a time (`bun test` is light;
any `tests/*.mjs` launches a browser of 4-5 GB). Stop every server and browser started.

## Bible reconciliations

Where two bible files disagree on something these steps build, the reading below is the one the step uses. A bible
writer folds it back into the losing file; the implementer does not choose again.

| # | Topic | Files in conflict | Reading used here | Why |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| R1 | Star doors' tune | atlas-home#star-doors says *Calling*; systems#gates, systems#tool-starsong, atlas-far c08 and atlas-isles say *Dusk* | **Dusk**, at night (21:00-04:59). *Calling* stays the boat/moonpath tune | the gate registry owns gate rules; four files against one |
| R2 | Gate flag spelling | atlas-home uses `find:home:stardoor-<lm>`, `find:home:ice-wall`, `bell:home:<lm>`; systems#gates says `gate:<realm>:<id>` | `gate:home:stardoor-<lm>`, `gate:home:icewall`, `gate:home:bell-<lm>`, `gate:<isle>:stardoor` (M27's S-238 already reads `gate:home:icewall`) | one prefix for every gate opened for good |
| R3 | Fish spots | side-content#act-fishing names 18 spot codes (`h1`…`d1`); atlas-home, atlas-isles and atlas-far place 21 ripple spots (home 4, isles 2 each, Rootdeep 2, Glassway 4, Kettle 1, Saltglass 1, Driftholm 1) | every placed ripple spot carries one **water code** from side-content's 18; two spots may share a code (home `fish-cave` shares `h3`, Rootdeep `fish-2` shares `r1`, the Glassway's wreck spot shares `g2`); species roll by code. Positions are the atlases'. Home `h1` is the **Meadow pond** (−70.6, 21.8): the village pond is the ducks' (Regatta, Ribbit's lily pad) | "exact positions are each atlas's" (side-content); 24 species and their waters stay as written |
| R4 | `collect.fish` value | side-content: biggest size in cm; systems#save-plan: number caught | `collect.fish['fish:<slug>']` = number caught (1-999, systems); biggest size in numeric flag `fish.best:<slug>` (cm) | systems owns the save shape |
| R5 | Nell's tank | atlas-home#interiors: "empty frame until Fishing tier 3"; side-content and systems#collectibles: the tank is the Fish Log's home and fills as species are caught | Nell's tank shows every species caught from the first catch; tier 3 gives Pip a **second, small** tank (`nook_gift_tank`) | collections need a home from the first find (research rule, CANON §10.3) |
| R6 | Starsong item-get line | dungeons#d7 r7 maps {attack}/{jump}/{dash} to low/mid/high; systems#items-tools maps Roll = low, Hop = mid, Swing = high | systems; the line is rewritten in S-210 | dungeons decision 25: systems wins on control prompts |
| R7 | Pelagia's duel bells | creatures#boss-pelagia: posts 4 m from the centre; dungeons#d7 r11 map: tiles (16,33), (20,34), (24,33) | the map's tiles (S-049's map test pins them); colours, pitches and shapes from creatures (teal shell G3, gold star C4, coral moon E4). The puzzle bells in r1-r10 keep dungeons#d7's rose/gold/sky and G3/B3/D4 | the distance does not change the duel |
| R8 | Boss looks | cast.md and creatures.md ART BRIEFs differ (Pelagia's shell hexes; Bosun Snore's cap vs woolly hat and 2.4 vs 3.0 m; Fumarole's towel colours) | **cast.md** owns looks, palettes and sizes; **creatures.md** owns arenas, moves, numbers and lines | cast is referenced by portraits and the young cast |
| R9 | Memory 4-9 staging | atlas-home#remembered-isle's memory table vs lore.md#mem-04…#mem-09 (spawns, hours) | **lore.md** (spawn, hour, lines, staging); atlas-home only for the Remembered Isle's dressing | lore.md is the text owner and story.md links it |
| R10 | Moonmere unhide | INDEX S-218 "opened by Calling"; systems#realm-registry "unhidden by S-219" | S-218 builds the realm and its portal behind `REALMS.moonmere.hidden = true`; S-219 adds the letters and unhides it | the player meets Moonmere with its point (the letters) already in it |
| R11 | Kettle dungeon id | INDEX writes `dungeons/kettle_belly.js` | realm id and file **`kettles_belly`** (systems#realm-registry; boss key `kettles_belly:fumarole`, which M27's S-236 reads) | systems fixes ids so saves, portals and tests agree |
| R12 | Bell Hollow tune | atlas-home#bell-targets pitches Woods C4, Peaks G4, Shores E4, Meadow D4 | kept as written | no conflict; listed so the Bell Duel's pitches are not reused by mistake |
| R13 | Stone-give lines | creatures' third handover line repeats story#ch15-17's stone-give lines (Pelagia "Take the clapper…", Fumarole "Mind, it's warm") | handover = creatures' lines 1-2; the stone scene = story's two lines (Pelagia adds "You ring like Shellby did." between them) | each line is said once |
| R14 | Glassway Hollow 18's name | dungeons#hollows "The Bell Hollow" collides with home's `bell_hollow` interior (atlas-home) | shown as **"The Drip Hollow"**; realm id `hollow_glassway_2` unchanged | two places may not share a name (charter#name-check) |
| R15 | Bell Duel | side-content: on "the Sunken Bell's top deck", sequence lengths only; creatures: a 5-round rematch of 4-7 notes | three duel bells on Bell Islet's south shore beside Pelagia; sets A/B/C of 3 rounds with the fixed sequences in S-216a; the first Set A win is her rematch | the activity owner's rules (side-content) win over creatures' summary; fixed data keeps tests stable |

| R17 | ss-37's tide stone | side-content: "the whale's tide stone"; atlas-far places none on Hummock | the Bell Islet stone (30, 240), the nearest | no new stone in a frozen realm |
| R18 | Spoon Point | atlas-far: (150, 70) y 26, "35 m" from the Teacup (it is 57 m) | (162, 92) y 26, a crag with a ledge ramp; 23 m from the Teacup rim | a glide from 10 m up covers ~30 m |

| R21 | Moonmere's hour | systems: 0.00; atlas-home: 00:30 | 00:30 (`tod: 0.0208`) | negligible; the atlas wrote the scene |

| R24 | Starsteel shard (c) | side-content: "the Belly's starsteel chest, contents reassigned by dungeons#d8" (d8 does not) | `r12:c4` gives the shard with its 40 gems; counted as found if that chest was opened before ss-38 | gem-chest count (48) and ledger unchanged; nothing missable |
| R25 | Thornglass prizes | atlas-far decision 31 defers to systems#gates, which names none | 30-gem caches (home's amount) in the Rootdeep (4), the Glassway (3) and Kettle Isle (4) | a prize had to be chosen; the ledger gains 330 + home's 60 |

<a id="s-206"></a>
### S-206 · feat: fishing with Nell: cast, a timed Swing and the Fish Log
Milestone M24 · Depends on: S-071, S-068, S-054, S-033a, S-074 · Size: M (about 460 lines) · Save: none · Flag: none ·
Bible: [side-content#act-fishing](../bible/side-content.md#act-fishing),
[cast#nell](../bible/cast.md#nell), [atlas-home#fish-spots](../bible/atlas-home.md#fish-spots),
[atlas-home#interiors](../bible/atlas-home.md#interiors), [systems#collectibles](../bible/systems.md#collectibles),
[systems#journal](../bible/systems.md#journal), [systems#brief-gates](../bible/systems.md#brief-gates)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M24 · The Glassway II: fishing, the Starsong and the Sunken Bell**
(`Roadmap: docs/roadmap/INDEX.md S-206 (M24 The Glassway II: fishing, the Starsong and the Sunken Bell)`).
Fishing is the game's slow, quiet activity: a reason to stand still by water in every land, a 24-slot collection with
a home (Nell's tank), and Nell's place in the story after the bloom. This step ships the whole mechanic, the Fish Log,
Nell's cottage and the tiers, with the four home spots; S-207 adds the spots on every other shipped land and the
painted icons.

#### What
1. **Species data** `js/content/fish.js` (pure): `export const FISH = [ { id, slug, name, code, when, tide, rarity,
   size: [lo, hi], hex: [a, b], line } ]`, all **24 rows** copied verbatim from
   [side-content#act-fishing](../bible/side-content.md#act-fishing) (name, spot code, "When", rarity, size, colours,
   Nell's line). Ids `fish:<slug>`, slug = name in kebab case (`fish:puddle-minnow` … `fish:skyfin`). Field mapping:
   - `code`: the table's spot column (`h1`, `h2`, `h3`, `c1`, `c2`, `p1`, `p2`, `a1`, `a2`, `t1`, `t2`, `r1`, `g1`, `g2`,
     `g3`, `k1`, `s1`, `d1`);
   - `when`: `'any' | 'dawn' | 'day' | 'dusk' | 'night' | [h0, h1]` (`Whiskerdown` is `[0, 3]`: 00:00-02:59);
     windows dawn 05-08, day 08-18, dusk 18-21, night 21-05 (hour h is in a window when lo ≤ h < hi, wrapping);
   - `tide`: `null | 'low' | 'high'` (Mooneye `high`, Kelp Wrasse and Sandbar Flounder `low`); a spot's tide is the
     flag `tide:<realm>` (0 low, 1 high, absent per [systems#flags](../bible/systems.md#flags)); a realm with no tide
     flag counts as high;
   - `rarity`: `common | uncommon | rare` with weights 60 / 30 / 10.
   Export `FISH_BY_CODE` and a pure `eligible(code, hour, tide)` and `roll(rng, code, hour, tide, opts)` that returns
   `{ fish, size, waitS, nibbles, windowS }`: wait 2-8 s uniform (× 0.75 when the fish is rare and `opts.charm`), 0-2
   nibbles evenly spaced in the wait, window 0.6 s (0.45 s rare), size an integer cm uniform in `size`. If no species
   is eligible at a spot (for example h1 at night with only day fish), `roll` falls back to the spot's `any` species,
   and if none, to Puddle Minnow; `eligible` is unit-tested so that **every code has at least one species at every
   hour and tide** (add a test that fails otherwise; with the table as written h3 has only the night goby, so the
   fallback rule is what makes h3 by day give a Puddle Minnow: record that in the test name).
2. **Spots on home** — post-pass `placeFishSpots(W)` in `js/world-post.js` (or the file the earlier home passes live
   in), pass row 19, `new RNG(7151 * 151 + 83)`, rules of [atlas-home#fish-spots](../bible/atlas-home.md#fish-spots):

   | id | code | placement |
   |---|---|---|
   | `home:fish-meadow` | `h1` | the Meadow pond (−70.6, 21.8): `near(pond edge, 0, 3)` on its south shore |
   | `home:fish-woods` | `h2` | the river's source pond (37, −78): `near(pond edge, 0, 3)` on its north-west shore |
   | `home:fish-jetty` | `h3` | the jetty T-head's south edge, facing south |
   | `home:fish-cave` | `h3` | the Storm Cave interior (`storm_cave`, S-122): a pool tile; a record on the interior, not a tile change |

   Each record `{ id, code, x, z, y: waterY, r: 1.5, realm }` goes in a new list `W.fishSpots`; the pass touches no
   other list and restores `W.walk/W.reach` (the `placeShells` contract). `G.debug.fish.spots()` lists them.
3. **Mechanic** `js/fishing.js` (`FishingMod`, registered in the module list like other gameplay modules):
   - Interactable per spot: `[E] Fish · Ripples` when Pip owns `rod`, is within 2.5 m of the ring edge and faces it
     (FP: the ring under the crosshair; TP: the 35° facing cone). Without the rod, `[E] Look · Ripples` "Something
     plips about under the water."
   - State machine `idle → cast (0.8 s) → wait (waitS, nibbles) → bite (windowS) → land | miss`:
     - **cast:** the rod swings out; the float lands in the ring; input lock off (Pip may look around).
     - **wait:** each nibble bobs the float 0.05 m with a tiny ring (no sound, nothing to do). A **Swing** now is too
       early.
     - **bite:** the float dips 0.15 m, a splash sprite, a "!" glint over the float, `sfx.fishBite` and a 120 ms pad
       rumble. A Swing inside the window **lands** the fish.
     - **miss** (too early, too late): toast "It got away!" (`nell.fish.miss`), the ring fades for 10 s, then returns.
       Nothing is lost.
     - Moving more than 0.4 m, rolling, opening a menu, or taking a hit reels in: toast "Reeled in." No penalty.
   - **Land:** a centred card for 2.2 s (or until {interact}): icon (atlas cell `fish:<slug>` from S-207; until then
     the `F-canvas` fallback, a round fish in the species' first hex with an eye and a tail), name, "<size> cm", the
     species' Nell line in her frame, footer "Back you go." Then `collect.fish[id] = min(999, n + 1)`, and when `size`
     beats `flags['fish.best:<slug>']`, set it. A first catch toasts "✿ New fish: {species}! (n/24)" (systems#collectibles)
     and emits `EV.FISH {id, size, first}` (the quest event of systems#quests).
   - **Viewmodel:** FP: the sword view-model is hidden while fishing; the code-built rod (`held_rod`, below) shows in
     its place, with a 1-draw line from the tip to the float. TP: the rod is parented to the same hand socket as the
     sword (Pip's GLB `armR` node from PR #24), the sword hidden; the GLB is not modified.
   - **Cue:** within **12 m** of a spot, a soft "plip" every 4-7 s (`sfx.plip`, positional) and the ring's sprite
     (`fx_fish_ripple`): the visual twin of the sound.
   - Spots are blocked during fights (a live attack token within 15 m): prompt hidden.
   - **Determinism for tests:** every cast draws from `new RNG(hash(spotId, castCount))`; `G.debug.fish.force({id,
     size, waitS})` forces the next roll.
4. **Nell gives the rod.** Dialogue entry in `js/content/dialogue/home.js`: `{ id: 'nell.fish.give', npc: 'nell',
   when: ['flag:wennaAwake', '!item:rod'], priority: 60, once: true, lines: ['nell.fish.1'], set: ['item:rod',
   'quest:act-fishing.start'] }` with item-get "You got Nell's Rod! Watch the float. When it dips, Swing." Item row in
   `js/items.js`: `rod: { name: "Nell's Rod", kind: 'key', icon: 'rod', desc: 'Her spare. Watch the float, not the
   fish.' }`, `rod_charm: { name: 'Rod Charm', kind: 'upgrade', icon: 'rod_charm', desc: 'A leaf-shaped float. Rare
   fish bite a little sooner.' }`, `nook_gift_tank: { name: 'A Little Fish Tank', kind: 'furnishing', icon:
   'fish_tank', desc: 'Every fish you ever met, swimming in circles. Gift slot in Pip\'s Nook.' }`.
5. **Activity quest** `js/content/quests/home.js`: `{ id: 'act-fishing', n: 203, kind: 'activity', owner: 'nell',
   title: 'act.fishing.title' ("Fishing with Nell"), steps: [ {id: 'catch', text: 'act.fishing.s1' ("Catch a fish.
   Any fish."), done: 'collect.fish>=1'}, {id: 'sixteen', text: 'act.fishing.s2' ("Catch sixteen kinds of fish."),
   done: 'collectKinds:fish>=16'}, {id: 'twenty', text: … "twenty kinds", done: '…>=20'}, {id: 'all', text: … "all
   twenty-four kinds", done: '…>=24'} ] }`. (`n` 203 is the activity's place in side-content §6's index: dash 201,
   vistas 202, fishing 203 … steam hop 212; if S-112 numbered activities another way, keep its scheme.) Hints: nudge
   "Nell knows every pond." / answer "Ripples with a soft plip are fishing spots. Nell's tank shows what you've caught."
6. **Tiers** (paid on the next Talk with Nell, priority 70, each `once`):

   | Kinds caught | Line (`js/text/en/home.js`) | Reward |
   |---|---|---|
   | 16 | `nell.fish.2` "Sixteen kinds! Here, a petal from my window box. Don't ask why I keep petals. Sailors are like that." | heart petal `collect.petal['act-fishing']` (the S-072 petal effect) |
   | 20 | `nell.fish.3` "Twenty. Here, a float for your rod, shaped like a leaf. The rare ones like a bit of style." | item `rod_charm` (rare fish wait × 0.75; a leaf float `#8fd14f` replaces the red one) |
   | 24 | `nell.fish.4` "All twenty-four. My tank's full and so's my heart. Take a little tank for your Nook. You'll want a bigger one, eventually." | item `nook_gift_tank` (S-302's gift slots display it; until then it shows in the Items tab) |

   Also `nell.fish.miss` "It got away!", `nell.fish.hint` (idle at the jetty, after `rod`) "Ripples and a plip. That's
   where they are. Fish don't hide well. They think they do."
7. **Fish Log** in Field Notes, Items tab: a new section "Fish Log" (kind `collectible-view`) with 24 slots in table
   order: found rows show the icon, name, "Caught × n", "Best: <cm> cm", the water ("Starseed Isle · a pond") and Nell's
   line; unfound rows show "???" and the slot number. The Completion row "Fish" (24) already exists from S-074's
   stats; regenerate `js/realms/stats.js` with `tools/realm-stats.mjs` so its total reads 24.
8. **Nell's cottage** interior (cottage 2), from [atlas-home#interiors](../bible/atlas-home.md#interiors):
   realm id per the scheme S-115 used for cottage 1 (`cottage_nell` in systems#realm-registry; `home_cottage_2` in
   atlas-home: keep the shipped scheme), card "Nell's Cottage" / "Smells of the sea", the shared cottage map, fixed
   pieces hammock (4, 1)-(5, 1), net-and-float wall, chart table (2, 2); NPC spot `home.nell_tank` at the chart table
   (evenings, per Nell's schedule); **the fish tank** on the west wall (1, 2)-(1, 3): a 2.0 × 1.0 × 1.2 m glass tank
   (code-built, `cottage_kit` piece) in which every caught species swims as its icon on a small card (one instanced
   draw, ≤ 24 instances, circling at 0.3 m/s); **the Fish Log board** on the east wall: 24 slots, a slot shows the
   species icon once caught, `[E] Read · Fish Log` opens the journal on the Fish Log. Portal record
   `home:portal-cottage-2` (`[E] Enter · Nell's Cottage`, needs `met:nell`, spawn `entry`), from
   [atlas-home#placePortals](../bible/atlas-home.md#placePortals) row 3 (a record on the existing door; the first knock
   keeps the shipped door line). Prints fixture `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/<id>.json`.
9. **Debug hooks:** `G.debug.fish.spots()`, `.force({id, size, waitS})`, `.cast(spotId)`, `.bite()`, `.swing()`
   (drive the real state machine).

#### Assets
- `fx_fish_ripple` (realm: core): generate with the `sprite strip` kind of
  [art-audio#kinds](../bible/art-audio.md#kinds): preamble + suffix + "a sprite strip of eight frames of soft circular
  water ripples spreading out, white, transparent background" (systems#brief-gates). 8 frames of 128², strip
  1024×128 webp ≤ 40 KB; process with `python3 -I tools/process_strip.py` (8 frames, loop 2 s). Fallback: 3 expanding
  ring decals (canvas).
- `held_rod` (code-built, ≤ 300 tris): a 1.2 m bamboo rod `#c9a46b` with two wraps `#3fa79a`, a brass reel `#c99a4a`,
  a red float `#e05040` (leaf float `#8fd14f` with the charm). ART BRIEF: silhouette a short springy bamboo rod with a
  round reel near the grip; 1.2 m; palette above; toon wood and brass; ≤ 600 tris painted; held pose angled up 35°.
  Image prompt: preamble + `sheet-items` + "a short bamboo fishing rod with a brass reel and a red float, and the
  same rod with a leaf-shaped green float. Palette: #c9a46b #3fa79a #c99a4a #e05040 #8fd14f." 3D prompt: "stylised
  low-poly bamboo fishing rod with brass reel and float, hand-painted, under 600 triangles, 1.2 m". Fallback: `cyl` +
  `torus` + `sphere`.
- `fish_tank` (code-built cottage kit piece, ≤ 400 tris): glass box with a wooden rim `#a8723e`, sand `#f3e3c3`, two
  weeds `#3f8a5a`. Image prompt (reference only): "a small wooden-framed glass fish tank with sand and weeds, cosy
  storybook style". Fallback: transparent `box` + `box` rim.
- Icons: `icons_fish` cells come in S-207; this step ships the canvas fallback only.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all. Home prints byte-identical; `placeFishSpots` changes nothing else on seeds 1-40 (world test).
- `bun test`: `FISH` has 24 rows matching the bible table (names, codes, sizes, rarity split 10 / 9 / 5); `eligible`
  returns ≥ 1 species for every code × hour (0-23) × tide; `roll` is deterministic for a seeded RNG; the windows
  are 0.6 / 0.45 s and the charm multiplies rare waits by 0.75.
- In-page: with `rod`, a forced cast at `home:fish-meadow` + `bite()` + `swing()` inside the window lands the fish:
  card shows, `collect.fish` counts 1, the toast reads "✿ New fish: … (1/24)", `fish.best:` holds the size; a swing
  during `wait` shows "It got away!" and the ring returns after 10 s; walking 0.5 m reels in.
- Tiers: with 15 species set by debug, landing a 16th and talking to Nell gives a petal once; 20 gives `rod_charm`;
  24 gives `nook_gift_tank`; talking again gives nothing more. The petal key `act-fishing` and a kebab-case species
  key (`fish:puddle-minnow`) survive `normalize` (unit test in `tests/save.test.js`).
- Nell's cottage: enter/leave with no leak; the tank shows exactly the caught species; ≤ 60 draw calls inside.
- The Fish Log shows 24 slots and passes `G.debug.hud.audit()` at 1080p and 390 × 844.
- Lines ≤ 220 characters; no `~`; Pip never speaks (content lint).

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test` (new `tests/fishing.test.js`: table, eligibility, roll; extended `tests/world.test.js`:
  `placeFishSpots` on seeds 1-40; `tests/content.test.js` picks up the new lines and items; `tests/stats.test.js`;
  `tests/save.test.js`: a save holding `collect.petal['act-fishing']` and every `FISH` id as a `collect.fish` key
  round-trips through `normalize` unchanged).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs side` (add case `fishing`: give `rod` via `G.debug.give`, warp to each home spot, force casts,
  assert land / miss / reel-in, the tier talks, Nell's cottage entry).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (the cottage interior row).
- `bun run test:smoke`; `node tools/build-pages.mjs && bun run test:pwa` (new sprite strip).

#### Out of scope
- Spots on the isles, the Rootdeep and the Glassway and the painted fish icons (S-207). Spots on Kettle Isle,
  Saltglass Flats and Driftholm ship with those lands' finds steps (S-228, S-25x, S-26x). Showing the Nook tank (S-302).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: hiding the sword view-model for the rod collides with combat input; fishing is blocked during fights and any hit
reels in. Risk: the tank's 24 sprite cards inside a small room; one instanced draw. Rollback: revert; no save field is
added (`collect.fish` and flags exist since v9 and v7), so a save with fish in it loads on the reverted build.

<a id="s-207"></a>
### S-207 · feat: 24 fish species and 18 spots across the shipped lands
Milestone M24 · Depends on: S-206, S-029b, S-037b, S-180 · Size: M (about 320 lines plus the atlas) · Save: none · Flag: none ·
Bible: [side-content#act-fishing](../bible/side-content.md#act-fishing), [art-audio#fish](../bible/art-audio.md#fish),
[atlas-isles#passes](../bible/atlas-isles.md#passes), [atlas-far#far-passes](../bible/atlas-far.md#far-passes),
[atlas-far#glassway-pois](../bible/atlas-far.md#glassway-pois)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M24 · The Glassway II: fishing, the Starsong and the Sunken Bell**. Every shipped land gets its waters,
so the Fish Log fills while the player travels; the painted icons make the log, the tank and the catch card a
collection worth looking at. After this step 21 of the 24 species can be caught (the last three live on Kettle Isle,
Saltglass Flats and Driftholm, which have no spots yet).

#### What
1. **Isle spots** — each isle's pass `placeIsleFishing` on `new RNG(seed * 73 + 1)`
   ([atlas-isles#passes](../bible/atlas-isles.md#passes)), placing `{ id, code, x, z, y, r: 1.5, realm }` into the
   realm's `W.fishSpots` at the nearest water cell within 3 m of the anchor (draws: one jitter angle per spot):

   | Realm | id | Anchor | code | Note |
   |---|---|---|---|---|
   | duskwick (40211) | `duskwick:fish-1` | Wick Square west rail over the Deep Lamp Pool (−20, 88) | `c1` | stilt deck rail |
   | duskwick | `duskwick:fish-2` | the Reef Shelf (172, 88) | `c2` | warm outflow (bubbles sprite) |
   | pinewhistle (40223) | `pinewhistle:fish-1` | the mill pond (−92, 66) | `p1` | |
   | pinewhistle | `pinewhistle:fish-2` | the stream mouth (−158, 158) | `p2` | |
   | archwater (40231) | `archwater:fish-1` | the landing's end (−220, 12) | `a2` | Kelp Shallows fish, tide-aware |
   | archwater | `archwater:fish-2` | the Moon Pool rim (140, −60) | `a1` | Mooneye at night, high tide |
   | threemills (40237) | `threemills:fish-1` | the pond's east bank by the sluice (−14, 110) | `t1` | |
   | threemills | `threemills:fish-2` | the channel mouth (−70, 220) | `t2` | |

   Far lands, pass `placeFishSpots` on `new RNG(seed * 83 + 9)` ([atlas-far#far-passes](../bible/atlas-far.md#far-passes)):

   | Realm | id | Anchor | code |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | rootdeep (40241) | `rootdeep:fish-1` | north shore (−30, −80), still water | `r1` |
   | rootdeep | `rootdeep:fish-2` | Drip Bay (−12, 185), running water | `r1` |
   | glassway (40253) | `glassway:fish-post` | Post Rock north pier (0, −270) "harbour" | `g1` |
   | glassway | `glassway:fish-bell` | Bell Islet north pier (0, 138) "the Glass" | `g2` |
   | glassway | `glassway:fish-kelp` | Kelp Knoll's kelp edge (−90, 150) "kelp" | `g3` |
   | glassway | `glassway:fish-wreck` | the *Merry Mabel*'s shadow (200, 80) "wreck" | `g2` |

   Tide-aware spots read `tide:archwater` / `tide:glassway` at cast time. A spot on a tide flat that is dry at low tide
   (none of the above should be; the test proves it) would hide its prompt. Every pass changes no other list; the
   realm prints stay equal (each realm's `tests/realms/<id>.test.js` "post-pass changes nothing else" check).
   Total ripple spots after this step: home 4 + isles 8 + Rootdeep 2 + Glassway 4 = **18** in 7 lands (bible
   reconciliation R3).
2. **Fish Log wording**: each spot's water name for the log ("Duskwick · the stilt deck", "Archwater · the Moon
   Pool", …) is a text id `fish.water.<code>`; write the 18 from the anchors' notes above (≤ 30 characters each).
3. **Completion and economy:** no gem rewards (fishing pays tier rewards only, S-206). Each fish spot counts as a
   point of interest in the coverage test of its realm (the `activity` kind of
   [atlas-isles#poi-rule](../bible/atlas-isles.md#poi-rule)); rerun the coverage tests and update the POI counts the
   tests print.
4. **Icons** `icons_fish` atlas (core): 6 × 4 cells of 128², 768×512 webp ≤ 120 KB, cell names = fish ids, packed with
   `tools/pack_atlas.py --append` (S-037). The catch card, the Fish Log, Nell's board and both tanks read cells by id
   and fall back to `F-canvas` per missing cell.

#### Assets
- **`icons_fish`** — two generated sheets of 12, a 4 × 3 grid each, 2048×1536, white background, made by a GPT Sol
  subagent (T3 `delegate_task`, Codex `image_gen.imagegen`) or a Gemini model. Prompt = the fixed preamble of
  [art-audio#preamble](../bible/art-audio.md#preamble) + the `sheet-items` suffix + "a grid sheet of 12 separate small
  round cute cartoon fish icons, side view facing left, each a different species, evenly spaced on plain white, no
  text:" + the 12 looks in table order. Looks (sheet A, species 1-12): 1 a tiny sage-green minnow with a pale belly ·
  2 a plump pink carp with golden fins · 3 a violet fish with gold spots and a crown-shaped dorsal fin · 4 a cream fish
  with coral stripes like a lighthouse · 5 a huge old grey catfish with long whiskers · 6 an ember-red snapper with a
  dark plum back · 7 a tiny glowing golden tetra with a teal stripe · 8 a brown trout with pinecone-scale pattern ·
  9 a misty silver-green char · 10 a pale moon-white fish with a violet back and big eyes · 11 a green wrasse with
  aqua cheeks · 12 a dark slate blenny with hexagon markings. (Sheet B, 13-24): 13 a golden bream with sky-blue fins ·
  14 a small roach with wheat-gold fins and a green back · 15 a glowing mint gudgeon on a dark body · 16 a see-through
  pale cavefish with a golden glow inside · 17 a clear glassy herring with a teal edge · 18 a round bronze fish shaped
  like a bell · 19 a flat sandy flounder with both eyes on top · 20 a tiny teal sprat with an orange belly · 21 a
  peach-and-mint loach · 22 a tiny blue pupfish with a sandy belly · 23 a white koi with lilac patches and cloud-like
  fins · 24 a deep navy fish speckled with pale gold stars. Palette per fish: the two hexes of its row in
  side-content. Process: white-key (art-audio pipeline one-liner) → `python3 -I tools/slice_sheet.py <sheet> --parts
  12` (check `_contact.png` order) → `python3 -I tools/export_sprites.py` at 120 px → `tools/pack_atlas.py --append`.
  Log both prompts in `docs/art/prompts.md`. Originality: these are original fish designs; never a recognisable
  character from another game.
- **ART BRIEF** (for the owner's later batch; no GLB needed — fish are only icons): none beyond the atlas.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all (home is untouched in this step; the isle and far realms' prints stay equal).
- `bun test`: every realm with spots places exactly the listed spots, on water, deterministic across runs; each spot's
  code exists in `FISH_BY_CODE`; 21 species have at least one placed spot; the three without (`k1`, `s1`, `d1`) are
  listed by the test as "waiting for their land".
- Coverage tests of Duskwick, Pinewhistle, Archwater, Threemills, the Rootdeep and the Glassway still pass.
- In-page: at `archwater:fish-2` at 22:00 with `tide:archwater=1`, `G.debug.fish.force` aside, 200 seeded rolls
  include Mooneye; at 10:00 with low tide at `glassway:fish-kelp` they include Sandbar Flounder.
- `icons_fish` ≤ 120 KB; `?noassets=all` shows `F-canvas` icons; precache test passes.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test` (extend `tests/fishing.test.js` with spot placement per realm; `tests/coverage.test.js`;
  `tests/assets.test.js` size limits).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs side` (case `fishing-lands`: `G.debug.realm(id, spot)` for one spot per realm, forced cast,
  land, assert the log row and icon cell).
- `node tools/build-pages.mjs && bun run test:pwa`; `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope
- `k1` (S-228), `s1` (Saltglass finds), `d1` (Driftholm finds). Fishing from the boat (not planned).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: an isle anchor lands on a tide flat. The test fails and the anchor moves along the shore to the nearest cell
that is water at both tides. Rollback: revert; no save change.

<a id="s-208"></a>
### S-208 · feat: the Starsong: three tunes for star doors, night and the boat
Milestone M24 · Depends on: S-048, S-199, S-078 · Size: M (about 480 lines) · Save: none · Flag: none ·
Bible: [systems#items-tools](../bible/systems.md#items-tools), [systems#tool-starsong](../bible/systems.md#tool-starsong),
[systems#gates](../bible/systems.md#gates), [atlas-home#star-doors](../bible/atlas-home.md#star-doors),
[atlas-isles#passes](../bible/atlas-isles.md#passes), [atlas-far#glassway-pois](../bible/atlas-far.md#glassway-pois)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M24 · The Glassway II: fishing, the Starsong and the Sunken Bell**. The seventh tool turns time and sea
into things Pip can play: Dusk and Dawn at a bench, Calling for the boat, and a gate (star doors) that only opens at
night. It reopens one find on home's landmarks and one on each earlier land (pacing rule 5). The player earns it in
the Sunken Bell (S-210); this step builds the tool, its gates and its finds, reachable before then only through
`G.debug.give('starsong')`.

#### What
1. **Item and ability.** `js/items.js`: `starsong: { name: 'Starsong', kind: 'tool', icon: 'starsong', desc: 'A leaf
   whistle with three notes. Low, mid, high.' }`; `js/abilities.js`: `starsong: { name: 'Starsong', from: 'starsong' }`.
   The tool ring (S-035) gains it in slot 6 (Lantern, Tide Bell, Hook, Sunglass, Paws, **Starsong**, Sling).
2. **Song mode** `js/tools/starsong.js` (pure core `parseTune(notes, times)` + a thin module):
   - `tool` (tap) enters **song mode** for 2.5 s: a three-line stave above the HUD tool slot; while it is open,
     **Roll = low**, **Hop = mid**, **Swing = high** play notes instead of their actions (input is consumed; Pip does
     not roll, hop or swing). Each new note restarts the 2.5 s window. Esc / `tool` again closes it.
   - Notes: low `#5a6ad8` (G4 sine-pluck), mid `#f2c45a` (B4), high `#ff8fb0` (D5); each shows its coloured dot on the
     stave and a ring puff over Pip (FP: at the bottom of the view; TP: over the hood). Sound twins every note.
   - Tunes (systems): **Dawn** = low, mid, high · **Dusk** = high, mid, low · **Calling** = mid, high, mid. After three
     notes the tune is matched and its effect fires (a soft chord plays the tune back); a non-tune just ends the
     mode. A single note followed by 1.0 s of silence fires `EV.NOTE {pitch}` alone (dungeon pitch doors, cloud
     platforms; S-209 listens). Every completed tune emits `EV.TUNE {tune, x, z}`.
   - Not in scenes, menus, or while drawing the Sling; in dungeons only `EV.NOTE` and `EV.TUNE` fire (no clock or boat
     effects).
3. **Effects** (each a tiny listener, `js/tools/starsong.js`):
   - **Dusk / Dawn at a bench** (Pip within 3 m of a bench of S-080, outside a fight): 2 s fade; Dusk sets the clock to
     **19:48**, Dawn to **07:12** (systems). Toasts "✿ The stars come out." / "✿ Good morning!". Anywhere else the tune
     just plays.
   - **Calling at a shore** (an overworld with a jetty, Pip within 6 m of sea water): opens the chart as if at the jetty
     with the toast "Nell heard you!" (S-081's chart). In the Glassway (free sailing) it moves the *Dandelion* to the
     nearest pier (`glassway:pier-*`) with Nell aboard and toasts "Nell heard you! The Dandelion is at <pier>."; in the
     chart-stop fallback it opens the Glassway sub-chart. Calling at the end of Nell's Jetty at night also sets the
     runtime `moonpath` state that S-218 reads (a module field, not saved, cleared at 05:00).
4. **Star door gate** (`js/gates.js` type `star_door`, needs `starsong` + night): opens when **Dusk** is played within
   **4 m** at night (21:00-04:59; bible reconciliation R1) — by day the prompt `[E] Look · Star door` says "The stars on
   it are asleep." At night its stars glow and a twinkle plays within 10 m (FP cue). Opening: 1.0 s roll aside / swing
   open, stays open, sets `gate:<realm>:<id>`. Solver: `needs: 'ability:starsong'` (the night is always reachable via
   a bench, so the solver ignores time).
   Two looks (one code-built model `star_door`, two forms):
   - `disc` (home landmark bases): 1.4 m round pale stone disc, 0.25 m thick, seven star holes in a spiral; rolls aside.
   - `door` (isles and the Glassway): a 2 × 2.8 m dark stone door slab carved with a moon and seven stars in a ruin
     frame; swings in.
5. **Home doors** — post-pass `placeStarDoors(W)`, pass row 20, `new RNG(7151 * 157 + 89)`
   ([atlas-home#star-doors](../bible/atlas-home.md#star-doors)): one `disc` door at each landmark keepsake, flush into
   the landmark's base on the keepsake's angle (`W.secrets[i].rot`), so the shipped keepsake stands in front of it;
   draws only a ±0.2 m height jitter; position helper `starDoorSpot(landmark)` is pure and exported (acorn pass row 16
   uses it). Behind each a 1 m alcove:

   | Door id | Landmark | Alcove | Flag |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | `home:stardoor-woods` | Grumble's Crown | 30-gem pouch `home:stardoor-woods` in `S.chests` | `gate:home:stardoor-woods` |
   | `home:stardoor-peaks` | the Cairn's east foot | acorn `home:a05` (its snore heard through the closed door; it is woken with the Sunglass as usual) | `gate:home:stardoor-peaks` |
   | `home:stardoor-shores` | the lighthouse's sea side | 30-gem pouch | `gate:home:stardoor-shores` |
   | `home:stardoor-meadow` | the windmill footing | 30-gem pouch | `gate:home:stardoor-meadow` |

   If S-161 (acorns) already placed `a05` openly, move nothing: the door is placed in front of it and the acorn stays
   where it is (the door just hides it until opened). Add 90 gems to the economy ledger (home caches row).
6. **Isle doors** — each isle's pass `placeIsleNight` on `new RNG(seed * 73 + 11)`
   ([atlas-isles#passes](../bible/atlas-isles.md#passes)), one `door` each, guarding the find its finds step placed:

   | Realm | Door id | Where | Guards |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | duskwick | `duskwick:stardoor` | the Reef Shelf (170, 80), r 8 | Lamp Log `duskwick:log-5` |
   | pinewhistle | `pinewhistle:stardoor` | Whistle Rise's ruin ring, west side | acorn `pinewhistle:acorn-10` |
   | archwater | `archwater:stardoor` | the arch's west-leg shoulder (132, −74) | acorn `archwater:acorn-9` |
   | threemills | `threemills:stardoor` | the Ring Stones in Haybury's back field (190, 70), r 4 | chest `threemills:chest-3` |
   | glassway | `glassway:stardoor` | Spiral Skerry's ruin ring (120, 150), placed by the far pass `placeNightFinds` on `new RNG(40253 * 89 + 11)` ([atlas-far#far-passes](../bible/atlas-far.md#far-passes)) | chest `glassway:c08` (S-205 placed it inert) |

   If a finds step placed its guarded find with an inert placeholder, this pass replaces the placeholder's `needs`
   with `gate:<realm>:stardoor`; if it placed nothing, place the find at the listed spot. Each isle now reopens one
   find for the Starsong (pacing rule 5 is checked by the solver report).
7. **Held model and HUD:** FP: a two-leaf whistle in Pip's left hand at the bottom-left, lifting to the "mouth" when a
   note plays; TP: hangs on the belt, lifted while playing. HUD tool icon `starsong` (S-037 icon atlas cell; fallback
   a canvas leaf with three dots).
8. **Debug hooks:** `G.debug.give('starsong')` (exists), `G.debug.song(['low','mid','high'])` plays notes through the
   real input path.

#### Assets
- `star_door` (code-built; `disc` ≤ 160 tris, `door` ≤ 400 tris): ART BRIEF per
  [atlas-home#star-doors](../bible/atlas-home.md#star-doors) for the disc (`#cfc8bc` stone, holes `#9fd8ff`,
  `nightGlow` 0.5 flaring to 1.0 for the 1.0 s roll; prompt "a round stone door disc with seven star-shaped holes
  arranged in a spiral, glowing faint blue, set into the base of a tower, cosy storybook style"); for the slab: a dark
  stone door 2 × 2.8 m `#3a3a4a` with a carved crescent and seven stars `#9fd8ff`, in a weathered Lamplighter frame
  `#cfc8bc`; prompt "a dark stone door in a ruined stone arch, carved with a crescent moon and seven little stars that
  glow faint blue at night, cosy storybook style"; 3D prompt "stylised low-poly dark stone door with carved moon and
  stars in a ruined arch, hand-painted, under 800 triangles, 2.8 m". Fallbacks: `cyl` disc + 7 canvas star decals;
  `box` slab + decal.
- `held_starsong` (code-built, ≤ 120 tris): a two-leaf whistle `#8fd14f` / `#5a9a3a` with three gold dots `#f2c45a`.
  ART BRIEF: silhouette two crossed leaves bound with gold thread; 0.18 m; toon; image prompt preamble + `icon` +
  "a little whistle made of two green leaves bound with gold thread, three tiny gold dots on it"; 3D prompt "tiny
  low-poly leaf whistle, 120 triangles". Fallback: two flattened `sphere` leaves.
- Icon cell `starsong` in the items icon atlas (S-037; generated with the `icon` kind, 128²; fallback canvas leaf).

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all. Home prints byte-identical; `placeStarDoors` changes nothing else on seeds 1-40; each isle's
  and the Glassway's prints equal.
- `bun test`: `parseTune` recognises the three tunes, rejects others, honours the 2.5 s window and the 1.0 s single
  note; the solver gains `starsong` and its report shows one home find and one find per isle opened by it; economy
  ledger includes the 90 home gems and still passes ≥ 105 %.
- In-page (`tools` suite): song mode consumes Roll/Hop/Swing (Pip does not move); Dusk at a bench sets 19:48 in 2 s;
  Dawn sets 07:12; a star door by day refuses with "The stars on it are asleep."; at night Dusk within 4 m opens it,
  sets the flag, and it stays open after reload; Calling at the home jetty opens the chart with "Nell heard you!";
  in the Glassway it moves the boat to the nearest pier (skipped when `sail = 'chart'`).
- First person: the stave and note dots are readable at 1080p and phone width (`hud.audit`).

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test` (new `tests/starsong.test.js`; `tests/world.test.js` row 20; isle/glassway realm tests for
  the night pass; `tests/progress-graph.test.js`; `tests/economy.test.js`).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs tools` (case `starsong`: give, song mode, bench tunes, a home door, the Glassway door, Calling).
- `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope
- Earning the Starsong (S-210 chest). Pitch doors and dungeon tunes (S-209, S-210). The moonpath and Moonmere (S-218).
  Cloud platforms by colour (S-268 era).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: consuming Roll/Hop/Swing during song mode could trap a player mid-fight; song mode refuses to open while an
attack token is held within 15 m. Rollback: revert; star-door flags left in a save are ignored (doors return closed,
the pouches already paid stay paid, nothing is taken back).

<a id="s-209"></a>
### S-209 · feat: the Sunken Bell rooms 1-6: three pitches open matching doors
Milestone M24 · Depends on: S-208, S-053 · Size: M (about 470 lines with map data) · Save: none · Flag: realm
`sunken_bell` hidden (`REALMS.sunken_bell.hidden = true`) until S-212c · Bible: [dungeons#d7](../bible/dungeons.md#d7),
[dungeons#entities](../bible/dungeons.md#entities), [dungeons#state](../bible/dungeons.md#state),
[dungeons#solver](../bible/dungeons.md#solver), [dungeons#rules](../bible/dungeons.md#rules),
[systems#portal-table](../bible/systems.md#portal-table), [creatures#enemy-bubble-puffer](../bible/creatures.md#enemy-bubble-puffer),
[creatures#enemy-gullgrab](../bible/creatures.md#enemy-gullgrab)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M24 · The Glassway II: fishing, the Starsong and the Sunken Bell**. Dungeon 7 is a dungeon of
**sound**: bells that open doors of their pitch, brass tubes that carry a note to another room, and clams that sing a
tune to be repeated elsewhere. This step builds the first six rooms and the three sound entities; S-210 finishes the
dungeon and the song doors.

#### What
1. **Data** `js/realms/dungeons/sunken_bell.js`: copy **verbatim** from [dungeons#d7](../bible/dungeons.md#d7) the
   composite map `sunken_bell L1` (all 41 rows: the map test of S-049 compares the whole map), the legend extension,
   the full room table (r1-r11) and the room-by-room entities. Fields: `id: 'sunken_bell'`, `name: 'The Sunken Bell'`,
   `kind: 'dungeon'`, `tile: 2`, grid 98 m, `palette: 'sunken_bell'` (stone `#2f7d8c`, trim `#f5e6c4`, accent
   `#b0793a`, sea-glass `#bfeee6`, dark tint `#173a44`), `music: 'sunken_bell'`, `light: { ambient: 0.6, torches:
   true, sun: false }` plus a wavering water-light card on each high round window (one shared animated card, ≤ 1 draw
   per room), `parent: 'glassway'`, `door: 'bell_door'`, `needsOnEntry: ['lantern']`. Name card line 2 "beneath Bell
   Shallows". In this step rooms **r7-r11** compile as plain floors and walls marked `stub: true` (no entities, no
   waves, no chests); the doors leading into them stay closed: the song door (25,16) r4→r9 and (5,16) r6→r7 render
   as closed song doors (S-210 activates them); the locked door (20,20) and the boss door are not reachable yet.
2. **Registry** `js/realms/index.js`: `sunken_bell` (`kind: 'dungeon'`, `hidden: true`, `parent: 'glassway'`, bundle
   `glassway`). **Portal** `glassway:bell` on the `bell_door` anchor (0, 278) placed by S-205 (a record on the
   existing anchor): `[E] Enter · The Sunken Bell`, `needs: 'flag:tide:glassway=0 & flag:bellDepths'` (systems'
   `quest:mq-bell>=2` written as the flag its step sets, [dungeons#d7](../bible/dungeons.md#d7) "Opens"); until S-212c
   sets `bellDepths` the door shows `[E] Look · Chapel door` "A round chapel door, locked. A spiral is carved where the
   keyhole should be." At high tide the door is under water and shows nothing. Spawn `@`
   (5,1); `X` (5,0) returns to `bell_door` facing north.
3. **Entities** in `js/dungeon-mod.js` (each emits `EV.PUZZLE {id, on}` and saves in `S.dungeons.sunken_bell`):
   - **Pitch bell** `x` low / `Y` mid / `z` high: a bronze bell on a wooden frame (1.2 / 0.9 / 0.6 m). A sword hit
     (or `[E] Ring · Bell`) rings it, 0.4 s cooldown: its pitch plays (low G3, mid B3, high D4 in the music's key) and a
     coloured ring expands 3 m (low rose `#f4a6c0`, mid gold `#ffd36b`, high sky `#8fd3ff`). Emits `EV.PITCH {pitch,
     col, row, t}`. Starsong notes count the same: song mode (S-208) emits `EV.SONGNOTE {pitch}` per note (add this
     to `js/tools/starsong.js` if S-208 emits only single notes), which this module turns into `EV.PITCH` at Pip's tile.
   - **Pitch door** `U` low / `V` mid / `W` high: a round bronze door engraved with one, two or three dots; opens for
     good when its pitch sounds within **6 tiles** (Chebyshev distance from the door tile to the sounding tile);
     `open['<room>:<glyph><n>'] = true`. The dots glow when a matching pitch is heard further away (a hint).
   - **Speaking tube** `f`: two mouths joined by a pipe drawn along the walls (`int_tube`). A pitch rung within 1 tile
     of one mouth sounds again out of the other **0.5 s** later (a `EV.PITCH` at the far mouth's tile, with a small
     brass "toot" puff there). r6's mouth (2,10) is joined to r2's (19,2).
   - **Giant clam** `I` + shell plate `p` (r3): standing on plate `r3:p1` (25,4) makes the three clams open and sing
     **Tune A** = low, high, mid (0.8 s apart, each a coloured ring from its clam), repeating every 6 s while Pip
     stays. Clams are **heard**, never a source of `EV.PITCH` for doors (a door ignores them; the music shows them as
     plain notes). Clams (23,2), (25,2), (27,2) sing low, high, mid in that order of position (west to east = first to
     third note).
4. **Rooms r1-r6** exactly as the bible's room notes: r1 sign `?` (3,3): "Ring low to go in. Ring true to go on.
   — the bell-keepers" (text id `gw.bell.sign.r1`), low bell `r1:x1` (6,4) → low door (10,4); r2 (ceiling 9) mid bell
   `r2:Y1` (15,3) → mid door (15,8); r2 high door (20,4) opens only from tube mouth `r2:f1` (19,2); map chest `&`
   (11,1); r3 clam choir, plate, wave 1 Bubble Puffer ×2 at (23,5), (27,5), Chime Stone `%` (21,7), door (25,8) `always`;
   r4 bells `r4:x1` (22,11), `r4:Y1` (25,12), `r4:z1` (28,11) (the Tune A song door south is S-210's); r5 (ceiling 8)
   wave 1 Gullgrab ×2 at (13,10), (17,10), Bubble Puffer ×1 at (15,14), on clear small key `r5:k1` (15,12), locked door
   (10,12) west; r6 high bell `r6:z1` (3,10) beside tube mouth `r6:f1` (2,10), wave 1 Bubble Puffer ×2 at (3,14),
   (7,14), gem chest `r6:c1` (1,15) 40 gems. At most 4 enemies alive per room; waves respawn only when Pip re-enters
   the dungeon from outside (dungeons#rules).
5. **Solver** (`js/realms/dungeon-compile.js`): model `EV.PITCH` as an event at a tile with a 6-tile door radius; a
   tube as a 0.5 s delayed copy at the far mouth; clams as "heard" (no door effect); the pitch available at a bell is
   ringable when Pip can reach any tile within sword reach of it. For this step the solver target is the r1-r6
   subset: from `@`, prove `&`, `%`, `r5:k1`, `r6:c1` reachable and the r2 high door openable only through the tube
   (a fixture test removes the tube and expects the solver to fail with "r2:W1 unreachable").
6. **Signs and hints** `js/text/en/gw.js`: the three Ask the Tree hint pairs of d7 verbatim (`gw.bell.hint.1-3`);
   hint 1 ("brass ear") is live from this step; 2 and 3 are wired in S-210.
7. **Music** `sunken_bell`: register the `depths` variant `{ from: 'depths', transpose: 0, voices: { lead: 'glock' },
   add: { pad: { voice: 'organ', vol: 0.4 } } }` in G major (the bells' pitches sit in its key); if `organ` is not a
   shipped voice, use the `pad` voice art-audio#track-data lists for organ-like pads. Every bell struck also plays its
   pitch through the music bus.
8. **Prints** `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/sunken_bell.json` (S-210 refreshes it once; frozen after S-212c).

#### Assets
- `tex_chapel_stone` (realm `glassway`): preamble + `tile` + "Seamless texture of pale sea-washed chapel stone blocks
  with soft teal water stains and tiny shells in the mortar, tileable. Palette: #2f7d8c #f5e6c4 #bfeee6 #b0793a."
  Process `python3 -I tools/make_seamless.py <in> <out> --blend 64`, then 1024 and 512 webp (hd ≤ 110 KB, lo ≤ 40 KB).
  Fallback `tex_dungeon_stone` tinted `#9fc9c4`.
- Code-built (each ≤ 300 tris; ART BRIEF lines from dungeons#d7 "Art (d7)"): `int_bell_low/_mid/_high` (bronze bells
  1.2 / 0.9 / 0.6 m on wooden frames, ribbons rose, gold, sky; fallback bronze cones), `int_door_pitch` (round bronze
  door with one, two or three engraved dots that glow when heard; fallback kit door leaf + coloured disc), `int_tube`
  (brass funnel 0.8 m on a curled pipe; fallback brass cone), `int_clam` (1.6 m clam, pearly pink lip, mouth flaps
  4 Hz while singing; fallback pink hemisphere). One painted ART BRIEF for the batch (for the owner's GLB pass):
  silhouette "a set of three bronze chapel bells on oak frames with ribbons"; sizes as above; palette bronze `#b0793a`,
  verdigris `#7fbfa8`, oak `#8a5a32`, ribbons `#f4a6c0` `#ffd36b` `#8fd3ff`; image prompt preamble + `sheet-items` +
  "three bronze bells of three sizes on little oak frames, each tied with a ribbon (pink, gold, sky blue)"; 3D prompt
  "stylised low-poly set of three bronze bells on oak frames, hand-painted, under 1,200 triangles".

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except "Frozen home island" (home untouched). The Glassway's prints equal (the portal is a
  record on S-205's anchor).
- `bun test`: the compiler builds `sunken_bell`; `tests/dungeon-maps.test.js` equals the bible map; the solver proves
  the r1-r6 targets and the tube-removed fixture fails as specified.
- In-page (`dungeons` suite): `G.debug.realm('sunken_bell')`; ringing `r1:x1` opens (10,4) and nothing else; ringing
  `r6:z1` opens r2's (20,4) after 0.5 s; standing on `r3:p1` plays Tune A three times in 18 s and opens no door;
  r5's clear drops the key; door states survive a reload; a dizzy Pip wakes at `@` with doors kept.
- Realm suite: `sunken_bell` enters and leaves with no leak; every room view ≤ 120 draw calls and ≤ 150 k tris in
  both cameras; generation ≤ 0.15 s.
- `REALMS.sunken_bell.hidden === true`.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test` (dungeon map test, compiler, solver with pitch events and the tube fixture, prints).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs dungeons` (case `sunken_bell_1`: drives `G.debug.hit(entityId)` or the real swing at each
  bell; add `G.debug.dungeon.ring(id)` if missing, which calls the bell's real hit handler).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`; `bun run test:smoke`; `node tools/build-pages.mjs && bun run test:pwa`.

#### Out of scope
- Song doors, r7-r11, the drips, the Starsong chest, keys 2 and Root Key (S-210). Pelagia (S-211). The quest and the
  portal going live (S-212c).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: the 6-tile radius lets a bell open a door the designer did not mean; the solver test lists every door each
bell can open and is compared to the bible's solver notes. Rollback: revert; the realm is hidden.

<a id="s-210"></a>
### S-210 · feat: the Sunken Bell rooms 7-11: sequences heard elsewhere
Milestone M24 · Depends on: S-209 · Size: M (about 380 lines) · Save: none · Flag: realm still hidden ·
Bible: [dungeons#d7](../bible/dungeons.md#d7), [dungeons#entities](../bible/dungeons.md#entities),
[systems#tool-starsong](../bible/systems.md#tool-starsong)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M24 · The Glassway II: fishing, the Starsong and the Sunken Bell**. The second half of dungeon 7 asks
the player to listen in one room and play in another: Tune A from the clams, Tune B from the drips. It holds the
Starsong (the reward) and the way to Pelagia.

#### What
1. **Song door** `T` in `js/dungeon-mod.js`: a round bronze door engraved with a three-dot staff in the tune's colours;
   opens for good when its `tune` sounds within **6 tiles** as three `EV.PITCH` events in order with **≤ 2.0 s**
   between notes (bells, tube mouths or Starsong notes all count); a wrong note resets the attempt with a soft "bonk"
   and the staff's dots go dark. Each correct note lights one dot. Saves `open['<room>:T<n>']`. Tunes:
   **Tune A "the Clam Song"** = low, high, mid · **Tune B "the Drip Hymn"** = mid, mid, high.

   | Door | Between | Tune | Note |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | (25,16) | r4 → r9 | A | rung on r4's three bells |
   | (10,20) | r8 ↔ r7 | B | rung on r8's bells (all within 6 tiles) |
   | (5,16) | r6 ↔ r7 | B | from the r7 side with the Starsong: the shortcut back to r6 |

2. **Drips** `w` (r9): three stone basins (23,18), (25,18), (27,18) under drips that play **Tune B** every 6 s (mid,
   mid, high; drips at (23,18) and (25,18) are mid, (27,18) high, each a falling drop and a coloured ring). Like clams
   they are heard, not door sources. Unlike the clams they need no plate (always on while Pip is in r9).
3. **Rooms r7-r11** (remove `stub: true`), entities exactly as the bible: r9 small key `r9:k1` (25,20), wave Bubble
   Puffer ×2 at (23,21), (27,21), gem chest `r9:c2` (21,23), locked door (20,20) west; r8 bells `r8:x1` (12,18),
   `r8:Y1` (14,19), `r8:z1` (12,21), Root Key chest `K` (16,22), gem chest `r8:c3` (18,22), robes on hooks (decor); r7
   (ceiling 8) treasure chest `$` (5,20) on a raised dais (the `1` tiles, +0.3 m) holding the **Starsong**; high door
   `W` (5,24) south; r10 wave Bubble Puffer ×2 at (3,27), (7,29), gem chest `r10:c4` (1,31), boss door `B` (10,28);
   r11 (ceiling 18) the great bell hanging silent from the roof, Pelagia's spot `M` (20,28), duel bells `r11:x1`
   (16,33), `r11:Y1` (20,34), `r11:z1` (24,33) built as plain props (S-211 makes them the duel), `X` (20,40) back up to
   the spire. Gem chests total 160 (r6 c1 from S-209 + c2, c3, c4 at 40).
4. **The Starsong chest.** Item-get (bible reconciliation R6, systems controls): "You got the Starsong! A leaf whistle
   with three notes. Press {tool}, then {dash}, {jump} or {attack} for low, mid or high." Effects `item:starsong`,
   `ability:starsong`; it is equipped. A second beat 1.5 s later shows the three tunes on a card in the leaf frame:
   "Dawn: low, mid, high. Dusk: high, mid, low. Calling: mid, high, mid." (`gw.bell.starsong.card`). The Items tab
   lists the tunes in the Starsong's detail.
5. **Hints** `gw.bell.hint.2`, `gw.bell.hint.3` (bible text) wired to their rooms.
6. **Solver** — whole dungeon: prove the bible's solver-notes path (r1 → … → r8 ring mid, mid, high → r7 `$` → play
   high → r10 → B → r11 → `X`), every chest (`&`, `%`, `K`, `$`, c1-c4), both small keys and the boss door reachable,
   and no soft-lock (the r6↔r7 song door needs the Starsong, which is behind it only from r7's side: the solver must
   show r7 is reached through r8 first). Replay the path as a scripted test.
7. **Map card**: the dungeon map (from `&`) shows bells as coloured dots and song doors with their tune's three dots.

#### Assets
- `int_great_bell` (code-built, ≤ 600 tris): the 6 m bronze bell with a spiral band and the Stair Stone as its
  clapper (a round pale stone `#e9e2cf` with a glowing spiral `#ffe9a8`), hanging from roof beams; ART BRIEF verbatim
  from dungeons#d7 (silhouette, 6 m tall, 5 m lip, bronze `#b0793a`, verdigris `#7fbfa8`, stone `#e9e2cf`, glow
  `#ffe9a8`, 3,000 tris painted / 600 code, swings 4° when rung; image prompt "<style line> A huge old bronze bell
  hanging silent in a sunken sea chapel, a glowing round stone as its clapper, soft teal water-light, peaceful."; 3D
  prompt "Stylised low-poly bronze church-style bell with a spiral band and a round stone clapper, verdigris,
  hand-painted, game-ready, under 3,000 triangles, 6 m tall."). Fallback: big bronze cone.
- `int_drip_basin` (code-built, ≤ 120 tris): a stone basin 0.9 m with a drip sprite (the shared particle card).
- Song door uses `int_door_pitch` with a three-dot staff decal (canvas).

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except "Frozen home island".
- `bun test`: map test equal; solver proves the full path, every chest and key, no soft-lock; the scripted replay
  reaches r11.
- In-page (`dungeons`): r4's Tune A opens (25,16) only in the right order and with ≤ 2 s gaps (a 2.5 s gap resets);
  r8's Tune B opens (10,20); the r7 chest gives the Starsong once, the card shows the three tunes; playing Tune B on
  the Starsong in r7 opens (5,16); playing high opens (5,24).
- Realm suite views of r7-r11 ≤ 120 draw calls (r11 with the great bell ≤ 150) and ≤ 150 k tris.
- Still `hidden: true`.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test`; `node tests/inpage.mjs dungeons` (case `sunken_bell_2`, using `G.debug.song([...])` for the
  Starsong and `G.debug.dungeon.ring(id)` for bells); `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`; `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope
- Pelagia's duel and the stone (S-211). Going live (S-212c).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: the r6↔r7 song door could be opened from r6's side by ringing r6's bells in Tune B order (r6 has only a high
bell, so it cannot); the solver test asserts that (5,16) opens from r6 only with the Starsong, which is only won in r7.
Rollback: revert; hidden realm.

<a id="s-210b"></a>

### S-210b · feat: The Sunken Bell grows the Carillon Loft, the Bell-Rope Room and the Bellringer's Closet: the chapel's bells swap (depth rule R14)

Milestone M24 · Depends on: S-210, S-060b, S-104b (shared annex pieces and the latch) · Size: M (200-500; most of it map data and entity tables) · Save: none · Flag: the dungeon realm stays `hidden: true` · Bible: [../bible/dungeons.md#d7-depth](../bible/dungeons.md#d7-depth), [../bible/dungeons.md#depth-common](../bible/dungeons.md#depth-common), [../bible/dungeons.md#depth-counts](../bible/dungeons.md#depth-counts), [../bible/dungeons.md#rules](../bible/dungeons.md#rules), [../bible/dungeons.md#d7](../bible/dungeons.md#d7)

Added by the playtime review (dungeons.md R14, CANON §11.1 A2; see [Index changes](#index-changes)). Filed after
S-210 and before S-211, which now depends on it.

#### Why
Roadmap item **M24 · The Glassway II: fishing, the Starsong and the Sunken Bell**. Every story dungeon had about one simple beat per room and almost no room where two
mechanics meet, so The Sunken Bell played in well under its budget and taught one idea without building on it. Rule R14
asks for ≥ 1.6 beats per counted room, ≥ 4 compound rooms, one mid-dungeon state flip that sends the player back
through rooms already solved, and a last room before the boss that uses everything. This step builds
The Sunken Bell's annex, flip, latch and synthesis from the bible, so the dungeon meets the rule before its boss lands.

#### What
1. **Annex levels.** Copy the map blocks of [dungeons.md#d7-depth](../bible/dungeons.md#d7-depth) verbatim
   into the dungeon's data module (`js/realms/dungeons/<id>.js`, the file its room steps created): `sunken_bell LA` (`at` [33,0]): r12 The Carillon Loft, r13 The Bell-Rope Room; `sunken_bell LB` (`at` [33,10]): r14 The Bellringer's Closet; extent 54 × 41, realm grid 124 m. Room
   table rows, entity rows and the room-by-room conditions exactly as the bible gives them. Annex levels join the §5
   rooms only through stair warps.
2. **Stairs.** r12 `^`(1,7) ↔ r10 (4,26), a hatch that opens to a mid note from a bell or tube (deaf to the Starsong); r14 `^`(1,7) ↔ r8 (18,18), sealed until the flip.
3. **Tile edits to the §5 map** (apply to the bible's map block and to the data in this PR; the map test compares
   against the edited block): r10 (4,26) `.` → `^`; r10 (2,30) `.` → `Y` (`r10:Y1`); r8 (18,18) `.` → `^`. Update the bible's room table, keys-and-chests ledger and solver notes to
   match, in the same PR.
4. **The flip.** **the Bell Rope** `r13:s1` (19,1), flag `sb.rope`, line `dg.sb.flip` "The great rope jerks. Every bell in the chapel below rings a wrong note.". From then on: **Q = r7** and **P = r8**: every bell in r7 and r8 swaps low ↔ high (pitch and colour), and the song door `T` (10,20) between them relocks and now wants **Tune E** (high, low, mid; heard in r12); r8's stair (18,18) unseals. Saved as `S.dungeons[<id>].state.flip = true` (v8
   container, no bump); the dungeon compiler applies the listed entity-state changes when it builds Q and P; the first
   entry into each after the flip plays the 0.4 s result cue (R7).
5. **The latch.** `B` (10,28) opens on `key:K && latch:r14:s1 && clear:r10`. Hitting the latch plays the toast `dg.latch` and a positional clunk at `B`.
6. **Synthesis in the room before the boss** (R14 item 4): r10: ring the new mid bell `r10:Y1` (2,30) within 6 tiles of the hatch (the hatch ignores the Starsong: "The hatch is deaf to songs."); its wave is `clear` for `B`.
7. **Ask the Tree** gains two hint pairs for this dungeon (systems#hints): "The Bell Pit's hatch is listening for a middle note." / "Ring the mid bell by the Bell Pit hatch, go down, step on the Carillon plate and remember the tune, then pull the Bell Rope." · "The chapel bells sing upside down now." /
   "Low bells ring high and high ring low in the chapel; play the Carillon's tune at the song door, then take the Vestry stair." (text ids `dg.sunken_bell.hint.4`, `.5`).
8. **Solver and depth test.** The flip is one more state bit in the lock-and-key graph (S-049); the solver proves the
   whole path (§5 rooms → annex → flip → Q and P re-solved → latch → `B`), every chest, and no soft-lock whether the
   player flips early or late. Extend `tests/dungeon-depth.test.js` (S-060b) with this dungeon's row: 14 rooms, 12 counted, ≥ 20 beats (planned 23), ≥ 4 compound rooms (planned 7); the test prints the per-room table
   for the PR body.
9. **Two waves from Act III** (dungeons.md R14; CANON §10.3): every combat room of this dungeon gets a second wave of mixed archetypes at the land's pattern tier (creatures.md#pattern-tiers), spawning 1.5 s after the first is calmed, at most 2 attackers at once; each room's `clear` waits for both. List the second waves in each room's entry in `dungeons.md`.

#### Assets
- The annex rooms reuse this dungeon's kit, textures and palette (no new generated images; the annex rooms are within
  the realm bundle already budgeted).
- **`int_bell_rope`** (code-built, ≤ 80 tris; the flip prop): a thick red-and-cream bell rope with a woollen tassel hanging from a dark ceiling hole. ART BRIEF: silhouette as described;
  size 4 m; palette `#c2563f` `#f4ead8` `#7fd3d0`; toon materials; 80 / 400 tris (code / painted GLB); pose static, it
  turns or swings once when used; image prompt "<style line> a thick red-and-cream bell rope with a woollen tassel hanging from a dark ceiling hole, cosy storybook dungeon prop, three-quarter
  view"; 3D prompt "stylised low-poly a thick red-and-cream bell rope with a woollen tassel hanging from a dark ceiling hole, hand-painted texture, game-ready, under 400
  triangles, 4 m". Manifest key `int_bell_rope` (commented-out GLB slot). Fallback: the code-built model.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep *Frozen realms* (this dungeon's prints are refreshed in this PR, inside its own build milestone,
  before the land is unhidden), *Hidden until whole*, *One save slot* (no bump), *Budgets* (R11: every annex room view
  ≤ 120 draw calls and ≤ 150 k triangles), *Tone*, *Assets*, *Machine*.
- `tests/dungeon-maps.test.js` passes with the annex blocks and the edited §5 block; data equals the bible blocks.
- The solver proves the full path, every chest, the flip in both orders, and no soft-lock; the depth test row passes
  the thresholds above.
- In page: hitting the flip changes Q and P exactly as listed; `B` stays shut until the latch (or d1's plate) and the
  Root Key; the hints show in Ask the Tree.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `agentctl heavy --job <job> -- bun test tests/dungeon-maps.test.js tests/dungeon-depth.test.js` and the
  dungeon's solver test.
- `agentctl heavy --job <job> -- node tests/inpage.mjs dungeons` (new case `sunken_bell_depth`: `G.debug.realm('<id>')`,
  play the intended path from the annex stair with `G.debug.dungeon.solve('<id>', { from: 'annex' })`, assert the flip's
  changes in Q and P, the latch, and `B`).
- `agentctl heavy --job <job> -- node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (prints refreshed, budgets).

#### Out of scope
- The boss (S-211); any new chest, key or reward (the 48-chest ledger is fixed); the land overworld.

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: the flip makes a room unsolvable from the side the player returns by; the solver run in both flip orders is the
guard, and the bible's fallback (§5A.1) adds a block and plate. Rollback: revert the PR; the §5 rooms work alone
(stair warps to a missing annex show as sealed hatches) and the realm stays hidden.
