<a id="s-211"></a>
### S-211 · feat: Pelagia's bell-order duel: a game of wits, never an attack
Milestone M24 · Depends on: S-210, S-045, S-210b · Size: M (about 450 lines) · Save: none · Flag: realm still hidden ·
Bible: [creatures#boss-pelagia](../bible/creatures.md#boss-pelagia), [creatures#boss-def](../bible/creatures.md#boss-def),
[creatures#encounter-kinds](../bible/creatures.md#encounter-kinds), [creatures#boss-grammar](../bible/creatures.md#boss-grammar),
[cast#pelagia](../bible/cast.md#pelagia), [story#ch15-17](../bible/story.md#ch15-17), [art-audio#track-data](../bible/art-audio.md#track-data)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M24 · The Glassway II: fishing, the Starsong and the Sunken Bell**. The first Act III Warden is not a
fight: an ancient turtle who kept a bell silent for a hundred years tests whether Pip listens. It proves the `wits`
encounter kind and gives the first Stair Stone.

#### What
1. **Def** `js/boss/pelagia.js`, registered in `BOSS_DEFS['glassway.pelagia']`:
   ```js
   { id: 'glassway.pelagia', save: 'sunken_bell:pelagia', name: 'Pelagia',
     title: 'Silent Warden of the Sunken Bell', kind: 'wits', model: 'boss_pelagia', height: 1.6, r: 1.6,
     keepOut: 2.6, room: 3.2, hp: null,
     arena: { realm: 'sunken_bell', anchor: 'r11:M', r: 10, shape: 'round', seal: 'bossDoor' },
     bells: { low: 'r11:x1', mid: 'r11:Y1', high: 'r11:z1' },
     rounds: [['low','high','mid'], ['mid','mid','low','high'], ['high','low','mid','low','high']],
     noteS: 0.8, splash: { r: 1.5, wind: 0.8, dmg: 1 },
     between: ['gw.pelagia.r1', 'gw.pelagia.r2'],
     handover: { lines: ['gw.pelagia.h1', 'gw.pelagia.h2'], scene: 'stone-give-bell', sit: 'rest' },
     after: { realm: 'glassway', anchor: 'glassway.pelagia', talk: ['gw.pelagia.after'] },
     plushie: { id: 'plush_pelagia', name: 'Pelagia plushie', price: 100, desc: 'A shell to hide in, a bell to ring.',
                got: 'A Pelagia plushie! The little bell really jingles~' },
     rematch: { via: 'act-bell-duel', gems: 60 },   // no Wake prompt; S-216's first Bell Duel win counts
     dream: null,
     bestiary: { card: 'card_boss_pelagia', lore: 'Pelagia remembers everything, which she says is a nuisance.',
                 tip: 'Listen first. Shell, star, moon!' } }
   ```
2. **Wits runner** `js/boss/wits.js` (the `wits` kind of creatures#encounter-kinds; pure state core + module):
   `intro → (play round n → answer) × 3 → handover`. The bar shows "Round n/3". The arena seals with the boss door's
   bronze leaf (seal `bossDoor`).
   - **Play:** Pelagia rocks and rings her shell bell once per note, **0.8 s** per note; the matching chapel bell glows
     its colour, plays its pitch and floats its shape above it: **low** teal `#3fa7a0`, G3, a shell · **mid** gold
     `#f2c14e`, C4, a star · **high** coral `#ff8f7a`, E4, a moon (bible reconciliation R7: the duel bells stand on
     the map tiles (16,33), (20,34), (24,33)).
   - **Answer:** Pip strikes the bells in the same order (any sword swing or `[E] Ring · Bell`). **No time limit.** Each
     right note lights the bell and adds its shape to a row of shapes over Pelagia (the visible answer so far).
     `[E] Ring · Pelagia` asks her to play the round again (no cost).
   - **Wrong note:** she sighs, slaps a flipper on the water; a **splash disc r 1.5 m** is drawn under Pip with a
     **0.8 s** tell (the shipped ground-circle telegraph in teal), then lands: half a heart if Pip is still inside;
     then she plays the same round again. Wrong notes never end the duel. Lines between rounds: R1 done
     `gw.pelagia.r1` "Hm. Lucky."; R2 done `gw.pelagia.r2` "...Not lucky. Again."
   - **Dizzy** (only if Pip stands in splashes at low hearts): he wakes on the r10 side of the boss door with the
     round count kept (`S.dungeons.sunken_bell.state['pelagia:round']`), Pelagia resting; re-entering resumes at that
     round (grammar rule 7, never a reset of progress).
   - Music: the `hush` track (art-audio#track-data: A minor, pad only, 60 bpm) so the bells are heard.
   - Accessibility: every note is a colour, a shape and a pitch; the answer row shows progress by eye alone.
3. **Lines** (`js/text/en/gw.js`, Pelagia's frame; she calls Pip "little one"):
   - Intro (after the name card): `gw.pelagia.i1` "Old bell. Old rules. I ring, you answer." · `gw.pelagia.i2`
     "Ring true, little one. Then we'll talk."
   - Handover (creatures): `gw.pelagia.h1` "...That was the old chapel's call. Nobody has rung it right in a hundred
     years." · `gw.pelagia.h2` "The last time this bell rang, the Ladder broke. I thought if it never rang again,
     nothing else would. Silly old shell."
   - Scene `stone-give-bell` (25 s, `js/content/scenes/glassway.js`, story#ch15-17's stone-give with creatures' third
     line folded in so "Take the clapper" is said once; record as reconciliation R13 in the PR):
     1. `{ shot: 'two', at: 'npc:pelagia' }` · Pelagia `gw.pelagia.s1` "Take the clapper, little one. It's a Stair
        Stone, you know. It's been ringing nothing for a hundred years."
     2. `{ emit: 'fx.clapperDrop', at: 'w:greatBell' }` — the great bell's clapper floats down, glowing; the bell rings
        **once**, softly (`sfx.bigBell` at 0.5 volume).
     3. Pelagia `gw.pelagia.s2` "You ring like Shellby did."
     4. Pelagia `gw.pelagia.s3` "Tell Shellby... no. I'll tell him myself. It's time I swam over."
     5. `{ emit: 'item', id: 'stone_bell' }` with "You got a Stair Stone! One of the three keystones of the old
        Starstair. Set it at the Summit Crystal Cairn." and `{ flag: { 'stoneHeld:bell': true } }`.
     `after: ['boss:sunken_bell:pelagia', 'quest:mq-bell.advance']`. Skip applies the item, flag and `after`.
     `replay: true` (replay shows the lines without giving anything).
   - After: `gw.pelagia.after` "Shellby still talks too much, I expect. Good."
4. **Item** `stone_bell` in `js/items.js`: `{ name: 'Stair Stone', kind: 'key', icon: 'stair_stone', desc: 'Pale,
   egg-shaped, about as big as a pumpkin. It hums. Set it at the Summit Crystal Cairn.' }`. In first person it is held
   in both hands at the bottom of the view only during the stone scenes (not while walking).
5. **After the handover**: Pelagia leaves the chapel (fade) and rests at `glassway.pelagia` (0, 245) on Bell Islet's
   south shore ([atlas-far#glassway-people](../bible/atlas-far.md#glassway-people)), Talk gives `gw.pelagia.after`.
   The r11 chapel stays open: the great bell hangs with no clapper. Plushie: Miso stocks `plush_pelagia` once
   `S.bosses['sunken_bell:pelagia']` (the S-045 stock rule); `S.plushies['sunken_bell:pelagia']`. Bestiary entry under
   bosses with the card, "Calmed × 1".
6. **Debug:** `G.debug.boss.answer(n)` answers round n correctly through the real bell handlers;
   `G.debug.boss.wrong()` rings a wrong bell.

#### Assets
- `boss_pelagia` (code-built per cast#pelagia, bible reconciliation R8; ≤ 3,000 tris): `sphere` shell squashed to 0.55
  height with `colorFn` hex plates `#3f7f6a` / `#5f9f86` and barnacle dots `#e8e0d0`, four flattened `capsule` flippers
  `#9fb8a0`, `sphere` head with the shared `face()` (heavy kind lids, eyes `#2a1a12`), a `lathe` bronze bell `#b8894a`
  on top, the faded ribbon `#e88a8a` on the front-left flipper. Animations by transform: breathe, rock-and-ring,
  flipper slap, slow smile (lids). 3.0 m long, 1.6 m tall. ART BRIEF for the owner's GLB (batch 11): cast#pelagia's
  brief verbatim (silhouette broad dome with four flippers and a bell knob; ≤ 12,000 tris; 3D prompt "[3D preamble] a
  huge ancient sea turtle, barnacled sea-green shell with a small bronze bell on top, faded pink ribbon on one
  flipper, 3 m long.") plus creatures' animation list. Manifest slot as a commented-out `models` entry `boss_pelagia`.
- `portrait_pelagia` (realm `glassway`): preamble + `portrait` + cast#pelagia's portrait subject; 512² webp ≤ 60 KB
  with alpha, processed with `tools/export_sprites.py`; fallback the canvas initial-in-a-circle portrait of S-032.
- `card_boss_pelagia` (realm `glassway`): preamble + `card-beast` + creatures' card subject ("an ancient gentle sea
  turtle with a barnacled green shell and a small bronze bell on top, eyes half closed, resting in a sunken stone
  chapel with light through the water"); 384² webp ≤ 150 KB; fallback canvas silhouette.
- `plush_pelagia` (code-built plushie per S-045's plushie recipe: the boss model at 0.12 scale, felt material).
- Duel bells: reuse `int_bell_*` frames with the duel colours and a painted shape decal each (shell, star, moon;
  canvas).

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except "Frozen home island". Tone: Pelagia never attacks; the only damage is the splash after
  a wrong note, with a 0.8 s drawn tell (charter A-rules; the unit test of every move's wind-up ≥ 0.55 s and damage 1
  includes `splash`).
- `bun test` (`tests/boss-pelagia.test.js`): the three sequences equal the bible; the runner advances only on the
  exact order; a wrong note replays the same round and never ends the duel; round count persists across a dizzy.
- Balance (wits): a bot that answers right finishes in ≤ 60 s; a bot that makes one wrong note per round and steps out
  of each splash finishes with no damage; one that stands still loses exactly 1 HP per wrong note.
- In-page (`bosses`): intro card, three rounds, handover, the stone scene, `stone_bell` held, `stoneHeld:bell` set,
  `S.bosses['sunken_bell:pelagia']` set, Pelagia at (0, 245) afterwards; Miso then lists the plushie.
- Lines ≤ 220 characters; Pelagia may say "Shellby" (she is not a young-cast speaker); no "Wenna" needed.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test`; `node tests/inpage.mjs bosses` (case `pelagia`: `G.debug.realm('sunken_bell','r11')`,
  `G.debug.boss.answer(1..3)`, a `wrong()` with Pip standing still and one stepping out, `G.debug.scene.skip`).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs dungeons` (sunken_bell cases still green); `bun run test:smoke`;
  `node tools/build-pages.mjs && bun run test:pwa`.

#### Out of scope
- Setting the stone and the memories (S-212a/b), the quest and unhiding (S-212c), the Bell Duel activity (S-216).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: sword swings near a bell during Pelagia's playback count as answers; input is ignored while she plays (the bells
dim). Rollback: revert; the dungeon is hidden, and a debug save holding `stone_bell` keeps an inert key item.

<a id="s-212a"></a>
### S-212a · feat: Stair Stones set at the Cairn raise Starstair flights and open memories by count
Milestone M24 · Depends on: S-211, S-190, S-095 · Size: M (about 420 lines) · Save: none · Flag: none ·
Bible: [story#stone-beats](../bible/story.md#stone-beats), [lore#memories](../bible/lore.md#memories) (Rules for every
memory), [lore#stair-stones](../bible/lore.md#stair-stones), [atlas-home#starstair](../bible/atlas-home.md#starstair),
[atlas-home#crown-lanterns](../bible/atlas-home.md#crown-lanterns), [atlas-home#passes](../bible/atlas-home.md#passes),
[systems#conditions](../bible/systems.md#conditions)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M24 · The Glassway II: fishing, the Starsong and the Sunken Bell**. CANON's count-keyed story beats:
whichever stone the player sets first, the first flight rises and memories 4-6 open; the second stone opens 7-9 (T5),
the third 10-12. This step builds that framework once, for all three sites, so S-232 and the Saltglass chain only
supply their stones. (Split from S-212, see Index changes.)

#### What
1. **Counts** `js/stones.js` (pure): `STONE_SITES = ['bell', 'kettle', 'saltglass']`; `stones(S)` = number of set
   `stone:<site>` flags; `stoneHeld(S)` = the site whose `stoneHeld:<site>` is set (at most one at a time is possible,
   but the code allows several and places them in site order). The condition grammar (S-025) gains or confirms the
   derived counts `stones` and `memories` (number of `mem:1`…`mem:12` set) and `lanterns` counts `stone:*` (each set
   stone lights one crown lantern through S-095's mechanism: if S-095 keyed slots by source, `stone:bell` lights L5
   (292.5°, bell blue `#9fd8ff`), `stone:kettle` L6 (67.5°, ember `#ff9a6a`), `stone:saltglass` L7 (202.5°, salt white
   `#f4f0dc`); if it fills by count, the count grows by one). Flag values follow story decision 2: `stone:<site>`
   stores the order number (`'next'` = one more than the stones already set).
2. **The Stone Ring** — home post-pass row 28 `placeStoneRing` on `new RNG(7151 * 223 + 151)` (reserved, no draws;
   row 27 `(211, 139)` is S-172's `placeSlowWalk`; the next free row becomes `(227, 157)`; confirm both rows 27 and 28 in
   the table in `atlas-home#passes` (add if missing) and add row 28 to the world test's collision check): a pure
   scan for the nearest cell to the Cairn's keepsake (109.4, −45.5) in the ring
   2.5-6 m that is `ok()` (atlas-home §1.3 defaults) and ≥ 2 m from the keepsake, its star door (S-208) and every
   earlier pass item; bell targets (row 21, S-227) hang on the landmark itself and are not ground items, so a later
   S-227 cannot move the ring. Record `{ id: 'home:stone-ring', x, z, y, solid: { r: 1.1 } }` in `W.stoneRing`,
   anchor `w:cairn.socket`. Look: three knee-high pale stone stumps in a triangle (r 1.2 m), each topped with an
   egg-shaped hollow carved with a spiral, facing the Cairn. Visible from `stoneSites` (story's C13 flag); before that
   not drawn.
   - Prompts: with any `stoneHeld:*` → `[E] Place · Stair Stone`; otherwise `[E] Look · Stone ring` "Three round
     hollows carved with spirals. Something egg-shaped would fit." (`home.stonering.look`). Set stones sit in their
     sockets in order (socket 1 north-west, 2 north-east, 3 south) and hum (`sfx.stoneHum`, positional, 15 m).
3. **Scene `stone-set`** (`js/content/scenes/home.js`, 25 s, `replay: true`), the story#stone-beats script verbatim
   with `<site>` bound to the held stone: `pov` 0.3 s (the stone held out) → `fx.stoneSet` at `w:cairn.socket` +
   `sfx.stoneSet` → `flag stone:<site> = 'next'`, clear `stoneHeld:<site>`, take the item → `crane` from `w:cairn` to
   `w:cairn.needle` 2.5 s → `sky.flight n = stones` → `sfx.crown`, `crown n = lanterns` → Wenna's line for the new
   count (frame `wenna`, she may speak here: "anywhere my roots go") → toast "✿ Three memory knots glow on the
   Taproot" → `after: ['quest:mq-remember.next']`. Wenna's lines (`home.scene.stone.1-3`):
   1. "I felt that. Like a step under a foot I'd forgotten I had."
   2. "Two flights. I can almost see the top. Go down to my roots, Pip. There's something I need you to see."
   3. "Three. The stair is whole. ...Go and see the last of it first. Then go up."
   Emits `EV.STONE {site, n}`.
4. **Flights** (atlas-home#starstair, meshes, not decks, not in prints): flight k (1-3) = nine floating slabs
   2.4 × 1.2 × 0.35 m rising 1.35 m per step along bearing 48° from the Stairfoot stub top; flight 2 starts 3 m past
   flight 1's last slab, flight 3 likewise; each slab bobs 0.1 m at 0.25 Hz (phase by index); edge trim `#ffe9a8` at
   `glow` 0.5; all 27 slabs one `InstancedMesh` (instances shown = 9 × stones). Flight k appears when `stones ≥ k`,
   rising from below the summit in 4 s during the scene (and already up on later loads). From every other realm the
   flights show on the shared sky layer as the `sky_starstair` card under the sky isle, frame = `stones` (0 hides it).
5. **Memory groups by count** (S-190's knots): knot group gates become data in `js/content/memories.js`:
   `groups: [{ knots: [1,2,3], open: 'flag:lumbraCalm', glow: '#ffe9a8' }, { knots: [4,5,6], open: 'stones>=1', glow:
   '#8be07a' }, { knots: [7,8,9], open: 'stones>=2', glow: '#c9b6ec' }, { knots: [10,11,12], open: 'stones>=3', glow:
   '#7fd3d0' }]`. Closed knot dark wood `#4a3a2a` with a faint pulse; open and unwatched glows its colour and thrums
   every 1.6 s (12 m, `sfx.knotThrum`); watched glows steadily and smaller. A knot whose memory data is not shipped yet
   says on Touch: "This one is still tying itself." (`rd.knot.notyet`), so a build with S-212a but not S-212b is safe.
6. **Quest `mq-remember`: extend, do not redefine.** The quest already exists from S-191b (story's `q:remember`, n 23,
   kind main, owner `wenna`, title "The Knots in the Roots", `start: 'flag:stoneSites'`, steps `group1`-`group4`). Keep
   its id, n, title, owner, start and `group1`. Replace its placeholder steps `group2`, `group3`, `group4` **in the same
   positions** (so a saved step index stays valid; no migration) with: `m456` "Watch the memories at the Taproot (4-6)"
   needs `stones>=1`, done `flag:mem:4 & flag:mem:5 & flag:mem:6`; `m789` "Watch the memories at the Taproot (7-9)"
   needs `stones>=2`, done `flag:mem:7 & flag:mem:8 & flag:mem:9`; `m101112` "Watch the memories at the Taproot
   (10-12)" needs `stones>=3`, done `flag:mem:10 & flag:mem:11 & flag:mem:12`. While a step's `needs` is false the
   journal shows S-191b's nudge "The roots wait for a Stair Stone." / answer "Set a Stair Stone on the Cairn; three more
   knots will wake." Once it holds: target `rootdeep` `taproot` spawn; nudge "My roots are remembering for you." /
   answer "Go down the Meadow Windmill root-gate to the Taproot and touch the glowing knots." `reward: []`. The content
   lint must still find exactly one quest with id `mq-remember`. (`mq-ch18` later starts on `stones>=3 &
   memories>=12`.)
7. **Compass:** while any `stoneHeld:*`, the objective reads "Set the Stair Stone · the Cairn" (≤ 32 chars) and the
   compass points at `w:cairn.socket` from any realm (the nearest exit toward home).
8. **Debug:** `G.debug.stone.give(site)`, `G.debug.stone.set(site)` (runs the scene path), `G.debug.stone.count()`.

#### Assets
- `stair_stone` (code-built, ≤ 200 tris): an egg-shaped pale stone 0.45 × 0.6 m `#e9e2cf` with a spiral inlay at
  `glow` 0.6, a faint hum shimmer; shared by the held item, the sockets and the great bell's clapper. Per-site glow
  tint (art-audio story items): bell `#7fb6c9`, kettle `#e66f4f`, saltglass `#c9a46b`.
  ART BRIEF: silhouette a smooth pumpkin-sized egg of pale stone with a glowing spiral; palette above; toon stone with
  soft inner glow; image prompt preamble + `icon` + "a pale smooth egg-shaped stone with a softly glowing golden spiral
  carved on it"; 3D prompt "stylised low-poly pale egg-shaped stone with a glowing spiral inlay, under 400 triangles,
  0.6 m". Icon cell `stair_stone` (128², items atlas) generated with the same prompt; fallback canvas egg.
- `stone_ring` (code-built, ≤ 500 tris): three pale stone stumps `#cfc8bc` 0.6 m tall with spiral hollows `#8fd8ff`.
  ART BRIEF: silhouette three weathered round stumps in a triangle; image prompt "three knee-high weathered pale stone
  stumps in the snow, each with an egg-shaped hollow carved with a spiral on top, cosy storybook style"; 3D prompt
  "three low-poly stone stumps with spiral hollows, 500 triangles". Fallback: three `cyl`.
- `star_slab` (code-built, ≤ 60 tris, instanced): ART BRIEF verbatim from atlas-home#starstair. Fallback `rbox`.
- `sky_starstair` (realm core): a 3-frame horizontal strip 768×256 webp ≤ 40 KB (frame k shows k flights rising toward
  the sky isle): preamble + `sky-card` + "a faint floating stone staircase rising diagonally into a pale sky toward a
  small floating island, drawn in three stages (one, two and three flights), soft glowing edges, transparent
  background"; slice with `tools/slice_sheet.py --parts 3`. Fallback canvas line stairs.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all. Home prints byte-identical; row 28 changes nothing else on seeds 1-40; the ring is reachable
  on foot on every seed 1-40 (test (d)); no stream collides for seeds 1-200.
- `bun test`: `stones()`, `stoneHeld()`, the `'next'` order rule, memory group gates, `lanterns` counting stones.
- In-page (new suite `stones`, file `js/debug/tests/stones.js`, CI matrix entry): `G.debug.stone.give('bell')`, warp
  to the ring, `[E] Place`, the scene plays (and skips cleanly: flags and `after` applied), `stone:bell = 1`, flight 1
  has 9 slabs visible, crown lantern lit within 10 s, knots 4-6 glow on the Taproot, `mq-remember` active at `m456`;
  then `give('kettle')` + set → `stone:kettle = 2`, flight 2, Wenna's line 2, knots 7-9 open (data-less knots say
  "This one is still tying itself.").
- Perf: the Peaks view with three flights and the ring stays within budget (one draw for the slabs).

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test` (new `tests/stones.test.js`; `tests/world.test.js` row 28; `tests/content.test.js`, which
  also asserts one `mq-remember` definition and knots 1-3 gated on `flag:lumbraCalm`).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs stones`; `node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (home views); `bun run test:smoke`;
  `bun run test:playthrough` (Act I unchanged); `node tools/build-pages.mjs && bun run test:pwa`.

#### Out of scope
- Memories 4-6 content (S-212b), 7-9 (S-232b), 10-12 (S-254b, M28). The Bell quest and the chapter test
  (S-212c). The climb portal and the needle ivy (S-264).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: a player who sets a stone before S-212b sees knots that "are still tying themselves"; S-212b follows in the same
stack. Rollback: revert; stone flags in a save stay set and are re-read when the step returns.

### S-212b · feat: memories 4-6: the seed-bearer, Wick's lamp and racing the tide
Milestone M24 · Depends on: S-212a, S-189, S-190c · Size: M (about 440 lines, mostly scene and echo data) · Save: none ·
Flag: none ·
Bible: [lore#mem-04](../bible/lore.md#mem-04), [lore#mem-05](../bible/lore.md#mem-05), [lore#mem-06](../bible/lore.md#mem-06),
[lore#memory-rules](../bible/lore.md#memory-rules), [lore#memory-echoes](../bible/lore.md#memory-echoes), [lore#memory-extras](../bible/lore.md#memory-extras),
[cast#young-cast](../bible/cast.md#young-cast), [story#stone-beats](../bible/story.md#stone-beats)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M24 · The Glassway II: fishing, the Starsong and the Sunken Bell**. "The children" group: the first
stone shows who the first kind hand was (a small turtle in a too-big hat), how young Wick lit the Ladder with her own
naming lamp, and two friends racing the tide. The player recognises Shellby by the hat; nobody says his name.

#### What
1. **Data** in `js/content/memories.js`, three entries `{ id, knot, group: 2, title, when, tod, spawn, face, cast[],
   dressing[], scenes[] }`, with every line, label, staging note, Look line and return line copied **verbatim** from
   lore.md (bible reconciliation R9: lore.md's spawns and hours win over atlas-home's table):

   | Memory | Title (card) | When / tod | Spawn · face | Scenes |
   |---|---|---|---|---|
   | 4 | "Memory 4 · The Seed-Bearer" | day 2, 09:00 | 4a near (0, 13) facing the pedestal | five chained scenes 4a-4e (each ≤ 60 s) joined by 0.6 s fades using `memory.place`: 4b near (−3, −104) facing (−3.2, −114); 4c near (106, −19) facing (114, −19); 4d near (22, 132) facing (26, 142); 4e near (0, 12) facing the Tree |
   | 5 | "Memory 5 · Wick's Lamp" | night 6, 20:30 | near (97, −50), the needle's foot, facing up the stair | one scene; `memory.lamps` lights every Ladder set from Wick's hook outward at rate 8 with `sfx.lampOn`; `shot wide` on the whole lit Ladder |
   | 6 | "Memory 6 · Racing the Tide" | day 5, 16:00 | the Shores beach near the east foot of the islet-1 bridge, facing east along the waterline toward the lighthouse (8.5, 117) | one scene; young Pelagia moves along the shallows at 1.4 m/s and young Shellby along the sand at 1.2 m/s (`go: true` steps) to the lighthouse door; the hat blows off and he runs back for it; at line 8 he ties the pink ribbon `#e88a8a` on her flipper |

   Cast per memory (models from S-189's young cast; extras per lore#memory-extras): 4 = "A young turtle" (young
   Shellby, too-big hat), two Lamplighters (some carrying lamp crates), two villagers, Young Grumble (half size, green
   light), Young Frostbun (rabbit-sized, blue light in a tiny knitted scarf), Young Pinch (smaller, no hat, pink
   light), Wenna (face decals; `smile` in 4e); 5 = Keeper Tallow (moth rig per lore#memory-extras, 1.0 m, navy coat
   `#2b2238`, chain `#c9a24a`), Young Wick, five Lamplighters; 6 = "A young turtle", Young Pelagia (in the shallows),
   Young Pinch at the lighthouse door in a folded paper hat (a white paper-hat prop).
   Dressing: 4 lamp crates on the plaza; 5 every step and landing of the old Ladder hung with unlit lamps except one
   empty hook on the first landing, the path line of lamps down the mountain toward the village (the instanced Ladder
   lamps: S-190c's `path` set, 142 lamps, plus its 4-lamp `landing` set; memory 5's run lights `stair`, `landing` and
   `path` outward from Wick's hook); 6 late sun, gulls, a sand line drawn at the start.
2. **Labels** exactly as lore#memory-rules: "A young turtle" (until `trueEnding`, then "Young Shellby"), "Young
   Grumble", "Young Frostbun", "Young Pinch", "Young Pelagia", "Young Wick", "Keeper Tallow", "A Lamplighter", "A
   villager", "Wenna". **No line says "Shellby"** (content lint over `rm.mem04-06.*` fails on the name).
3. **Look lines** (`[E] Look · <label>`, memory frame, in order then the last repeats): 4 A Lamplighter "That's the
   smallest seed-bearer I've ever seen." / A villager "Has he got a hat on, or has the hat got him on?" · 5 A
   Lamplighter "Look at that. I'll bet they can see it from the farm isle." / Young Wick "Hm. Hm." · 6 Young Pelagia
   "Ting." / Young Pinch "I folded it from a moth's old chart. Don't tell the moths."
4. **Return lines** (`rd.wenna.mem04-06`, once, `wenna` frame, voiced from the Taproot, then `mem:<n>` set): 4 "He was so
   frightened of the crab corner. He went to it last. And he went." · 5 "Oh, little Wick. She never told me that lamp
   was hers. She's still keeping a light for somebody, isn't she?" · 6 "Those two. Every afternoon that summer, racing
   the tide past my roots. She always won. He always lost the hat." After all three are watched, the one-time Taproot
   line (story#stone-beats "Coming back from a memory triple") `rd.wenna.trip2` "Small Shellby, in that hat. Oh, I'd
   forgotten the hat." (Wenna may say his name; only memory figures may not.)
5. **Music** (group 2): the music-box village theme variant "bright, with pizzicato" (the memory-box track's
   `children` variant in art-audio#track-data; if S-190 registered only one memory variant, add `{ from: 'memorybox',
   bpm: +12, add: { pizz: true } }` per the art-audio grammar).
6. **Lore tab**: Memories 4-6 appear in Field Notes → Lore → Memories once watched (replayable forever; replays set no
   flags). The Pip-is-not-there rules (no shadow, no look-at, no damage) come from S-190 and apply unchanged.
7. **Ribbon continuity:** young Pelagia wears no ribbon until line 8; adult Pelagia (S-211) already wears it.
8. **Echoes for memories 4-6** (S-190b's `echoes` field and runtime; lore#memory-echoes), verbatim from the **Echoes**
   table under lore#mem-04, #mem-05 and #mem-06 (text ids `rm.mem0N.echo.<k>`), placed with `near(x, z, 0, 4)` after
   free roam starts (for memory 4, after scene 4e, at the plaza):
   - 4: A villager near (0, −30) "A turtle went past with a lamp and a very determined face. I said good morning. He
     said "Busy.""; Young Grumble near (−3.2, −110) "I gave it to a kind hand! First try! ...Do I get a badge? Somebody
     should make badges."; Young Frostbun near (110, −21), knitting, "Knitting a new scarf. The old one went on a
     journey. Brrr-rr."
   - 5: A villager near (21.5, −4) "Wake the little ones. No, all of them. They'll want to say they saw it."; A
     Lamplighter near (10, 110) "From down here it looks like a necklace. A really long one. For a giant. A tidy
     giant."; Young Pinch near (24, 136), paper hat, "Every lamp from every isle, and not one for my lighthouse. ...I'm
     not jealous. A bit."
   - 6: Young Pelagia near islet 1 (−40, 139) "Next year I'll race the gulls. Gulls cheat. They fly."; A villager near
     (−10, 125) under a parasol "Those two again. Every afternoon. I've started bringing a chair."; A young turtle near
     (−13, 21) "The hat looks better without the ribbon. ...No it doesn't. Oh well."
   Memory 6's mote stays at the scene's end until S-212e adds the Tide Race vignette (then it rises at the finish ring).

#### Assets
- `prop_paper_hat` (code-built, ≤ 60 tris): a folded white paper boat-hat `#f4f1ea` with faint chart lines (canvas
  decal). ART BRIEF: silhouette a folded paper hat; 0.35 m; image prompt preamble + `icon` + "a little folded paper
  hat made from an old sea chart"; 3D prompt "low-poly folded paper hat, 60 triangles". Fallback: two `box` planes.
- `prop_ribbon` (code-built, ≤ 40 tris): pink ribbon bow `#e88a8a`. Fallback: `torus` loop.

#### Acceptance criteria

- `bun test`: content lint finds every `rm.mem04-06` line ≤ 220 characters, labels from the allowed list, no

  6; skipping each scene ends in the same state; replaying from the Lore tab sets nothing; after the third, the triple
  line plays once; `mq-remember` advances to `m789`.
- First person: every speaker is within 8 m of the spawn and in the default view cone, or the step before is a shot
  to them (a script check over the scene data, lore#memory-rules).
- Each of memories 4-6 shows its three echoes at the lore positions after free roam starts; each Look shows the lore line
  verbatim and sets `seen:echo:mNN.k`; the echo lint and placement test (S-190b) pass for the nine new rows; memory 5's
  wide shot with all 212 Ladder lamps lit stays ≤ 350 draw calls (lamps 2 draws).

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test`; `node tests/inpage.mjs stones`; `node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (the `remembered` row);
  `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope

### S-212c · feat: the Sunken Bell's quest: puffins, the chapel key, low tide, and the ch-bell chapter test
Milestone M24 · Depends on: S-212a, S-211, S-030, S-203 · Size: M (about 430 lines) · Save: none · Flag: unhides `sunken_bell`
(`hidden: false`) · Bible: [story#ch15-17](../bible/story.md#ch15-17), [story#act-III-reactions](../bible/story.md#act-III-reactions),
[systems#quests](../bible/systems.md#quests), [atlas-far#glassway-states](../bible/atlas-far.md#glassway-states),
[atlas-far#glassway-tide](../bible/atlas-far.md#glassway-tide), [systems#test-harness](../bible/systems.md#test-harness)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M24 · The Glassway II: fishing, the Starsong and the Sunken Bell**. The Sunken Bell becomes a chapter
anyone can play from Puffin Post: hold the tide low, find the chapel key in the smallest wreck, wade to the spire,
solve the dungeon, answer Pelagia, carry the stone home. Its chapter test guards the whole chain.

#### What
1. **Quest `mq-bell`** (`js/content/quests/glassway.js`, main, `n: 15`, starts on `flag:ch14`; systems step ids plus a
   `key` step for the story's Wreck Reach beat):

   | # | id | Journal (text id) | Done when (`on`) | Target | Hint nudge / answer |
   |---|---|---|---|---|---|
   | 0 | `people` | "Hold the tide low." | `flag:tide:glassway=0` after talking to a puffin clerk (`TALK_END`, `GATE`) | `glassway.clerk-1` | "The puffins know when the spire shows." / "Ring the Tide Bell at a tide stone in the Glassway to make the tide go out." |
   | 1 | `key` | "Find the chapel key." | `item:chapel_key` (`ITEM_GET`) | the *Wobbly Gull* hatch | "The smallest wreck sits high and dry." / "The Wobbly Gull is stuck on Gull Rock's ledge. Light the Lantern and go down its hatch." |
   | 2 | `low_tide` | "Open the Sunken Bell." | `flag:bellDepths` (`PUZZLE`) | `w:bellDoor` | "At low tide, a stone walk shows in Bell Shallows." / "From Bell Islet's south shore, walk the stone path to the spire at low tide and unlock its door." |
   | 3 | `depths` | "Find what the bell-keepers left." | `item:starsong` | `sunken_bell` | "Bells answer bells." / "Ring each bell near the door of its own pitch. Brass tubes carry a note to another room." |
   | 4 | `pelagia` | "Answer the bell." | `boss:sunken_bell:pelagia` | r11 | "She'll ring first. Listen." / "Strike the shell, star and moon bells in the order Pelagia rings them. Mistakes only splash." |
   | 5 | `place` | "Set the Stair Stone at the Cairn." | `flag:stone:bell` (`STONE`) | `w:cairn.socket` | "The stone wants to go home, high up." / "Take the Stair Stone to the stone ring at the foot of the Summit Crystal Cairn." |

   `set: ['set:stone:bell']` is applied by the scene itself; the quest's done shows "✿ The Sunken Bell".
2. **People lines** (`js/text/en/gw.js`): puffin clerk (`glassway.clerk-1`, after `ch14`, priority 70)
   `gw.clerk.bell.1` "The old spire? Shows its feet at low tide, it does. Stamped fact." · `gw.clerk.bell.2` "Tide
   stones all over. Ring one with that bell of yours. Tide goes out, spire comes up." (cast#postmistress-pell names the
   clerks; use their ids). Pell after step 1: `gw.pell.bell.1` "A chapel key? There's one on the Wobbly Gull, last I
   heard. Up on Gull Rock. Smallest wreck, biggest gull."
3. **The *Wobbly Gull*'s hold** — a hatch record on S-201's wreck (Glassway pass `placeSideMarks`, `new RNG(40253 *
   107 + 19)`, no draws): a `dark_mouth` gate (needs `lantern`, lit) at the sloop's deck hatch (≈ (183, −146), y 8);
   `[E] Enter · The hold` → interior `int_wobbly_hold` (48 m grid, one room, dark: ambient 0.22, the charter's
   dark-room floor; the Lantern lights itself):
   ```
   #####
   #..c#
   #...#
   #.@.#
   ##X##
   ```
   `c` (3,1) is a pickup on a hook: `chapel_key` item-get "You got the chapel key! A big iron key with a spiral bow. It
   smells of seaweed." (`items.js`: kind `key`, icon `key_spiral`). Look on the bunk wall: "A sailor's bunk. A note:
   'Back by teatime. — W.' The tea has been waiting a long time." Prints fixture for the interior.
4. **The chapel door** (`bell_door`, S-209's portal record): at step 2 with low tide → `[E] Unlock · Chapel door`
   (consumes `chapel_key`, sets `bellDepths`, 1.2 s door-roll, `sfx.bigLock`); from then on `[E] Enter · The Sunken
   Bell` at low tide. At high tide the walkway and door are under water; the Look line "The spire's door is under the
   tide." shows from Bell Islet's shore.
5. **Unhide** `REALMS.sunken_bell.hidden = false`; refresh `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/sunken_bell.json` one last time
   (frozen after this PR). The solver graph gains the edge `glassway:bell` with `needs: 'ability:tide & item:chapel_key
   & ability:lantern'`.
6. **Glassway states** (atlas-far#glassway-states, via `placeStates` `new RNG(40253 * 109 + 21)`, no draws):
   `stone:bell` → the spire's bell rings once each dusk at **19:00** (a bright "ting" heard across the Glassway,
   `sfx.seaBell`), a lamp burns in the belfry window at night (`glow` 0.8, `#ffe9a8`), Pelagia stays at (0, 245). The
   moment the stone is given, within 10 s: the Sunken Bell rings once across the Glassway and the spire's lamp lights
   (story#ch15-17 "The isle's change").
7. **Reactions**: the s1 column of [story#act-III-reactions](../bible/story.md#act-III-reactions) for every row (Elder
   Shellby "A too-big hat, you say? Hmph. Hats were bigger then." keyed to `mem:6` watched, per story: "Shellby's three
   columns key to memories 6, 9 and 12 watched instead"; everyone else keyed to `stones>=1`), priority 30, copied
   verbatim.
8. **Chapter test** `tests/fixtures/chapters/ch-bell.json` (S-030 format) with start save
   `tests/fixtures/saves/ch-bell-start.json` (`ch14` done; items `lantern`, `glider`, `tide_bell`, `hook`, `sunglass`,
   `mitts`, sword 2; hearts 10; Pip at `glassway` `post`; `tide:glassway` absent = high). Script: talk clerk → warp
   to Post Rock tide stone → ring (`G.debug.tool('tide')`) → assert step 1 → warp `glassway:wobbly-hatch` → enter
   with the Lantern → pick up the key → warp `w:bellDoor` → unlock → enter → `G.debug.dungeon.solve('sunken_bell')`
   (replays the solver path through the real handlers) → `G.debug.boss.answer(1..3)` → skip scenes → warp home
   `w:cairn.socket` → place → assert `stone:bell = 1`, `stones = 1`, flight 1 visible, a crown lantern lit, knots 4-6
   open, `mq-bell` done, `mq-remember` at `m456`. It writes `tests/fixtures/saves/ch-bell-end.json` for later steps.
   CI: one chapter job `ch-bell` in the matrix (S-030 convention).

#### Assets
- `int_wobbly_hold` furniture from the shared interior kit (bunk, sea chest, hook); code-built hatch on the sloop
  (≤ 80 tris). `key_spiral` icon cell (preamble + `icon` + "a big old iron key with a spiral-shaped bow, a strand of
  seaweed on it"; 128²; fallback canvas key). Belfry lamp: the shared window-glow sprite.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except "Frozen home island" (home untouched except reactions). The Glassway's prints equal
  (hatch and states are records); `sunken_bell` prints frozen in this PR.
- `bun test`: quest lint (every step has text, done and a two-part hint); the progression solver completes `mq-bell`
  from the ch14 state with only Act II tools, the Lantern and the Paws (pacing rule 6) and reports the Sunken Bell
  needs no Act III tool; the solver checks both tide states.
- `node tests/chapter.mjs ch-bell` passes and writes the end fixture; the chain check (S-030) accepts `ch-bell` after
  `ch14`.
- The dusk ring plays at 19:00 with `stone:bell`; the belfry lamp shows at night.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test`; `node tests/chapter.mjs ch-bell`; `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`; `bun run test:smoke`;
  `bun run test:playthrough` (unchanged).

#### Out of scope
- The other two stones (S-232, Saltglass). Old Friends (S-217). The Bell Duel (S-216).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: a player leaves the spire with the tide rising; the tide rule lifts Pip to the nearest dry ground ("Nell fished
Pip out"), and the door's prompt reappears at the next low tide. Rollback: revert sets the realm hidden again; a save
in the dungeon is moved to its parent spawn by S-024's Continue rule (a hidden realm on Continue loads its parent).

<a id="s-212e"></a>

### S-212e · feat: vignette 2 The Tide Race: run beside young Pelagia's prints in memory 6

Milestone M24 · Depends on: S-212b, S-190c, S-112 · Size: M (about 300 lines) · Save: none · Flag: none ·
Bible: [lore#vignette-tide-race](../bible/lore.md#vignette-tide-race), [lore#memory-vignettes](../bible/lore.md#memory-vignettes),
[lore#mem-06](../bible/lore.md#mem-06), [lore#memory-hours](../bible/lore.md#memory-hours),
[side-content#act-dash](../bible/side-content.md#act-dash), [art-audio#kinds](../bible/art-audio.md#kinds)

Added by this file (see [Index changes](#index-changes)): the playable part of memory group 2.

#### Why
Roadmap item **M24 · The Glassway II: fishing, the Starsong and the Sunken Bell**. "The children" group's vignette:
after watching the race, Pip runs it himself beside young Pelagia's flipper prints, with no timer and no loser, on the
Dash course framework players already know from Clover. It is about 4 of the memories' 90 minutes (lore#memory-hours)
and the group's one stretch of real play. Pip changes nothing: the prints are hers, and they appear where she ran.

#### What
1. **`course` vignette mode** (`js/vignettes.js`, `registerVignetteMode('course', ...)`): builds an S-112 course
   (`js/dash.js` or wherever S-112 put the framework; grep `ringGate`) with a new option `timer: false`: no start post,
   no countdown, no HUD clock, no best time written to `collect.dash`, no prize, no fail; rings light in order and a
   missed ring stays lit until passed (Pip may go back). `onRing(k)` callbacks drive reveals and overheard lines. The
   course is torn down when the vignette ends or Pip leaves the memory. S-112's timed courses must be unchanged (its
   tests stay green).
2. **Memory 6's vignette** (lore#vignette-tide-race, verbatim): `vignette: { id: 'tideRace', mode: 'course', when:
   'after', start: memory 6's spawn (the sand line) facing east, card: 'Every afternoon that summer.', rings: 10,
   end: the lighthouse door (8.5, 118.7) }`. Rings: 10 points evenly spaced along the waterline polyline from the start
   to the door (the polyline is the scene's young Pelagia `go` path, already in S-212b's data), each moved to the nearest
   cell with `h` −0.4 to 0.2 within 6 m, ≥ 4 m apart; ring 10 at the door.
3. **Prints:** when Pip passes ring k, three `decal_prints_flipper` decals appear (0.3 s fade-in each, 0.4 s apart)
   in the shallows between ring k and k + 1, on young Pelagia's `go` path; a parallel line of `decal_prints_turtle`
   (pair cell) runs on the sand 2 m inland at one pair per 1.5 m; between rings 5 and 6 the turtle line turns back
   2 m to a hat-scuff cell and comes on again. One instanced decal mesh per texture (2 draws in all); at most 60
   decals.
4. **Overheard lines** (`rm.mem06.v.1-3`, memory frame, once each, positional): ring 3 Young Pelagia (from 6 m ahead,
   in the shallows) "Keep up, keep up! The tide's winning and it hasn't even got legs!" · ring 6 A young turtle (from
   8 m behind, on the sand) "I've stopped! I'm not stopping! I'm just... resting my hat!" · ring 9 Young Pinch (from the
   lighthouse door) "Nearly there! Mind the crab hole! ...That's my crab hole. Mind it anyway."
5. **Finish:** at ring 10, a wave decal runs up the sand (1 s) and every print fades over 2 s; the gold mote rises at the
   door; free roam and memory 6's echoes (S-212b) start. Nobody wins or loses; no gems.
6. **Skip:** Pip placed at the door, prints cleared, mote up. Replays run the vignette again.
7. **Debug:** `G.debug.memory.vignette()` reports `{ ring: k, of: 10 }`.

#### Assets
- `decal_prints_turtle` (lore assets table): **generate** a 512 × 256 alpha image with two cells side by side (cell 1 a
  pair of small round turtle footprints pressed into wet sand, cell 2 a soft curved scuff where a big hat dragged),
  image prompt: art-audio preamble + `decal` suffix + "two small round footprints of a little turtle pressed into wet
  sand, and beside them a soft curved scuff mark in the sand, top-down, soft painterly storybook style, transparent
  background. Palette: #6f5a44 #b8894a #e9d8b0." Process with `tools/slice_sheet.py` (2 cells) and
  `tools/export_sprites.py` to webp ≤ 30 KB; manifest key `decal_prints_turtle` (`decals`, `remembered`, lazy).
  Fallback: canvas, two `#6f5a44` ovals at 0.35 alpha; the scuff a soft arc.
- `decal_prints_flipper`: **generate** a 256² alpha image, prompt: preamble + `decal` suffix + "one print of a young
  seal's flipper pressed into wet sand at the water's edge, top-down, soft painterly storybook style, transparent
  background. Palette: #6f5a44 #b8894a #e2c9a0."; process with `tools/export_sprites.py` to webp ≤ 18 KB; key
  `decal_prints_flipper`. Fallback: canvas `#6f5a44` teardrop fan at 0.35 alpha.
- The wave run-up reuses the shipped shore foam texture if one exists (grep `foam`), else a canvas white gradient band.
- Generated images are original and imitate no other game's art.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Unit (`tests/vignettes.test.js`): `course` with `timer: false` writes nothing to `collect.dash`, has no fail path,
  and fires `onRing` in order only; S-112's timed course tests are unchanged and green.
- In-page (`stones` suite, case `tide-race`): from a save with `stone:bell = 1`, enter memory 6, skip its scene → the
  card "Every afternoon that summer." → ten rings on walkable shallows (`h` −0.4 to 0.2, ≥ 4 m apart); the bot passes
  rings 1-10 by warping 2 m short of each and stepping through; after each ring three flipper prints appear ahead; the
  three overheard lines fire once each at rings 3, 6 and 9; at ring 10 the prints fade and the mote is at the door;
  echoes appear; nothing is written to `collect.dash`; no gems change.
- Skip mid-race places Pip at the door with the mote up; `G.debug.memory.skip()` from memory start ends memory 6 with
  the mote at the door.
- Budget: the race view down the beach with 60 decals and the rings stays ≤ 350 draw calls; decals add 2.
- Invariants: all lines (no save change; home prints unaffected).

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test tests/vignettes.test.js tests/dash.test.js tests/memories.test.js` (use the shipped name of
  S-112's test file).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs stones` (new case `tide-race`); `node tests/inpage.mjs side` (S-112's Dash cases unchanged);
  `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope
- The other vignettes (S-190c, S-232b, S-254d); any change to Clover's timed courses or their prizes; new rings art.

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: the waterline points fall in deep water on seed 7151; the nearest-cell rule with the `h` band and a placement
test catch it, and the PR shortens the course to the walkable stretch (≥ 8 rings) and records it. Rollback: revert;
memory 6 ends at its scene and the mote rises there as in S-212b.

<a id="s-212d"></a>

### S-212d · feat: Mote in the present: a note on the first stone, a figure on the stair, and the crown's pull

Milestone M24 · Depends on: S-212a, S-090b · Size: M (about 300 lines) · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/story.md#stone-beats](../bible/story.md#stone-beats), [../bible/story.md#mote-present](../bible/story.md#mote-present),
[../bible/cast.md#mote](../bible/cast.md#mote), [../bible/atlas-home.md#sky](../bible/atlas-home.md#sky)

Added in review (see [Index changes](#index-changes)): from the Firstlight theft to the ch18 chase Mote appeared only
in memories of a hundred years ago, so the antagonist vanished for the longest act and nothing at home showed the
stakes rising.

#### Why

Part of **The Glassway II: fishing, the Starsong and the Sunken Bell**. Three count-keyed beats keep Mote present
through Act III without a fight or a twist reveal: a note left on the stone Pip just set, a silent figure on the
half-built stair one night, and the Great Tree's leaves drawn upward at night a little harder for every stone. They are
keyed to the stone count, never to which stone, so every order works.

#### What

1. **Stone 1: the note** (after `stones == 1`; data in `js/content/mote-notes.js`, S-090b's runner): a
   `prop_scrap_lamp` appears on the set socket's rim (`home:mote-trace-cairn`, at `w:cairn.socket` + 0.4 m toward the
   needle; placed by the stone-set runtime, not a world pass, so prints are untouched). `[E] Read · A note`: note 4,
   "A stair. For her? Or for me? ...Either way, it will take you too long. — M" (`seen:mote-note-4`, Lore entry under
   "Mote", Hoot's margin "He came to the Cairn. In person. While I was asleep. I am going to start sleeping less.").
   The lamp stays for good (nothing missable).
2. **Stone 2: the figure on the stair** (scene `stone2-mote`, 40 s, replayable): plays once, the first time Pip is on
   home between 21:00 and 04:00 with `stones >= 2` and within 60 m of the Cairn (or arrives home at night with
   `stones >= 2`, then the compass shows "The Cairn" until it plays). Staging:
   ```js
   steps: [
     { emit: 'sfx.mote' },
     { emit: 'spawn', actor: 'mote', model: 'mote', at: 'w:starstair.flight2.top', state: 'dim' },  // the top slab of flight 2 (S-212a's flight records); glow 0.4
     { shot: 'look', at: 'actor:mote', dur: 0.6 },
     { wait: 2.0 },                                   // he stands looking at the Tree's crown, not at Pip
     { pose: 'mote', anim: 'lookDown' },
     { say: 'wenna', frame: 'wenna', t: 'home.scene.stone2mote.1', text: 'Mote?' },
     { wait: 1.2 },
     { say: 'mote', t: 'home.scene.stone2mote.2', text: '...Don\'t.' },
     { emit: 'actor.path', actor: 'mote', to: 'sky:skyIsle', s: 3.0, go: true },
     { emit: 'sky.streak', from: 'actor:mote', to: 'sky:skyIsle', go: true },
     { emit: 'despawn', actor: 'mote' },
   ],
   after: ['set:moteSeenStair']
   ```
   No fight, no prompt; Pip's attack is disabled in scenes as always.
3. **The crown's pull** (`fx.leafPull`, `js/fx/leaf-pull.js`): from `act2done` until `mainEnding`, at night
   (19:48-04:36) on home, leaves stream upward out of the Great Tree's crown toward the sky isle's bearing: a GPU
   points system (one draw, shared leaf sprite `fx_leaf` or a canvas leaf), **particle count 20 × (1 + stones)** (20,
   40, 60, 80), each rising 6-14 m above the crown and fading; it only drifts leaves and takes nothing back (no
   bough, blossom or lantern dims). From `ch19` the Ladder's-foot particles (atlas-home#sky) replace it.
4. **Reactions** (priority 45, `js/content/dialogue/home.js`, once each, `when` as listed):
   - Shellby, after `stones >= 1` (his s2 row): "Leaves going up, at night. Trees don't do that, dear. Not on their own."
   - Shellby, after `moteSeenStair`: "You saw him on the stair. ...Then he's still close. That's something. Close is
     something."
   - Grumble Shroom (Catchers' row, at the plaza in the evening, after `moteSeenStair`): "Hmph. Little light on the
     stair last night. Didn't come down. Didn't take anything. Hmph."
   - Captain Pinch: "Aye, I saw him from the lighthouse. Stood on yer stair like a lad on a dock with no boat."
   - Frostbun: "...Saw him. Cold up there. He looked... cold." (rolled r's per cast)
5. **Text** (`js/text/en/mote.js` and `home.js`): every line above, each ≤ 220 characters.
6. **Debug:** `G.debug.mote.stair()` plays the scene; `G.debug.fx.leafPull(n)` forces the count.
7. **Tests**
   - `tests/mote-notes.test.js`: note 4 appears only with `stones >= 1`.
   - In-page (`stones` suite): with `stones = 2` at 22:00 near the Cairn, `stone2-mote` plays once and sets
     `moteSeenStair`; with `act2done` at night the leaf-pull particle count is 20 × (1 + stones) for stones 0-3; by day
     0; draw calls +1 at most.
   - Chapter tests for the stone orders (S-256) still pass (the scene is skippable and never blocks a quest).

#### Assets

- `fx_leaf` (generated, optional): preamble + `card` + "a single small green leaf with a soft gold edge, drifting,
  painterly. Palette: #7fae5a #c9e39a #f2d98a."; 128 px webp ≤ 8 KB, alpha; fallback a canvas leaf shape. Manifest
  key `fx_leaf` (core).
- Reuses Mote's model (S-090) in `dim` state (emissive 0.4) and `prop_scrap_lamp` (S-090b).

#### Acceptance criteria

- After the first stone a note sits on the socket and reads note 4; after the second, the first night at home plays
  `stone2-mote` once and sets `moteSeenStair`; from `act2done` the crown's night leaves rise with 20 × (1 + stones)
  particles and nothing is taken back.
- Shellby and the three Catchers gain their lines; every line ≤ 220 characters; Pip never speaks; Mote is never hit.
- Home views stay within budget at night with 80 particles (one extra draw call).
- Invariants: *Frozen home island* (no world pass), *Tone*, *Budgets per view*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

```sh
bun test tests/mote-notes.test.js tests/content.test.js && bun test
git diff --exit-code tests/fixtures/world-prints.json
free -h && node tests/inpage.mjs stones
```

#### Out of scope

Chapter 18 (M30, from S-264). Mote's notes on the isles (S-090b and the chain steps).

#### Risk & rollback

A scene, a particle effect and lines. Rollback: revert; Act III returns to memory-only Mote.

<a id="s-214a"></a>
### S-214a · feat: the Merry Mabel: a tilted wreck dungeon of nine rooms (hidden)
Milestone M25 · Depends on: S-201, S-143, S-122, S-053 · Size: M (about 380 lines with map data) · Save: none ·
Flag: realm `merry_mabel` hidden until S-216a · Bible: [dungeons#o3](../bible/dungeons.md#o3),
[dungeons#entities](../bible/dungeons.md#entities), [dungeons#rules](../bible/dungeons.md#rules),
[atlas-far#glassway-pois](../bible/atlas-far.md#glassway-pois), [art-audio#track-data](../bible/art-audio.md#track-data)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M25 · Glassway stories, the Merry Mabel and Moonmere**. The optional wreck dungeon reuses two Act II
tools (Hook and Tide Bell) on a broken ship: a sagging deck, flooded holds and a Root Key floating on a ring of rafts.
(Split from S-214: rooms here, Bosun Snore in S-214b, see Index changes.)

#### What
1. **Data** `js/realms/dungeons/merry_mabel.js`: copy **verbatim** from [dungeons#o3](../bible/dungeons.md#o3) the map
   `merry_mabel L1` (31 rows), legend (`h` hookbloom on a barnacled mast-stump, `,` `;` tide floor and raft, `Q` tide
   stone; digits in r2 and r9 are the sag and the tilt, walkable, 0.3 m per digit), room table, entities, ledger,
   hints. Fields: `id: 'merry_mabel'`, `name: 'The Merry Mabel'`, `kind: 'dungeon'`, grid 98 m, `palette:
   'merry_mabel'` (timber `#5a3d2b`, brass `#c9a24a`, sail canvas `#efe2c4`, bilge `#2f6f7a`, dark tint `#22180f`),
   `light: { ambient: 0.55, torches: true }` plus green sea-light porthole cards (one shared animated card), a creak
   every 5 s (`sfx.creak`, positional on a random wall), `music: 'merry_mabel'` (variant `{ from: 'depths', transpose:
   0, voices: { lead: 'uke', comp: 'strum' }, swing: 0.25 }` at 72 bpm, art-audio#track-data), `parent: 'glassway'`,
   `door: 'mabel_breach'`, `needsOnEntry: ['hook', 'tide_bell']`. Name card line 2 "aground in Wreck Reach".
2. **Rooms** exactly as the bible: r1 sign `?`(3,3) "MERRY MABEL. Crew: asleep. Visitors: welcome, quietly." (text
   `gw.mabel.sign.r1`); r2 the sagging deck (cols 14-16 open hold), hook `h` (18,4) from (13,4) landing (17,4); r3 the
   Galley, basin **A** starts high, `r3:Q1` (22,2) rings it low (copper pots float at high water, a bobbing prop); r4 the
   Cargo Hold (ceiling 7), hook `h` (39,4) from (32,4) landing (38,4), door (38,8) south; r5 Crew Quarters, wave Snippy
   Crab ×3 at (33,10), (37,10), (35,14) (the shipped `crab` species, guard and all), clear → small key `r5:k1`
   (35,12); r6 Chart Room, map `&` (25,9), gem chest `r6:c1` (21,15) 40 gems, chart table decor "A pencilled route
   that stops at the reef." (Look), locked door (20,12); r7 the Bilge, basin **B** starts high, Root Key chest `K`
   (14,10) in a raft ring (walk the row-12 rafts from the east strip at high water), `r7:Q1` (18,10) for low water,
   wave Bubble Puffer ×1; r8 Captain's Cabin, ship's log `?`(2,10) "Day 1: set sail with a fine crew. Day 2: crew
   asleep. Day 3: still asleep. Ship asleep too. Very peaceful.", gem chest `r8:c2` (1,15), boss door `B` (5,16);
   r9 the Lower Deck (ceiling 6), arena 19 × 13 tiles with the floor tilting up to the east (digits 0-7), `M` (12,23)
   spawn, deckhand spots (8,20), (16,20), (12,27), `X` (10,30) to the islet. In this step **r9 is built empty**: the
   three seals doze at their spots (scenery) and Bosun Snore is a snoring mound behind a closed hatch prop with
   `[E] Look · Hatch` "A snore comes up through the boards. A big one. Not yet."
3. **Registry and portal**: `merry_mabel` (`hidden: true`, bundle `glassway`, `parent: 'glassway'`). Portal record
   `glassway:mabel` on S-205's `mabel_breach` anchor (215, 55): `[E] Enter · The Merry Mabel`, `needs: 'ability:hook &
   ability:tide & flag:tide:glassway=0 & quest:ss-36>=1'` (systems' `ability:hook` plus the breach's low-tide rule and
   the side-story unseal of dungeons#o3; `quest:ss-36` exists from S-216a; until then the breach shows `[E] Look ·
   Hull breach` "Planks nailed across it from the inside. Someone in there is snoring.").
4. **Solver**: tide basins A and B with their stones, rafts, hookblooms, the tilt digits as walkable heights; prove the
   bible's solver path r1 → r2 (hook) → r3 (ring low) → r4 (hook) → r5 (clear, k1) → r6 (&, c1) → unlock → r7 (rafts at
   high, K; ring low, cross) → r8 (c2) → B → r9, every chest reachable, no soft-lock (both stones reachable from both
   water states; a fixture that removes `r7:Q1` must fail).
5. **Prints** `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/merry_mabel.json` (S-214b may refresh it once).
6. **Hints** `gw.mabel.hint.1-3` verbatim from the bible.

#### Assets
- `tex_ship_planks` (realm `glassway`): preamble + `tile` + "Seamless texture of old ship deck planks, warm brown with
  pale salt stains, small brass nails and a few barnacles, tileable. Palette: #5a3d2b #c9a24a #efe2c4 #2f6f7a."
  `make_seamless.py --blend 64`, 1024/512 webp ≤ 110/40 KB. Fallback `#5a3d2b` stripes.
- `int_hammock` (code-built, 80 tris: canvas hammock between two posts; fallback stretched `box`).
- `int_copper_pot` (code-built, 60 tris, floats at high water; fallback `cyl`).
- Mast-stump hookbloom: the shared `hookbloom` gate model on a barnacled `cyl` stump.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except "Frozen home island". Glassway prints equal (portal is a record).
- `bun test`: map test equal; solver path, chests, no soft-lock, the removed-stone fixture fails.
- In-page (`dungeons`): from `G.debug.realm('merry_mabel')`, hook r2 and r4; ring `r3:Q1` and `r7:Q1` (water moves over
  2.5 s, a Pip on a flooding tile is lifted); walk the rafts to `K`; r9 shows the dozing seals and the hatch Look line.
- Realm suite: every room ≤ 120 draw calls and ≤ 150 k tris; generation ≤ 0.15 s. `hidden === true`.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test`; `node tests/inpage.mjs dungeons` (case `merry_mabel`); `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`;
  `bun run test:smoke`; `node tools/build-pages.mjs && bun run test:pwa`.

#### Out of scope
- Bosun Snore and the ship's bell (S-214b). ss-36, unhiding and the lighthouse bell (S-216a).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: the tilt digits make the r9 floor a staircase rather than a slope; heights step 0.3 m per tile, under the hop
height, and S-214b adds the slide. Rollback: revert; realm hidden.

<a id="s-214b"></a>
### S-214b · feat: Bosun Snore on the tilted lower deck, and the ship's bell
Milestone M25 · Depends on: S-214a, S-045, S-209 · Size: M (about 420 lines) · Save: none · Flag: realm still hidden ·
Bible: [creatures#boss-bosun-snore](../bible/creatures.md#boss-bosun-snore), [creatures#boss-def](../bible/creatures.md#boss-def),
[creatures#boss-grammar](../bible/creatures.md#boss-grammar), [creatures#balance-targets](../bible/creatures.md#balance-targets),
[cast#bosun-snore](../bible/cast.md#bosun-snore)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M25 · Glassway stories, the Merry Mabel and Moonmere**. An optional bout with a sleeping walrus whose
openings come from the arena itself: roll him into a dozing seal and he wakes flustered. It gives the ship's bell
that ss-36 carries home to Captain Pinch.

#### What
1. **Def** `js/boss/snore.js`, `BOSS_DEFS['glassway.snore']`: `save: 'merry_mabel:bosun'` (systems#save-plan's key),
   name "Bosun Snore", title "Sleeping Bosun of the Merry Mabel", `kind: 'bout'`, `model: 'boss_snore'`, `height: 1.4`
   (lying), `r: 1.3`, `hp: 80`, `enrageAt: 0.5`, `cool: [1.2, 2.0]`, `arena: { realm: 'merry_mabel', anchor: 'r9:M', w: 20,
   d: 12, shape: 'rect', seal: 'bossDoor', tilt: { deg: 8, dir: 'east' } }`. Moves (creatures table):

   | Move | Tell (sound + body + ground) | Shape | Wind | Active | Opening | Weights P1 / P2 |
   |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
   | `sleeproll` | a long snort, he rocks twice | stripe from him downhill to the low rail through Pip's line; a seal in the lane shows a gold "!" | 1.0 | 5 m/s | hits the rail: none (he sleeps on); **rolls into a seal**: the seal wakes with a honk, Snore wakes flustered, **2.5 s gold** | 50 / 40 |
   | `snore` | an inhale whistle, his whiskers lift | ring expanding at 4 m/s to r 8, height 0.5 | 0.9 | - | - | 50 / 35 |
   | `doublesnore` (P2) | two inhales | two rings 0.6 s apart | 0.9 | - | - | 0 / 25 |

   All moves deal 1 HP; rings are hopped or rolled through (the shipped ring idiom). **Escalation at 50 %**: the deck
   creaks and the lanterns swing for 2 s (warning), then the tilt flips to **8° to port** (rolls run west); `speedK`
   1.4. **The slide**: on the tilted deck a standing Pip (no move input for 0.25 s) slides 0.5 m/s downhill; walking
   cancels it (a `player.mods.slide` hook, never a change in `player.js` logic). Grammar rules hold (≤ 2 threats; shrug;
   passive contact never hurts).
2. **Seal deckhands** **Doze**, **Drowse** and **Nod** (cast names) at (4, 0), (−4, 3), (0, −4) m from the deck centre
   (the map's (8,20), (16,20), (12,27)): `hittable: false`, never attack. A seal hit by a roll wakes (honk, sits up,
   2 s) and dozes off again 8 s later; each seal can be woken any number of times.
3. **Handover** (creatures lines, `js/text/en/gw.js`, Snore's tic "...zzzrk..." per cast): `gw.snore.h1` "SNRRK...
   wha? Who's on watch? ...Nobody's on watch." · `gw.snore.h2` "We ran aground in the last big storm, and I thought,
   I'll just rest my eyes till the tide comes in. That was two summers ago." · `gw.snore.h3` "The bell? Take it to that
   crab with the hat. A ship's bell ought to ring somewhere." Reward: key item `ships_bell` (systems id) "You got the
   Merry Mabel's bell! Brass, barnacled, and still ringing true." Intro line (after the name card) `gw.snore.i1`
   "Mine... zzzrk... the bell's mine... zzzrk... finders keepers." Sets `S.bosses['merry_mabel:bosun']`.
4. **After**: per side-content#ss-36 (bible reconciliation R16 below): Bosun Snore dozes on Gull Rock's lower ledge
   afterwards (`glassway.snore` spot `near((185, −160), 0, 4)`, placed by the Glassway `placeSideMarks` pass, no
   draws); Talk: `gw.snore.after` "Tide's in. Well. Tide was in. Some time ago." His deckhands stay aboard the Mabel's
   lower deck. **No plushie.** **Rematch**: `{ hpK: 1.25 (100 HP), enrage: 0.75, gems: 60, verb: 'Wake' }` from his
   Gull Rock spot, loading the r9 arena; the deck tilts as before. No Dream.
5. **Balance test** (`tests/inpage` boss suite, creatures#balance-targets): a careful bot (rolls him into seals,
   hops rings) wins ≥ 90 % of 20 seeded runs in 45-90 s losing ≤ 3 hearts; a never-dodging bot wins ≤ 50 %.
6. **Bestiary** under bosses: Hoot "Bosun Snore has slept through two storms and a tea party. Mine." · Clover "Make
   him roll into a seal. They wake him up!"; card `card_boss_snore`.

#### Assets
- `boss_snore` (code-built per cast#bosun-snore, R8; ≤ 3,000 tris; 2.4 m long): large `lathe` body `#b8866a`, two
  `cone` tusks `#fff8e6`, bushy whisker tufts `#f3ead2`, a navy-and-white striped jersey (`colorFn` bands `#2a3a6a` /
  `#f4f1ea`), red woolly hat `#c2563f`, brass whistle `#c99a4a` on a cord; closed-eye arcs; transform animations
  breathe, snort-and-rock, roll (whole-body tumble), flustered sit-up. ART BRIEF (owner GLB, batch 11): cast's brief
  verbatim (≤ 10,000 tris, 3D prompt "[3D preamble] a vast sleepy walrus sailor, striped jersey, red woolly hat, tusks,
  whiskers, brass whistle, 2.4 m long.") + creatures' animation list. Commented-out `models` slot.
- `seal_deckhand` (code-built, ≤ 500 tris, 3 instances): `otter` rig without ears, flipper arms, 0.9 m, grey
  `#8f97a3`, belly `#d8dde2`, striped knitted caps `#2f7d8c` / `#f3e6c8`. ART BRIEF per creatures#boss-bosun-snore
  ("Seal deckhands").
- `int_ship_bell` (code-built, ≤ 150 tris): the 0.6 m brass bell on a bracket with a rope pull; ART BRIEF verbatim
  from dungeons#o3 (image prompt "<style line> An old brass ship's bell on a wooden bracket with a frayed rope pull, a
  little barnacled, cosy."; 3D prompt "Stylised low-poly brass ship's bell on a wooden bracket with a rope pull,
  hand-painted, game-ready, under 400 triangles, 0.6 m."). Also the held key-item model.
- `portrait_snore`, `card_boss_snore` (realm `glassway`): preamble + `portrait` / `card-beast` + the cast portrait
  subject and creatures' card subject; 512² ≤ 60 KB and 384² ≤ 150 KB webp; canvas fallbacks.
- Music: `warden_bosun` (`{ from: 'warden', voices: { lead: 'uke' }, swing: 0.2 }`).

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except "Frozen home island". Every move's wind-up ≥ 0.55 s and damage 1 (unit test); seals are
  never damaged; passive contact never hurts.
- `bun test`: def validates; the tilt flip at 50 % changes roll direction; slide speed 0.5 m/s only when idle.
- Balance test passes (numbers in the PR body).
- In-page (`bosses`): bout, handover, `ships_bell` given once, `S.bosses['merry_mabel:bosun']`, Snore at Gull Rock,
  rematch via Wake from there.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test`; `node tests/inpage.mjs bosses` (case `snore`, with `G.debug.actors.boss('glassway.snore')`
  move forcing); `node tests/inpage.mjs dungeons`; `bun run test:smoke`; `node tools/build-pages.mjs && bun run test:pwa`.

#### Out of scope
- ss-36, unhiding the Mabel and the lighthouse bell (S-216a).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: the idle slide fighting the dizzy/knockback code; the slide only applies in state `normal` with no input.
Rollback: revert; hidden realm; a debug save with `ships_bell` keeps an inert key item.

<a id="s-215"></a>
### S-215 · feat: side stories 34 Return to Sender and 35 The Collector
Milestone M25 · Depends on: S-203, S-075, S-078 · Size: M (about 470 lines) · Save: none · Flag: none ·
Bible: [side-content#ss-34](../bible/side-content.md#ss-34), [side-content#ss-35](../bible/side-content.md#ss-35),
[lore#bottles](../bible/lore.md#bottles), [cast#postmistress-pell](../bible/cast.md#postmistress-pell),
[cast#mr-whelk](../bible/cast.md#mr-whelk), [atlas-far#glassway-interiors](../bible/atlas-far.md#glassway-interiors)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M25 · Glassway stories, the Merry Mabel and Moonmere**. Two Glassway stories that send Pip back across
the whole world: every Bottle Chain letter gets "delivered" to the place it talks about, and a hermit crab gets a new
home that turns out to be Captain Pinch's childhood shell.

#### What
1. **ss-34 Return to Sender** (`js/content/quests/glassway.js`, `n: 134`, owner `pell`, unlock `flag:ch14`, reward 5
   gems per delivery, total 55):
   - Step `ask` "Pell wants every letter delivered": Talk with Pell with ≥ 1 bottle found (`collect.bottle` any key
     except `home:12`). Line `pell.1` "Every letter deserves to arrive. Even ones addressed 'to whoever'. Whoever is
     somewhere. Usually."
   - Step `deliver` "Deliver the bottle letters (n/11)": on each later Talk, Pell gives a key item `letter_copy_<n>` for
     every found bottle `n` (1-11) whose copy was not yet given ("Copied! Off you go, sender. Stamped!"). With the copy
     in hand, a **letterbox** (0.9 m post, a painted box, the letter's "years ago" from lore#bottle-table carved on it:
     "1,200 years") appears at its destination; `[E] Give · Letter` takes the copy, sets `ss34.sent:<n>`, pays 5 gems,
     cycles `pell.2` ("Delivered ✿ Stamp?" / "Delivered! I'll mark the ledger." / "Another one home." — shown as a
     toast in Pell's frame). Pell's ledger in Puffin Post shows a gold star per delivery. Bottle 12 (home, shipped):
     "It found you. That's delivery." (`pell.12`, said once if asked).
   - Done when all 11 are sent: `pell.3` "Eleven letters, eleven letterboxes. A thousand years of post, delivered. I
     need a sit down." Journal open/done lines verbatim from the bible.
   - **Letterbox spots** (destinations per side-content#ss-34; each a record that is drawn only while its copy is held
     or after delivery):

     | n | Destination | Placement |
     |---|---|---|
     | 1 | the Summit Crystal Cairn | home row 29 `placeLetterboxes`, `new RNG(7151 * 227 + 157)` (next free becomes `(229, 163)`; rows 27 and 28 are S-172 and S-212a): `near(Stone Ring (S-212a), 2, 5)` |
     | 2 | Grumble's Crown | `near(Crown keepsake, 2, 5)` |
     | 3 | Pinch's Lighthouse door | `near(lighthouse door, 2, 4)` |
     | 4 | the Meadow Windmill | `near(windmill door, 2, 5)` |
     | 5 | the well | `near(well (9, −7.5), 2.5, 4)`, clear of Puddle's rim perch |
     | 6 | the pedestal | `near(pedestal (0, 7.3), 3, 6)` outside the plaza's NPC ring |
     | 7 | the Lamplighters' Tower foot | Duskwick `placeIsleStories` `new RNG(40211 * 73 + 7)`, `near(Tower foot, 2, 6)` |
     | 8 | the Whistling Pine | Pinewhistle `placeIsleStories` (40223), `near(pine foot, 2, 6)` |
     | 9 | the Great Arch | Archwater `placeIsleStories` (40231), `near(arch east foot, 2, 6)`, dry at both tides |
     | 10 | Tansy Mill | Threemills `placeIsleStories` (40237), `near(Tansy door, 2, 5)` |
     | 11 | the Academy bell tower | Saltglass Flats: added by that land's finds step (see Index changes); until then copy 11 shows "Saltglass Flats · not yet charted" in the item detail |

     The home pass obeys atlas-home §1.1 (draws only from its own stream, rule-4 margins, world test seeds 1-40). Each
     isle pass is additive and leaves its realm prints equal.
2. **ss-35 The Collector** (`n: 135`, owner `whelk`, unlock `flag:ch14`, reward 35 gems):
   - `ask` at Mr Whelk's shop (`glassway.whelk` (−225, 40), interior `int_whelk_shop` door (−228, 44) per
     atlas-far#glassway-interiors: one room, shell shelves, a velvet cushion; build it here if S-203 did not):
     `whelk.1` "Too small. Pinches at the elbows. I'll trade my whole collection for a roomier home. Rare shells only."
   - `shells` "Find six rare shells (n/6)": `[E] Pick up · Rare shell` (key items `shell_<id>`, kind `delivery`); placed
     by the Glassway pass `placeSideMarks` `new RNG(40253 * 107 + 19)` at the nearest walkable cell within 4 m of:

     | Shell | Anchor | Condition |
     |---|---|---|
     | spiral conch | Gull Rock spiral path, (165, −135) | - |
     | glass cowrie | sandbar B2, (−208, −40) (≥ 3 m from chest c05) | low tide (on the bar) |
     | moon scallop | Hummock's back, (−130, 258) | night 21:00-04:59 (glows faintly) |
     | bell whelk | Bell Islet, (−10, 190) | - |
     | star limpet | the *Biscuit Tin*'s keel, (154, 66) | low tide |
     | sunset tellin | Post Rock's south bight sand, (−30, −150) | 18:00-19:59 (glows) |

     Off-condition the shell is not drawn and its spot shows nothing (the journal step names the condition: "Some
     shells only show at low tide, at night or at sunset.").
     **Carried shells** (side-content#ss-35): the glass cowrie and the star limpet are carried by a Gullgrab (S-204's
     `gullgrab` species with `carry: 'shell_<id>'`, the shell drawn in its beak instead of gems) that circles 4 m above
     the anchor while the shell's condition holds and `!item:shell_<id>`; it attacks as a normal Gullgrab. Bonked, it
     poofs and the shell falls to the nearest walkable cell as the pickup. If it despawns, it respawns circling on the
     next realm entry. One quest-scoped spawn each, outside the land's spawn caps.
   - `trade` "Ask around for a big empty shell": Whelk `whelk.trade` "Bigger. Redder. Ask a crab. Crabs moult. Crabs
     forget." → `pinch` "Fetch Captain Pinch's old shell": Talk with Captain Pinch (home): `pinch.1` "Me old shell? Aye,
     I outgrew it the year I got me hat. It's in the cove. Take it, lad. It's had a good life." → key item
     `pinch_old_shell` (systems id) "Captain Pinch's old shell! Red, roomy, with a lighthouse painted on it. In crayon."
   - `move` "Bring the shell to Mr Whelk": 10 s scene `ss35-move` (`replay: true`): he climbs out of his teapot, into
     the red shell, wiggles; `whelk.2` "Roomy! Red! A lighthouse painted on the side! ...Is that a crab's name inside?
     'Pinch, age 6.' Charming." World mark: Whelk's model swaps the teapot for the red shell; his stall shows the six
     rare shells under glass. Journal lines verbatim.
3. **Economy**: +55 (ss-34) and +35 (ss-35) in `js/content/economy.js`; ledger test stays ≥ 105 %.

#### Assets
- `letterbox` (code-built, ≤ 120 tris): a small painted wooden letterbox on a post, teal `#2f7d8c` with Pell's brass
  badge `#c99a4a`, a carved year plate (canvas text). ART BRIEF: silhouette a small round-topped letterbox on a 0.9 m
  post; image prompt preamble + `prop` + "a small round-topped wooden letterbox on a post, painted teal with a brass
  puffin badge, a little carved number plate"; 3D prompt "low-poly letterbox on a post, 120 tris". Fallback `box` +
  `cyl`.
- Rare shells (code-built, ≤ 60 tris each, one instanced type with per-instance colour): conch `#f2c9a0`, cowrie
  `#e8f4f2` (glassy), scallop `#cfd8ee` (glow at night), whelk `#c98b4b`, limpet `#f2e6b0` (star ridges), tellin
  `#ff9a6a` (glow at sunset). Icon cells `shell_*` (one generated `sheet-items` N = 6, 128² each; prompt "six small
  rare seashells: a spiral conch, a glassy cowrie, a pale moon scallop, a bell-shaped whelk, a star-ridged limpet, a
  sunset-orange tellin"; process `slice_sheet.py --parts 6`, `export_sprites.py`, `pack_atlas.py --append`).
- `whelk_red_shell` (code-built, ≤ 200 tris): Pinch's old red shell `#e0603a` with a crayon lighthouse decal (canvas).
- `letter_copy` icon: one cell, a folded paper with a puffin stamp (generated with the `icon` kind; fallback canvas).

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all. Home prints byte-identical; row 29 changes nothing else on seeds 1-40; each isle's prints
  equal.
- `bun test`: quest lint; letterbox placement per realm (positions deterministic, reachable); economy ledger.
- In-page (`side`, case `glassway-stories`): with bottles 1, 3 and 7 set by debug, Pell gives three copies; giving at
  the home Cairn, lighthouse door and the Duskwick Tower foot pays 15 gems and stars the ledger; letterbox 11 is
  absent; ss-35's six shells appear only under their conditions (`G.debug.tod`, `flags.set('tide:glassway', 0)`); the
  cowrie and the limpet circle in a Gullgrab's beak and drop when it is bonked (`G.debug.hit`); the trade chain
  completes and the Whelk model swaps.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test`; `node tests/inpage.mjs side`; `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`; `bun run test:smoke`;
  `node tools/build-pages.mjs && bun run test:pwa`.

#### Out of scope
- Letterbox 11 (Saltglass finds step). The Moonmere bottle itself (S-218a).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: the home letterboxes add six small solids near busy landmarks; each uses `ok()` margins and has `solid: { r:
0.25 }` only. Rollback: revert; quest flags left in a save are ignored.

<a id="s-216a"></a>
### S-216a · feat: side story 36 The Merry Mabel, and the Bell Duel with Pelagia
Milestone M25 · Depends on: S-214b, S-211 · Size: M (about 400 lines) · Save: none · Flag: unhides `merry_mabel` ·
Bible: [side-content#ss-36](../bible/side-content.md#ss-36), [side-content#act-bell-duel](../bible/side-content.md#act-bell-duel),
[creatures#boss-pelagia](../bible/creatures.md#boss-pelagia) (Rematch), [atlas-far#glassway-states](../bible/atlas-far.md#glassway-states)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M25 · Glassway stories, the Merry Mabel and Moonmere**. Captain Pinch's first ship gets its bell home,
and Pelagia's duel becomes a replayable activity with tiers. (Split from S-216: ss-37 is S-216b.)

#### What
1. **ss-36 The Merry Mabel** (`js/content/quests/home.js`, `n: 136`, owner `pinch`, unlock `flag:ch14 &
   ability:hook`, reward 40 gems): steps `ask` (Talk with Pinch at the plaza edge in the evening or his cove: `pinch.1`
   "The *Merry Mabel*. Me first ship. Went down in the great storm, a hundred year back. Nobody aboard, mind." then
   `pinch.2` "Her bell's still down there, I'd wager. A skipper misses his bell more than his boat. Don't ask me why.")
   → `wreck` "Explore the Merry Mabel" (enter `merry_mabel`) → `bosun` "Calm Bosun Snore" (`boss:merry_mabel:bosun`)
   → `bell` "Bring the Merry Mabel's bell to Captain Pinch" (`[E] Give · Ship's bell` at the lighthouse door; takes
   `ships_bell`) → done line `pinch.3` "Belay that... there's salt in me eye. Aye. Aye, that's her. Thank ye, lad.
   She'll ring at dusk." (Item ids follow systems: `ships_bell`; side-content's `mabel_bell` is the same item.)
   - **World marks**: the bell hangs on the lighthouse gallery rail (home post-pass dressing record on the lighthouse
     item of S-075, no RNG) and rings **three times at 19:00** every day, heard across the Shores (`sfx.shipBell`,
     positional, 120 m); the Mabel's mast loses its bell (atlas-far#glassway-states). Journal lines verbatim.
   - **Unhide** `merry_mabel` (`hidden: false`); its portal's `quest:ss-36>=1` now resolves. Refresh and freeze its prints.
2. **Bell Duel activity** `act-bell-duel` (`n: 210`, owner `pelagia`, opens `boss:sunken_bell:pelagia`): Talk with
   Pelagia at (0, 245) → "A Bell Duel? Ting." menu (`[E] Talk`, options "Set A", "Set B" (after A), "Set C" (after B),
   "Not now"). Three duel bells stand on posts beside her on Bell Islet's south shore at (−4, 250), (0, 252), (4, 250)
   (Glassway `placeSideMarks` records, no draws; bible reconciliation R15: side-content says "on the Sunken Bell's top
   deck"; the shore keeps the activity where Pelagia lives). Same bells as the duel (teal shell G3, gold star C4, coral
   moon E4), the `hush` track, the S-211 wits runner with `splash.dmg = 0` (harmless water, no damage: "unlike the story
   duel"). A wrong note replays the same round. Fixed sequences (L low, M mid, H high):

   | Set | Round 1 | Round 2 | Round 3 |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | A | L M H | H L M M | M H L H M |
   | B | L L H M H | M H H L M L | H M L M H L L |
   | C | M L H H L M H | L H M L M H H L | H H M L L M H M L |

   Tiers (first clear of each set): **A** 100 gems; **B** a small bronze bell for Pip's Nook (gift `nook_gift_bell`,
   kind `furnishing`; it rings when Pip walks past once S-302 displays it; the Nook's "Bell hook" display, atlas-home
   #pips-nook, shows it if that display already exists); **C** the bell charm `give:nook_gift_bellcharm` (gift item, kind `furnishing`; listed in Items under Keepsakes until S-302a puts it on the Nook's trophy stand) and `pelagia.duel.c` "...Ting. You've outrung me.
   Don't tell Shellby. He'd be insufferable." Line on start `pelagia.duel.1` "Shellby always rang the low one too
   early. Don't ring it too early. ...Or do. It made me laugh." Best set saved in flag `act.bell.best` (1-3).
   - **Rematch row**: the first Set A win sets `S.rematches['sunken_bell:pelagia'] = true` and pays the def's 60 gems
     (creatures: "the Bell Duel's first win pays the 60 gems and counts in the Rematches row").
   - Accessibility: each bell lights as it rings; the answer row of shapes shows progress.
3. **Economy**: +40 (ss-36), +100 (Bell Duel A), +60 (Pelagia rematch) in the ledger.

#### Assets
- Lighthouse bell: reuse `int_ship_bell` on a gallery bracket. `nook_gift_bell`: code-built 0.3 m bronze bell
  (`int_bell_mid` scaled 0.33). Icon cells for `ships_bell` and `nook_gift_bell` (generated `icon` kind; canvas
  fallback).

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all. Home prints byte-identical (the gallery bell is a dressing record on an existing item).
- `bun test`: quest lint; activity sequences equal the table; tier rewards paid once; ledger ≥ 105 %.
- In-page (`side`, case `mabel-bell`): from `ch-bell-end.json` + `ability:hook`, ss-36 runs start to end with
  `G.debug.dungeon.solve('merry_mabel')` and `G.debug.killBoss('merry_mabel:bosun')`; the bell rings three times at
  19:00 (`G.debug.tod(19)`); (`activities`, case `bell-duel`): Set A with one wrong note takes no damage and pays 100 +
  60 gems once; B gives `nook_gift_bell`.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test`; `node tests/inpage.mjs side`; `node tests/inpage.mjs activities`; `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope
- ss-37 (S-216b). Showing Nook gifts (S-302).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: a player calmed Bosun Snore through debug before ss-36 existed; the quest's `bosun` step reads the boss flag and
completes at once. Rollback: revert re-hides the Mabel; the gallery bell disappears; gems paid stay paid.
