<a id="s-216b"></a>
### S-216b · feat: side story 37 The Whale Who Rolls Over
Milestone M25 · Depends on: S-216a · Size: M (about 330 lines) · Save: none · Flag: none ·
Bible: [side-content#ss-37](../bible/side-content.md#ss-37), [cast#hummock](../bible/cast.md#hummock),
[atlas-far#glassway-states](../bible/atlas-far.md#glassway-states), [atlas-far#glassway-pois](../bible/atlas-far.md#glassway-pois)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M25 · Glassway stories, the Merry Mabel and Moonmere**. The Glassway's gentlest story: an islet that is
a whale with pins and needles, three gull families to rehouse, and a whole island that visibly rolls over.

#### What
1. **Quest** `ss-37` (`n: 137`, owner `hummock` (she), unlock `flag:ch14 & ability:tide`, reward 35 gems):
   - `listen` "The Snoozing Whale talks in her sleep": `[E] Talk · Hummock` at `glassway.hummock` (−125, 255), her
     head (lines start "(from below)" per cast): `whale.1` "(from below) Mmmm... pins and needles... left fin... can't
     roll... birds... mmm."
   - `nests` "Move the three gull nests to Gull Rock (n/3)": three nests on Hummock's back (S-201's back-state props;
     Glassway `placeSideMarks` records at (−135, 250), (−120, 258), (−110, 252)). `[E] Pick up · Nest` makes it a
     **carried item** (held in both hands in the FP view-model; in TP above Pip's head; while carried Pip cannot swing,
     can roll, and rolling sets the nest down safely where he stands; `[E] Pick up · Nest` again). Carrying works on the
     boat and through chart stops (the nest rides along). `[E] Place · Nest` on Gull Rock's three ledges (records at
     (160, −140) y 6, (178, −158) y 12, (165, −165) y 17, each a flat 1.2 m stone shelf; gulls land on placed nests).
     The gulls are friendly seabirds (a flapping instanced bird card), not Gullgrabs.
   - `tide` "Raise the tide at the whale's tide stone": the Bell Islet tide stone (30, 240) (the nearest; bible
     reconciliation R17: ss-37 says "the whale's tide stone", which the atlas does not place) rung for high water
     (`tide:glassway = 1`) with all three nests moved.
   - `roll` "Watch the whale roll over": 12 s scene `ss37-roll` (`replay: true`): camera `wide` from Bell Islet; the
     islet tilts and rolls 120° over 4 s (the prop group rotates about its long axis; Pip, if on it, is first moved by a
     0.4 s fade to Bell Islet's pier); a soft wave crosses the Glassway (water plane swell 0.4 m for 3 s); Puffin Post's
     stamps blow away (a paper-card burst at Post Rock if in view, else a toast "✿ The whale rolled over! Pell's stamps
     blew away."). `whale.2` "(from below) Ahhhh. ...Much better. Goodnight, little one. Wake me in another hundred
     years. Or don't." Sets `ss37.rolled`.
2. **Rolled state** (Glassway `placeStates` `new RNG(40253 * 109 + 21)`, no draws): flag `ss37.rolled` swaps Hummock's
   two prop sets and two collider/deck sets (built here as `back` and `rolled` records; the islet is a prop with a deck
   top, not terrain, so the realm prints do not change): rolled = a gentle slope flank with a new beach and a sheltered
   cove; the **Barnacle Garden** shows; chest `glassway:c10` (−125, 240) becomes reachable (its S-205 gate
   `ss-37`); a **gem bag** (35 gems, the story reward) sits in the cove (`[E] Open · Gem bag`); her blowhole puffs a
   little rainbow once a minute (an additive arc card). The gulls wheel over Gull Rock from now on.
3. Journal lines verbatim. Economy +35.

#### Assets
- `gull_nest` (code-built, ≤ 120 tris): a woven nest `#b08a5a` with three speckled eggs `#f4f1ea` / `#8f97a3`; also the
  carried item. ART BRIEF: silhouette a round woven nest with three eggs; 0.6 m; image prompt preamble + `prop` + "a
  round woven seabird nest of dry grass and driftwood twigs with three speckled eggs"; 3D prompt "low-poly woven nest
  with three eggs, 120 tris". Fallback `torus` + three `sphere`.
- `hummock_rolled` (code-built set, ≤ 1,500 tris): the flank slope, cove rocks and barnacle clusters `#e8e0d0`, skin
  `#5a7a8c` / `#c9dbe0`. ART BRIEF for the owner (batch 11, with `prop_hummock_eye`): "an enormous gentle blue-grey
  whale lying on her side in shallow turquoise water, her flank a grassy little island with a sheltered cove and
  barnacle garden, one huge closed eye with lashes, cosy storybook style"; 3D prompt "toon whale-island 60 × 26 m
  lying on its side, grassy flank, barnacles, under 6k triangles".
- `fx_rainbow_puff` (generated `fx` kind, 256² alpha webp ≤ 30 KB: "a tiny soft rainbow arc in a puff of mist,
  transparent background"; fallback canvas arc).

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except "Frozen home island". Glassway prints equal (both states are prop/deck records); the
  coverage test still passes in both tide states and both whale states.
- `bun test`: the rolled deck set is walkable and reachable from Hummock's pier; c10 is unreachable before
  `ss37.rolled` and reachable after (solver).
- In-page (`side`, case `whale`): carry three nests (one roll-drop and re-pickup on the way), place them, ring high
  tide, the scene plays and skips cleanly, the islet's colliders swap, c10 and the gem bag open.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test`; `node tests/inpage.mjs side`; `node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (Glassway views);
  `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope
- The Glassway Dash (S-220).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: a rotating prop group with deck colliders; the swap is instant at the scene's fade, never during play.
Rollback: revert; `ss37.rolled` in a save is ignored and Hummock shows her back state.

<a id="s-217"></a>
### S-217 · feat: side story 9 Old Friends: Shellby and Pelagia
Milestone M25 · Depends on: S-212c · Size: M (about 330 lines) · Save: none · Flag: none ·
Bible: [side-content#ss-09](../bible/side-content.md#ss-09), [lore#mem-06](../bible/lore.md#mem-06),
[cast#pelagia](../bible/cast.md#pelagia), [systems#boat](../bible/systems.md#boat), [charter#first-person](../bible/charter.md#first-person)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M25 · Glassway stories, the Merry Mabel and Moonmere**. The emotional payoff of memory 6: two old
friends who raced the tide a hundred years ago walk one more race on a sandbar. It also gets Shellby back in a boat,
which opens his Slow Walk legs on the other lands (ss-13).

#### What
1. **Quest** `ss-09` (`js/content/quests/home.js`, `n: 109`, owner `shellby`, unlock `boss:sunken_bell:pelagia`,
   reward 40 gems):
   - `ask` "Pelagia asks after an old friend": Talk with Pelagia (0, 245): `pelagia.1` "You ring like Shellby did. Is
     he... is the old shell still about? Does he still wear that hat?"
   - `tell` "Tell Elder Shellby": Talk with Shellby by the pedestal: `shellby.1` "Pelagia. Oh, my. Oh, my dear. ...I
     haven't been in a boat in eighty years, little sprout." then `shellby.2` "Ask Nell if she has room for an old
     shell. And don't tell Pelagia I said 'old'."
   - `sail` "Sail Shellby out to the Bell Shallows": Talk with Nell at the jetty: `nell.ss09` "Room for an old shell?
     Always." Shellby becomes a **passenger**: seated in the *Dandelion*'s bow (third person: visible; first person:
     his hat brim shows at the bottom-left while sailing) for free sailing, or riding along through chart stops in
     the fallback. Arriving at Bell Islet's pier sets the step; Shellby walks to the sandbar's west end.
   - `race` "Watch the slow race on the sandbar": needs **low tide** (`tide:glassway = 0`; the step text adds "(low
     tide)" and the hint names the Bell Islet tide stone). The sandbar is **B4** (Kelp Knoll ↔ Hummock, (−120, 200) →
     (−125, 235), 35 m; it lies in Bell Shallows). Two shells mark its ends (records, Glassway `placeSideMarks`, no
     draws). Scene `ss09-race` (40 s, `replay: true`) mirrors memory 6 **shot for shot**: same `two` framing on the
     pair at the start, same line rhythm, the two old turtles walk the bar side by side at 0.5 m/s (`go: true`); Pip may
     walk with them (the scene keeps control; first-person rule: both stay within 40° of view and 2-8 m ahead while Pip
     walks toward the far shell). Lines: `pelagia.2` "Ready? Same as always. Down to the water and back." · `shellby.3`
     "She always won." · `pelagia.3` "I always let you think I let you win." At the far end the pink ribbon on her
     flipper lifts in the wind (a 1 s close shot). Nobody is told who won. `after: ['set:ss09.done', 'gems:+40']`.
   - Shellby is taken home by the scene's fade (he is at his bench next morning).
2. **World marks**: two shells stand together at the bar's east end (a big one and a small one with a moss tuft,
   records shown from `ss09.done`); on home, **Pelagia's head shows in the shallows off the lighthouse rocks**
   17:00-19:00 (spot `home.pelagia_rocks` = `near(lighthouse rocks (6, 128), 0, 6)` on water, placed by home row 6
   `placeSpots` as an appended record with no draws, so earlier spots do not move), talkable from the beach:
   `pelagia.home` "Ting. He's slower. So am I. It's lovely."
3. **After**: `shellby.4` (home, priority 30, once) "Pelagia got me back in a boat. Now I'd like to see everything. Will
   you walk with me sometime?" — sets `ss13.legs` so ss-13's island legs open (bible side-content decision 12).
4. Journal lines verbatim (open "Pelagia remembers a turtle in a too-big hat. I know only one turtle. His hat is a
   reasonable size now." / done "They raced. Nobody won. Both of them say they did.").

#### Assets
- `shell_pair` (code-built, ≤ 120 tris): a big pale shell `#e8e0d0` and a small one with a moss tuft `#7fae4b`.
  ART BRIEF: silhouette two seashells side by side on a sandbar; image prompt preamble + `prop` + "two seashells
  standing together on wet sand, one big and pale, one small with a tuft of moss on top"; 3D prompt "two low-poly
  seashells, 120 tris". Fallback two `cone`s.
- Shellby as passenger reuses his shipped GLB (no change to the model; a sit pose by transform).

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all. Home prints byte-identical (Pelagia's home spot is an appended record).
- `bun test`: quest lint (each step has text, done, a two-part hint); line lengths; Pelagia and Shellby may say
  "Shellby" here (they are not memory figures).
- In-page (`side`, case `old-friends`): from `ch-bell-end.json` (+ `boss:sunken_bell:pelagia`), run all steps with the
  boat in both `sail` modes (`free`, and `chart` via `REALMS.glassway.sail = 'chart'`), the race at low tide only, the
  scene skips cleanly, `ss09.done`, Pelagia's head at the rocks at 18:00 and not at 20:00.
- First person: the scene's partner rule holds (a check over the scene data: actors stay within 40° and 2-8 m ahead).

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test`; `node tests/inpage.mjs side`; `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope
- Shellby's Slow Walk legs (ss-13's step). Pelagia in the ch22 plaza (later).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: a passenger on the boat during free sailing; the passenger is a child of the boat mesh with no collider.
Rollback: revert; the quest flags are ignored.

<a id="s-218a"></a>
### S-218a · feat: Moonmere: the moonpath off Nell's Jetty leads to Mote's mirror-home (hidden)
Milestone M25 · Depends on: S-188, S-208, S-212c · Size: M (about 470 lines) · Save: none · Flag: realm `moonmere`
hidden until S-219 (bible reconciliation R10) · Bible: [atlas-home#moonmere](../bible/atlas-home.md#moonmere),
[atlas-home#placeJetty](../bible/atlas-home.md#placeJetty), [lore#moonmere-lore](../bible/lore.md#moonmere-lore),
[systems#realm-registry](../bible/systems.md#realm-registry), [systems#portal-table](../bible/systems.md#portal-table),
[art-audio#variants](../bible/art-audio.md#variants), [art-audio#track-data](../bible/art-audio.md#track-data)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M25 · Glassway stories, the Merry Mabel and Moonmere**. Moonmere is the home isle as Mote saw it from
the sky for a hundred years: silver, still, a little wrong, hung with his scrap lamps. It is the first place the
player stands inside Mote's longing before meeting him properly. (Split from S-218: enemies are S-218b.)

#### What
1. **Registry** `js/realms/index.js`: `moonmere: { id: 'moonmere', name: 'Moonmere', kind: 'variant', variantOf:
   'home', size: 360, seed: 7151, palette: 'moonmere', music: 'moonmere', ambience: 'moonmere', hidden: true, open:
   'flag:stone:bell', arrive: 'moonpath', tod: 0.0208 /* 00:30 always */, bundle: 'moonmere', enemies: 'moonmere',
   views: [...] }`. Prints equal home (S-188's variant test). Variants never count as visiting home.
2. **The moonpath (home)**: the appended entry of pass row 5 `placeJetty` (no draws, so the jetty does not move): a
   `deck` 2.0 m wide, 27 m long from the T-head's south edge due south (stops at z ≤ 176), top Y 0.15, `active: false`.
   It becomes **active** when the Starsong's *Calling* is played on the T-head at night (21:00-04:59) with
   `stone:bell` set (S-208's runtime `moonpath` state), until the next 05:00. It renders as a shimmering silver ribbon
   (`moonpath_strip`, additive, scrolling) only while active. Feed "✿ The moon lays a path on the water." Walking off
   its far end (a 3 m walk-in trigger) is portal `home:moonpath` (`needs: 'flag:stone:bell & tod:night &
   runtime:moonpath'`) → `moonmere` · `moonpath`. Calling at the T-head by day: "The moon isn't up." (toast).
3. **Skin** (palette `moonmere`, art-audio#variants + atlas-home): grade `{ sat: 0.35, tint: '#cfd8ee', k: 0.55 }`
   through the shared grade uniform (no new shader programs); sky top `#2a3358`, horizon `#5a6a9a`, fog `[24, 220,
   '#cfd8ee', 0.40]`, moon light `#f6f3d8` at 0.9, the moon disc 2.5× size low in the south; hemisphere `#cfd8ee` /
   `#2a3358`; water `#1e2848` → `#cfd8ee` shallows, glint × 2; round-tree leaves and palm fronds silver-white; flowers
   swapped to pale bell-flowers; silver motes instead of fireflies. Home post-pass items are hidden except the jetty,
   benches (not usable: "Not here."), and the Stone Ring (empty). No NPCs. Dizzy wakes Pip at the Moon Tree.
4. **Variant pass v2** `new RNG(7151 * 197 + 131)` (atlas-home row v2), "slightly wrong, as seen from far above":
   every cottage wears the **same roof** (cottage 1's); the windmill has **six sails**; the signpost (4, 17.5) has an
   extra arm pointing straight up reading "HOME"; the lighthouse's **stripes run the other way**. Plus:
   - **The Moon Tree**: the Great Tree in silver (bark tint `#e8eef8`, leaves `#cfd8ee`, `glow` 0.3), eyes closed,
     hung with **40 scrap lamps** (jam jars, tin cans, a teapot, a colander, a lantern with a cracked pane) in one
     instanced draw, swaying.
   - **Twelve moon-lanterns** `moonmere:lantern-1..12` (silver lanterns on posts, unlit), each `near(anchor, 0, 4)` by
     `ok()`: 1 the moonpath landing (Moonmere's T-head root); 2 the plaza signpost (4, 17.5); 3-6 in front of each
     landmark (Grumble's Crown (10, −131), the Cairn's east foot (109.4, −45.5), the lighthouse door, the windmill
     door); 7-9 at each arena's gate (Woods (−3.2, −114), Peaks (114, −19), Shores (26, 142)); 10-12 in each fairy ring
     ((−36.1, −44.8), (23.6, −106.9), (−16.9, −86.7)). `[E] Kindle · Moon-lantern` with the Glowbud Lantern lights it
     for good (`gate:moonmere:lantern<n>`), toast "Moon-lanterns n/12". A lit moon-lantern makes ghosts hittable within
     5 m (S-218b). In this step a lit lantern opens to show an empty base plate (S-219 puts a letter there).
   - **The Mirror Garden** (plaza): 12 oval standing mirrors (1.6 m) in silver frames on a ring r 11 m round the Moon
     Tree's trunk (0, −1.5), every 30° from bearing 15°, each nudged ≤ 1 m to clear the pedestal. Before all twelve
     lanterns are lit: `[E] Look · Mirror` "A mirror. It only shows the moon." When all twelve are lit the garden wakes:
     toast "✿ The mirrors are shining.", the mirrors show the painted moonlit sky (`mirror_face`) and each Look line
     becomes "A mirror. For a moment it shows the real isle, warm and gold, and then the moon again."
   - **The bottle** `moonmere:11` (Bottle Chain 11, Yuzu's letter, lore#bottle-table) on islet 2's beach,
     `near(islet 2, 0, 5)`, the S-075 pickup.
5. **Exit**: the far end of Moonmere's own moonpath (south off its T-head, the same strip, always active here) is a
   walk-in portal back to home `jetty_end` at any time; the pause menu also offers "Walk back down the moonpath".
6. **Music and ambience**: track `moonmere` = `{ from: 'mote', bpm: 70, voices: { lead: 'musicbox' }, add: { pad: {
   voice: 'choir', vol: 0.55 } }, delay: { beats: 1, fb: 0.5, mix: 0.4 } }` (if the `mote` track is not shipped yet,
   `from: 'village'` with the same overrides); ambience `{ waves: [400, 0.05], wind: [500, 0.5, 0.02, 0.01], ev:
   [['ambTwinkle', 0.12, -1], ['ambOwl', 0.03, -1]] }`.
7. **Views** (`REALMS.moonmere.views`): densest = the Mirror Garden from (0, 18) yaw 0; landmark = the Moon Tree from
   (−20, 30) yaw 30; town = the plaza signpost (4, 20) yaw 300; widest = Moonmere's T-head looking north (−14, 140)
   yaw 0. Budgets as home (the variant adds ≤ 6 draws: lamps, lanterns, mirrors, moonpath, motes, grade pass none).

#### Assets
- `load_moonmere` (bundle `moonmere`): preamble + `loading` + "The same island as a silver dream seen by moonlight: a
  great tree of silver hung with small mismatched lamps, a still mirror-smooth sea with a path of moonlight across it,
  a garden of mirrors. Palette: #cfd8ee #2a3358 #f6f3d8 #c9b6ec." 1600×900 + 960×540 webp ≤ 200 / 60 KB.
- `moonpath_strip`: ART BRIEF/prompt verbatim from atlas-home#placeJetty ("a narrow ribbon of glittering silver
  moonlight reflected on calm night water, top view, tileable lengthwise, painterly, transparent background"),
  256 × 1024 webp alpha; fallback canvas noise stripes `#f6f3d8` / `#cfd8ee`.
- `moonmere_card_moonpath`, `moonmere_card_mirror` (art-audio cards, `card` kind, prompts verbatim; ≤ 90 KB each).
- `moon_tree_lamps` (code-built, ≤ 80 tris each, one instanced type with 5 shapes by instance): ART BRIEF verbatim
  from atlas-home#moonmere. `moon_lantern` (code-built ≤ 200 tris) and `mirror_plinth` (≤ 150 tris, glass face
  `mirror_face` 256 × 512 webp: preamble + `card` + "an oval mirror showing a painted moonlit night sky with soft
  stars"): ART BRIEFs verbatim from atlas-home#moonmere. Fallbacks as listed there.
- Variant wrongness pieces are transforms or recolours of shipped props (no new models).

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all. Home prints byte-identical (the moonpath and Pelagia spots are appended records); `moonmere`
  prints equal home (variant test).
- `bun test`: registry row; portal condition parsing; the moonpath deck is inactive by default; lantern placement
  deterministic and reachable on seed 7151.
- In-page (`realm`, case `moonmere`): `G.debug.flags.set('stone:bell', 1)`, `tod(22)`, warp to the T-head, play
  Calling → the strip activates and the walk-in enters Moonmere at 00:30 with the silver grade; the four wrong details
  exist; kindling 12 lanterns wakes the garden; the exit returns to `jetty_end`; at 05:00 the home strip deactivates.
- Realm suite: Moonmere's 4 views within budget in both cameras; no leak on 5 enter/leave cycles.
- `REALMS.moonmere.hidden === true` (the portal works in tests through `G.debug.realm`; players reach it in S-219).

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test` (`tests/realms/moonmere.test.js`: prints equal home, lantern spots, grade values; `tests/world.test.js`
  row 5 append changes nothing else); `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`; `bun run test:smoke`;
  `node tools/build-pages.mjs && bun run test:pwa` (new `moonmere` bundle ≤ 2.5 MB).

#### Out of scope
- Hushlings and the silver species (S-218b). The letters, ss-47 and unhiding (S-219).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: the variant must not leak home's live state (shop, NPC schedules) into Moonmere; the variant's NPC set is empty
and the shop prop is hidden. Rollback: revert; hidden realm; the moonpath strip stays inactive.

<a id="s-218b"></a>
### S-218b · feat: Moonmere's creatures: Hushlings and the silver home species
Milestone M25 · Depends on: S-218a, S-042 · Size: S (about 240 lines) · Save: none · Flag: realm still hidden ·
Bible: [creatures#enemy-hushling](../bible/creatures.md#enemy-hushling), [creatures#silver-variants](../bible/creatures.md#silver-variants),
[atlas-home#moonmere](../bible/atlas-home.md#moonmere) (Enemies), [creatures#bestiary](../bible/creatures.md#bestiary)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M25 · Glassway stories, the Merry Mabel and Moonmere**. Moonmere needs gentle resistance that teaches
its own rule: things here are only "bonkable" in light, so the moon-lanterns matter twice.

#### What
1. **Species `hushling`** (`ENEMY.hushling`, ghost archetype of S-042, tier 2): HP 4, r 0.44, floats 0.3-1.0 m,
   emerge `fade`, gems 2 (tier 2 drop), poof `#f6f3d8` (moon dust). Params `{ drift: 1.1, bumpWind: 0.65, bump: 3,
   bumpT: 0.3, tier: 2, split: true }`. Behaviour: hittable only inside light: the Lantern's radius (6 m, 9 m with the
   Bright Wick) or within **5 m of any lit moon-lantern**; tell: **goes "shh" and swells for 0.65 s**, then bumps 3 m
   (1 HP); at half HP it splits into two halves of 2 HP each (the halves count as one swarm token). Model (code-built,
   ≤ 500 tris): a soft navy blob `#2a3358` at 0.8 alpha with a silver fresnel rim `#cfd8ee`, two moon-cream eyes
   `#f6f3d8`, one tiny capsule arm raised to its lips. Animations by transform: sway, "shh", split.
2. **Silver variants** (not bestiary entries): `slime_silver`, `mushroom_silver`, `bat_silver`, `bee_silver`,
   `crab_silver`, `snowpuff_silver` = the shipped home species with **the same params and tells, HP + 1**, gem tier 2,
   material swap only (main → `#cfd8ee`, shade → `#2a3358`, accents → `#f6f3d8`), poof into moon dust. They count
   toward their home species' bestiary entry; the home card gains a small silver "Moonmere" stamp once one is poofed.
   (Use the shipped species ids; if the shipped slime id differs, keep the shipped one.)
3. **Spawn table** `moonmere` (village region none, as home; caps as home 7 / 6 / 6 / 6):

   | Region | Weights |
   |---|---|
   | Woods | `hushling 3, slime_silver 3, mushroom_silver 2, bat_silver 2` |
   | Peaks | `hushling 2, snowpuff_silver 3` |
   | Shores | `hushling 2, crab_silver 3` |
   | Meadow | `hushling 3, bee_silver 2, bat_silver 2` |
   | Sprout Village | none (town) |

   Never within 12 m of the Mirror Garden ring or the moonpath landing.
4. **Bestiary** `hushling`: Hoot "Hushlings say 'shh' to everything, including me." · Clover "Moon-lanterns or your
   Lantern. Then they're bonkable." Card `card_hushling`.

#### Assets
- `card_hushling` (bundle `moonmere`): preamble + `card-beast` + "a soft navy shadow creature with a silver outline and
  moon-cream eyes, holding a little finger to its lips, in a silver moonlit garden." 384² webp ≤ 150 KB (budget row
  says 35 KB for beast cards in this bundle: export at q70 to meet it). Fallback canvas silhouette.
- ART BRIEF `enemy_hushling` (owner batch 11): silhouette a soft shadow blob with one tiny arm raised to its mouth;
  0.7 m; palette `#2a3358`, `#cfd8ee`, `#f6f3d8`; toon at 0.8 alpha with silver fresnel; animations sway, "shh",
  split; 500 / 2,000 tris; 3D prompt "stylised low-poly soft navy shadow blob creature with a silver rim and a tiny
  arm raised to its lips, under 2,000 triangles, 0.7 m".

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except "Frozen home island" (home untouched). Tone: Hushlings poof; every move's wind-up ≥ 0.55 s
  and damage 1 (unit test over all moves).
- `bun test`: species def validates; spawn table parses; silver variants have HP + 1 and identical tells.
- In-page (`species`, case `hushling`): a swing outside light passes through; inside the Lantern's radius or near a lit
  moon-lantern it hits; the 0.65 s swell precedes damage; the split at half HP; no spawn in the village or the garden.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test`; `node tests/inpage.mjs species`; `node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (Moonmere views with enemies).

#### Out of scope
- The letters and unhiding (S-219).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: ghosts unhittable in a night realm frustrate players without the Lantern; the Lantern is required to enter
Moonmere's main activity anyway, and lit moon-lanterns add safe pools. Rollback: revert; Moonmere spawns nothing.

<a id="s-219"></a>
### S-219 · feat: side story 47 Letters Never Sent and the Mirror Garden
Milestone M25 · Depends on: S-218b, S-033a, S-203 · Size: M (about 400 lines, a third of it letter text) · Save: none · Flag:
unhides `moonmere` · Bible: [side-content#ss-47](../bible/side-content.md#ss-47), [story#ch15-17](../bible/story.md#ch15-17), [lore#mote-letters](../bible/lore.md#mote-letters),
[lore#asset-moonmere-paper](../bible/lore.md#asset-moonmere-paper), [lore#spoiler-ladder](../bible/lore.md#spoiler-ladder),
[systems#journal](../bible/systems.md#journal)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M25 · Glassway stories, the Merry Mabel and Moonmere**. Twelve unsent letters from a brother to his
sister, one in each moon-lantern: the player learns Mote's side in his own voice, in any order, long before the
finale asks them to forgive him.

#### What
1. **Letters** `js/text/en/lore-mote.js`: Mote's letters 1-12 copied **verbatim** from
   [lore#mote-letter-1](../bible/lore.md#mote-letter-1) … [lore#mote-letter-12](../bible/lore.md#mote-letter-12) (each
   ≤ 640 characters, lore decision 28), with their night headings ("Night 1" … "Night 36,447"). Ids
   `lore.mote.<n>.head`, `.body`. Card: the `letter` frame on `moonmere_paper` (lilac-silver `#e6e3f4`), ink `#3b4f9a`,
   hand `hand_mote` (a CSS style over system fonts: quick, slanted forward 10°, every number underlined; no font file).
2. **In the lanterns** (lore#mote-letters "Where"): letter n sits in moon-lantern n. When a lantern is kindled (S-218a)
   it opens and a folded paper boat (`prop_moonmere_letter`) sits on its base plate: `[E] Read · Letter` shows the
   card and sets `moteLetter:<n>`. Within 6 m of a lantern whose letter is unread: one soft high bell with a shimmer
   (`sfx.letterBell`), the FP cue for an off-screen find.
3. **Quest** `ss-47` (`n: 147`, owner none: the journal credits "Mote"; unlock `flag:stone:bell`; reward 30
   gems "found tucked in the last letter, for the post": paid when the 12th letter is read, whatever order):
   - `moonpath` "Walk the moonpath": done on `realm:moonmere.visited` (a player who already visited completes it at
     once). Hints (`ht.ss47.moonpath.nudge` / `.answer`): "The moon lays a path for those who call." / "At night, stand at the end of Nell's Jetty and play the Calling (mid, high, mid) with {tool}."
   - `garden` "Find the Mirror Garden": walk within 12 m of the Moon Tree trunk.
   - `letters` "Read the twelve unsent letters (n/12)": `count:moteLetter>=12` (the count of `moteLetter:*` flags).
   - `tree` "Sit under the Moon Tree": `[E] Sit · Moon Tree` at the trunk after all twelve; 15 s scene `ss47-tree`
     (`replay: true`): the silver leaves chime (a soft arpeggio of Mote's falling 5-1-3 motif); a single star crosses
     Moonmere's sky north to south. No lines (Mote is never met here). Done.
   - Journal: open "Moonmere is the isle, reflected, by someone who missed it. I do not know how I know that. I know
     it." / done "Twelve letters. I shall not quote them. Some things are not for Field Notes." Hoot's journal-only
     line `hoot.ss47` "Twelve letters to his sister, all unsent. I have written many unsent letters. Mine were to
     publishers. His are better."
4. **Lore tab**: Field Notes → Lore → **Mote's Letters** (12 slots by night order; unread show the slot number). Read
   letters replay from there. Spoiler class B (lore#spoiler-ladder): the realm opens only after `stone:bell`, which
   is after `moteNamed`, so the letters may say "Mote" and "Wenna".
5. **World mark (home)**: after `ss47.done`, a silver lantern buoy glows each night at the start of the moonpath off
   Nell's Jetty (a record appended to row 5 `placeJetty`, no draws; `glow` 0.8 `#f6f3d8`, 21:00-05:00).
6. **Unhide** `REALMS.moonmere.hidden = false`. Ask the Tree: when the player has `stone:bell` and has never entered
   Moonmere, the "nothing active" answer may name it: "The moon has a path, little sprout. Ask Nell's jetty at night."
   (`rd.ask.moonmere`, in Wenna's voice, only from `wennaAwake`).
7. **Signposts** (side-content#ss-47; Moonmere was reachable only by a hidden verb, so three things point at it):
   - **Pelagia's third stone-give line.** In `js/content/scenes/glassway.js` scene `stone-give-bell` (S-211), insert
     after step 4 (`gw.pelagia.s3`) a step: Pelagia `gw.pelagia.s4` "On a still night, play the Calling at the end of a jetty. The moon lays a path for those who call. Someone up there walks it." In `js/content/dialogue/glassway.js` add
     `glassway.pelagia.moonpath` (`npc: 'pelagia'`, `when: ['flag:stone:bell', '!realm:moonmere.visited']`,
     priority 45, lines `['gw.pelagia.s4']`), so a player who took the stone before this step shipped still hears it.
   - **Nell at the jetty.** `js/content/dialogue/home.js` entry `home.nell.moonpath` (`npc: 'nell'`, `when:
     ['flag:stone:bell', '!realm:moonmere.visited', 'tod:night']`, priority 45, line `home.npc.nell.moonpath`
     "Moon's on the water tonight. Lamplighters used to sing to it from here.").
   - **Step 0** `moonpath` of the quest above, with its two hints.
8. **Economy** +30.

#### Assets
- `moonmere_paper` (bundle `moonmere`): the lore prompt verbatim ("A single sheet of soft handmade paper pressed from
  cloud fibres, pale lilac-silver, faintly luminous, with a few tiny pressed star-flower petals caught in it near the
  edges and soft deckled edges, filling the whole frame, lit evenly from the front, no writing. Palette: #e6e3f4
  #c9c3e6 #f6f3d8 #8fa8ff."); crop 3:4, 768 × 1024, webp q80 (`tools/export_sprites.py --no-trim`), ≤ 150 KB; fallback
  `F-canvas` `#e6e3f4` with a 2 px `#3b4f9a` border. (art-audio's `ui_letter_moon` is this same asset; register only
  `moonmere_paper`.)
- `prop_moonmere_letter` (code-built, ≤ 40 tris): a folded paper boat `#e6e3f4` with a tiny star sticker; ART BRIEF per
  lore#brief-moonmere-letter.
- `buoy_silver` (code-built, ≤ 120 tris): a small silver buoy with a lantern top; fallback `cyl` + `sphere`.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all. Home prints byte-identical (the buoy is an appended record).
- `bun test`: the 12 letters equal lore.md word for word (a test reads the bible copy in `docs/roadmap/bible/lore.md`
  and compares, normalising whitespace); each ≤ 640 characters; tokens none; `~` and `!!` absent.
- In-page (`side`, case `moonmere-letters`): enter via the moonpath (debug `stone:bell`, night, Calling), kindle three
  lanterns in random order, read their letters (flags set, cards in Mote's hand), then set the rest by debug, the 30
  gems pay once, sit under the Moon Tree, the scene plays and skips cleanly; the Lore tab lists 12 letters.
- Signposts: with only `stone:bell` set, Field Notes lists ss-47 at step `moonpath` and Ask the Tree gives its nudge
  then answer; the `stone-give-bell` scene shows `gw.pelagia.s4` after `s3`; Pelagia and (at 22:00) Nell say their
  moonpath lines, and neither does after `realm:moonmere.visited`; by day Nell does not.
- `REALMS.moonmere.hidden === false`; the progression solver reaches every moon-lantern with `lantern & starsong &
  flag:stone:bell`.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test`; `node tests/inpage.mjs side`; `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`; `bun run test:smoke`;
  `node tools/build-pages.mjs && bun run test:pwa`.

#### Out of scope
- Mote himself (Act IV). Dormy's night stories (S-115, shipped).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: a player reaches Moonmere before `moteNamed` through an unusual stone order; impossible: `stone:bell` needs
`ch14`, which needs `act2done`, which comes after `moteNamed` (the solver asserts it). Rollback: revert re-hides the
realm; read-flags stay in the save.

<a id="s-220"></a>
### S-220 · feat: the Glassway's two Hollows and its Dash course
Milestone M25 · Depends on: S-205, S-073, S-112, S-074 · Size: M (about 320 lines) · Save: none · Flag: none ·
Bible: [dungeons#hollows](../bible/dungeons.md#hollows), [dungeons#h17](../bible/dungeons.md#h17), [dungeons#h18](../bible/dungeons.md#h18), [side-content#act-dash](../bible/side-content.md#act-dash),
[atlas-far#glassway-pois](../bible/atlas-far.md#glassway-pois), [systems#completion](../bible/systems.md#completion)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M25 · Glassway stories, the Merry Mabel and Moonmere**. The Glassway's last finds: a tide puzzle and a
song puzzle in two Hollows (a gem bag and a heart petal), and Clover's seventh Dash course, run across the sandbars at
low tide.

#### What
1. **Hollow 17 "The Puffin Hollow"** `hollow_glassway_1` (`js/realms/hollows/hollow_glassway_1.js`), door
   `hollow_glassway_tide` at Kelp Knoll's south cave mouth (−140, 178), shown at low tide only (the door's sill is
   under water at high tide; Look from the shore "A cave mouth, under the tide."). Map verbatim from
   [dungeons#h17](../bible/dungeons.md#h17) (11 × 11: gem bag `*` (1,1) in a 2-thick raft ring, push
   blocks (3,8) and (5,8), tide stone `Q` (9,7), lever `r` (1,9), exit `X` (5,10)). Solution: at low water push
   block (3,8) north ×4 to (3,4) and block (5,8) west ×2 then north ×2 to (3,6); ring the tide stone for high water;
   the blocks are stepping stones (hop, water gap, hop) to the rafts. Prize: **60-gem bag**. Hint pair verbatim.
2. **Hollow 18** `hollow_glassway_2`, displayed name **"The Drip Hollow"** (bible reconciliation R14: dungeons#hollows
   calls it "The Bell Hollow", which is already home's `bell_hollow` interior; the realm id is unchanged), door
   `hollow_glassway_bell` in Spiral Skerry's ruin ring (130, 165). Map verbatim from H18: drips (3,7), (5,7), (7,7)
   play **Tune D** = low, low, high every 6 s (heard, not door sources); bells low (2,5), mid (5,5), high (8,5) (the
   d7 pitch-bell entity); playing Tune D on the bells or on the Starsong within 6 tiles raises the hidden bridge `=`
   (5,2)-(5,3) to the **heart petal** `+` (5,1). Hint pair verbatim.
3. Both Hollows: `kind: 'hollow'`, grid 48 m, palette from the Glassway (`tex_chapel_stone` walls), no map, Chime Stone,
   keys or chests; `collect.hollow['hollow_glassway_1']`, `['hollow_glassway_2']` (realm ids, as S-071's `normalize`
   requires) and the H18 petal `collect.petal['hollow_glassway_2:p1']`; the solved door glows gold outside; Completion
   row "Hollows" for the Glassway = 2; regenerate `js/realms/stats.js`.
4. **Glassway Dash** (course 7, `collect.dash['dash:glassway']`, tenths of a second): start post at the Post Rock bench (−10, −200) →
   along sandbar **B1** → Tern Tuft (−165, −100) → along **B2** → finish at the Shell Stack (−232, 30); about 360 m;
   **10 rings** (2.4 m painted hoops, numbered), placed by the Glassway pass `placeSideMarks` (records, no draws) along
   that line every ~36 m at ground + 1.2 m. **Low tide only**: at high tide the start post says "The course is under
   the sea. Low tide, please!" (`clover.dash.tide`) and a run in progress when the tide is rung high ends with no
   result (rings on the bars sink with the water). The Tide Bell is on the fast line (one stone to ring first: Post
   Rock's (−30, −250)). Par `P` = the careful-bot time from the in-page test, recorded in the course data; medals gold
   ≤ 1.10 P, silver ≤ 1.30 P, bronze ≤ 1.60 P; first bronze 15 gems, first silver 20, first gold a ribbon on Clover's
   windowsill (ribbon colour for the Glassway `#7fd3d0`).
5. **Coverage**: rerun the Glassway coverage test in both tide states with the Hollow doors and Dash posts counted.

#### Assets
- Hollow walls reuse `tex_chapel_stone` (S-209). Dash rings and posts: the shared S-112 kit (no new asset).
- Drip basins reuse `int_drip_basin` (S-210).

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except "Frozen home island". Glassway prints equal.
- `bun test`: both Hollow maps equal the bible (map test); the solver proves each prize reachable (H17 through the
  tide states and blocks; H18 through Tune D) and no soft-lock (the lever resets H17's blocks).
- In-page (`dungeons`, cases `hollow_glassway_1/2`; `activities`, case `dash-glassway`): solve both; the bot runs the
  Dash at low tide and records `P`; at high tide the post refuses.
- Completion: the Glassway's Hollows row shows 2; heart petal count rises by one.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test`; `node tests/inpage.mjs dungeons`; `node tests/inpage.mjs activities`; `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope
- Kettle's Hollows and Dash (S-234).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: the Dash bot cannot finish at the recorded par when a Gullgrab interferes; timed runs pause the spawner (spawn
cap 0, side-content §6). Rollback: revert; hollow flags in a save are ignored.

<a id="s-220b"></a>

### S-220b · feat: the Glassway's Hollows get their second stage, and The Bell Hollow becomes a Deep Hollow

Milestone M25 · Depends on: S-220, S-090b · Size: M (200-500; mostly map data) · Save: none · Flag: the land's Hollow realms keep the land's hidden flag · Bible: [../bible/dungeons.md#hollows](../bible/dungeons.md#hollows), [../bible/dungeons.md#h18](../bible/dungeons.md#h18), [../bible/lore.md#lamp-logs](../bible/lore.md#lamp-logs), [../bible/story.md#motes-traces](../bible/story.md#motes-traces)

Added by the playtime review (dungeons.md §7 and R15, CANON §11.1 A3; see [Index changes](#index-changes)). Filed after S-220.

#### Why
Roadmap item **M25 · Glassway stories, the Merry Mabel and Moonmere**. Every Hollow was a one-step room that played in 2-3 minutes and all 24 shared one
shape. Now every Hollow has two stages with the prize behind the second, and one Hollow per land is a three-room
**Deep Hollow** (15-20 min) that ends in a reading nook with a Lamp Log and a note from the Lamp Thief, so the
optional bucket of CANON §11 is real play and each land has one hidden place worth remembering.

#### What
1. **The reliquary** (if S-093b has not added it yet, this step does; later steps reuse it): entity `reliquary: { when:
   [...] }` on a Hollow's prize tile; a code-built cage of four curved roots `#6f5a44` with gold buds `#ffe9a8`, 1.0 m,
   ≤ 120 tris; it opens in 1.0 s with `chime_up` when its condition holds; its state lives in
   `S.dungeons['<hollow>'].stage` until the prize is taken, then is dropped (the shipped Hollow rule). The solver proves
   stage 1, then stage 2, then the prize.
2. **Stage 2 for H17 The Puffin Hollow** (copy each Hollow's **Stage 2** line from [dungeons.md#hollows](../bible/dungeons.md#hollows)):
   - **H17 The Puffin Hollow** (`hollow_glassway_1`): the lid hangs from a hookbloom `h` on the far wall at (9,1): hook it from the raft ring; condition `hooked`.
3. **The Deep Hollow H18 The Bell Hollow** (`hollow_glassway_2`): replace its one-room data with the 31 × 11 composite map
   from [dungeons.md#h18](../bible/dungeons.md#h18) (copy, never paraphrase; realm grid 78 m). Room 1 is the old
   puzzle (stage 1) with a door (10,1) where its prize stood. **Room 2 The Tide Chimes**: (its own tide, starts high) ring tide stone (11,2) for low water: three drips (14,2), (16,2), (18,2) land on dry stones and play **Tune F** = high, mid, low; play it on the Starsong (the bells (12,8), (15,8), (18,8) are across the pit): the bridge (15,4)-(15,6) rises; exit door (20,9). **Room 3 The Reading
   Nook**: the prize on its pedestal (25,1); **Lamp Log 21** on a page pinned to stone (23,3) (`Read · Lamp Log`;
   it moves here from its atlas slot in the Glassway: edit that land's finds data so the slot is skipped, update the
   atlas row and run `node tools/realm-stats.mjs`; land and game totals unchanged); a scrap jam-jar lamp (`prop_scrap_lamp`
   from S-090b, or its fallback) holding a folded note (27,3), `[E] Read · Note`: "The drips sang the old tune. I sang it too. Badly. — M" (text id
   `lore.hollow.glassway`, ≤ 220 characters; it never names him, the charter spoiler rule; reading it adds the Lore
   entry "A note in a Hollow" and sets `trace:hollow:glassway`; if story.md's traces define this land's note, its
   text wins); and its own exit `X` (25,10) back to the spiral door.
4. **Hints** (Ask the Tree): one pair per stage-2 line (nudge = the condition's object, plain = the line), and for the
   Deep Hollow's room 2: "The drips only sing on dry stone." / "Ring the tide stone for low water, listen to the drips (high, mid, low) and play it on the Starsong.".
5. `STATS` is regenerated (`node tools/realm-stats.mjs`): same Hollow ids, same prizes, the log's new position.

#### Assets
- `int_reliquary` (code-built as above; ART BRIEF: a small cage of four curved roots with gold buds over a stone
  pedestal; 1.0 m; palette `#6f5a44` `#8a5a3c` `#ffe9a8`; toon bark; 120 / 500 tris; it opens like a flower; image prompt
  "<style line> a small cage of four curved tree roots with tiny gold buds over a mossy stone pedestal, cosy"; 3D prompt
  "stylised low-poly root cage over a stone pedestal, hand-painted, under 500 triangles, 1.0 m"). Fallback: four bent
  cylinders. The Deep Hollow reuses the land's Hollow kit and the scrap lamp; no new images.

#### Acceptance criteria
- `tests/dungeon-maps.test.js` passes with the edited Hollow blocks (16 `+` and 8 `*` still counted across the Hollows);
  data equals the bible blocks.
- The solver proves each Hollow's two stages and the Deep Hollow's three rooms in order, with no soft-lock, using only
  the tools listed in the Hollow table.
- In page: a bot solves each stage-2 Hollow and the Deep Hollow; reading the note sets `trace:hollow:glassway`; the
  Lamp Log counts once; prizes and `collect.hollow` keys are unchanged.
- Content lint (≤ 220 characters, no spoiler name). Invariants: keep *Frozen realms* (Hollow realms only; the land's
  overworld prints are untouched except the log slot, refreshed in this PR before the land is unhidden or, if it is
  already unhidden, by a finds-data change that the prints test lists), *One save slot*, *Tone*, *Assets*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `agentctl heavy --job <job> -- bun test tests/dungeon-maps.test.js tests/stats.test.js tests/content.test.js`.
- `agentctl heavy --job <job> -- node tests/inpage.mjs dungeons` (new case `hollows_glassway`).

#### Out of scope
- New prizes or Hollows; the land's other finds.

#### Risk & rollback
Data changes inside Hollow realms plus one log slot. Revert the PR: the one-stage Hollows and the log's atlas slot come
back.

<a id="s-222"></a>
### S-222 · feat: Kettle Isle cone recipe: the teakettle volcano and spring terraces (hidden)
Milestone M26 · Depends on: S-138 · Size: M (about 470 lines) · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.kettle.hidden = true`
(until S-232a) · Bible: [atlas-far#kettle](../bible/atlas-far.md#kettle) (Card, Shape and recipe, Regions, Arrival,
Named anchors), [atlas-far#far-passes](../bible/atlas-far.md#far-passes), [art-audio#palettes](../bible/art-audio.md#palettes),
[systems#realm-registry](../bible/systems.md#realm-registry)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M26 · Kettle Isle: the forge, the Belly and Starsteel**. The second Stair Stone place is a warm
volcano shaped like a teakettle: a cone with a spout ridge, a rock-arch handle, bath terraces and a forge town. This
step builds its ground and regions so every later M26 step has a frozen world to place things in.

#### What
1. **Registry** (`js/realms/index.js`):
   ```js
   kettle: { id: 'kettle', name: 'Kettle Isle', kind: 'overworld', size: 512, seed: 40261, recipe: 'cone',
     palette: 'kettle', music: 'kettle', ambience: 'kettle_forge', hidden: true, open: 'flag:ch14', arrive: 'jetty',
     tod: null, bundle: 'kettle', enemies: 'kettle', dash: true, benches: 5, rootgate: true, views: [ /* below */ ] }
   ```
2. **Recipe `js/realms/recipes/cone.js`** (`class ConeWorld extends RealmWorld`), stage order `island → cone → spout →
   switchbacks → terraces → forgefall → stream → ashbeach → teacup → tubes → regions → scatter → trails → reach → splat
   → colliders → anchors`; streams `new RNG(seed * k + c)` with the home constants (7·3, 13·5, 17·3, 31·17) so far
   post-passes keep 71·3 and up. Numbers from [atlas-far#kettle-shape](../bible/atlas-far.md#kettle-shape):

   | Piece | Build |
   |---|---|
   | Island | ellipse centre (0, 0), rx 205, rz 185; coastline radial noise ±12 % (wavelength 70 m); beach ring 10 m sloping y 0.4 → −1.6; base land y 2-4 (value noise amp 1, wavelength 50 m); land ≈ 0.12 km² |
   | **The Kettle** | cone centre (20, −10), base r 115: `h = 3 + 42 · (1 − r/115)^1.8` (summit y 45); cells with `h > 30` are **not walkable** except the spout-ridge path band and the lid; the **lid** is a flat disc r 12 at y 45 with a 1 m rim (unreachable on foot; reached only through the Belly; `W.unreachable` may contain exactly the lid) |
   | **The Spout** | a ridge from the cone's east flank (46, −10) y 32 east to **the Spout Mouth** (115, −15) y 28: ridge top 9 m wide, flanks blending to the cone; the Mouth a round hollow 10 m across, floor y 26 (steam column origin) |
   | **Switchbacks** | a 5 m path band carved into the north-east flank (walkable, slope ≤ 0.45): (130, −30) y 2 → (85, −60) y 8 → (110, −45) y 14 → (70, −45) y 20 → (95, −30) y 26 → joins the ridge at (80, −12) y 30.5; then along the ridge west to (50, −10) y 32 and east to the Mouth. Alcove `c09` shelf at the third leg (100, −50). Spur 4 m wide from the ridge (88, −8) down to the Belly mouth (90, 5) y 24 |
   | **Spoon Point** | a basalt crag at (162, 92), top disc r 4 at y 26, with a 3 m ledge ramp from (128, 48) y 4 winding up its west side (bible reconciliation R18: atlas-far puts it at (150, 70) "on the spout ridge's south flank", 57 m from the Teacup, beyond a glide from y 26; here it is 23 m from the Teacup rim, within the 30 m a 10 m drop glides) |
   | **Bathhouse Terraces** | five flat terraces cut into the south flank across bearings 150°-230° from the cone centre, tops y 6, 10, 14, 18, 22, each 10 m deep; stone stairs 3 m wide at bearings 165° and 215°; spring basins (flat pools 0.5 m deep, wadeable, `wadeK` 0.62) at (−55, 115), (−20, 105), (10, 100), (35, 100), (55, 108) and one per terrace end |
   | **Forgefall** | a west-coast shelf, floor y 4-8, centre (−150, −40), r ≈ 45 |
   | **Stream** | spline (−40, 70) → (−110, −20) → (−120, −55), 4 m wide, 0.4 m deep (wadeable), falls 8 m over **the Forge Ledge** at (−120, −55) (a cliff step with a walkable ledge top) and runs to the sea at (−200, −60) |
   | **Ash Beach** | black sand on the east and south-east coast (bearings 40°-150° from the island centre), y 0.4-3, tide pools (flat 0.3 m water discs); a rock causeway (top y 0.4, 4 m wide) from (150, 90) to **the Teacup** stack at (185, 115) (the stack itself is a prop in S-223b; reserve a disc r 7 here) |
   | **The Handle** | reserve the arch footprint west of the cone, (−85, −45) to (−85, 25): the underpass (−85, −10) is walkable ground y 6-8; the arch itself is a prop (S-223b) |
   | **Lava tubes** | three `over`-ceiling tunnels (the shipped overhead mechanism): (1) the Belly mouth at (90, 5) y 24 (a 6 m-deep alcove ending at the dungeon door); (2) the Sling Hollow's mouth on Ash Beach (130, 140) (a 6 m alcove ending at the Hollow door); (3) a **shoulder tube** from the top terrace (40, 60) y 22 to the switchbacks (75, 0) y 26: a 4 m trench path with an `over` roof collider 3.5 m above the floor and a dark roof card; dark inside (Lantern) |
   | Jetty | west shore (−205, −20), facing 247°, a `deck` 2.4 × 18 m |
   | Water | plane `max(1000, 512 + 600)` square, deep `#2a6a78`, shallow `#8fc7a0`, foam `#f8f2ea`; no tide |

3. **Regions** (REGIONS rows; cards exactly):

   | id | Card line 1 / line 2 | Extent rule | Landmark anchor | `max` | Spawn (inert until S-226; final rows in a comment) |
   |---|---|---|---|---|---|
   | `forgefall` | Forgefall / Mind the sparks | x < −70, and the jetty | the Hammerwheel (−125, −65) | 0 | none (town) |
   | `terraces` | Bathhouse Terraces / Towels provided | bearings 150°-250° from (20, −10), y < 24, plus the south coast | Soakley's Bathhouse (−10, 95) | 4 | `ash_moth 2` + placed `steam_puff` × 5 |
   | `ashbeach` | Ash Beach / Warm sand, warmer feet | bearings 40°-150°, y < 12, plus the Teacup | the Teacup (185, 115) | 6 | `soot_snuffle 4, ash_moth 2` |
   | `spout` | The Spout / Still a little steamy | everything else | the Kettle (20, −10) | 6 | `soot_snuffle 3, ash_moth 3` + placed `steam_puff` × 4 |

4. **Reach**: BFS from `jetty`; every walkable cell reachable on foot except the lid (and the Spoon Point top, which is
   reachable by its ramp: assert it). `W.unreachable` ⊆ the lid disc.
5. **Anchors and spawns** ([atlas-far#kettle-anchors](../bible/atlas-far.md#kettle-anchors)): spawns `jetty` (−195, −20)
   facing 67°, `belly` (90, 12), `lid` (20, −10) y 45, `gate` (−78, −4); named anchors `w:kettle` (20, −10),
   `w:kettle.lid` y 45, `w:spoutMouth` (115, −15) y 28, `w:hammerwheel` (−125, −65), `w:forge` (−152, −50),
   `w:bathhouse` (−10, 95), `w:teacup` (185, 115), `w:bellyMouth` (90, 5), `w:handle` (−85, −10) y 26, `w:spoonPoint`
   (162, 92) y 26.
6. **Palette `kettle`** (art-audio#palettes): swatch `#3b3540 #f4d1a6 #e66f4f #8fc7a0`; sky day `#74a6d4`/`#f4e2cc`,
   gold `#ffcf9a`, dusk `#6a5a9c`/`#ff9a7a`, night `#221e30`/`#4a3448`; clouds `#f6f0ea` / `#ffc0a8` / `#6a5a84`; water
   as above; fog per region `forgefall [26, 215, #e8d8cc, .22]`, `terraces [24, 210, #f2ece6, .35]`, `ashbeach [32,
   240, #e6dcd2, .18]`, `spout [22, 200, #ece4e0, .30]`; terrain layers `kettle_fernturf, kettle_ashwood, kettle_heath,
   kettle_blacksand, kettle_ash, kettle_cinder, kettle_tuff`, cliff `kettle_cliff` (all fall back to the home layer in
   the same slot until S-224). Splat: Ash Beach → blacksand; terraces → cinder paths + fernturf; the cone above y 24 →
   ash and tuff; Forgefall → cinder; the north coast → heath.
7. **The buoy**: the Glassway's `glassway:buoy-kettle` (308, −150) portal record (systems#portal-table row
   `glassway:buoy_kettle`, "Sail · Harbour buoy", `needs: 'flag:ch14'`) → `kettle` · `jetty`, card
   `card_sail_far_kettle`; while `kettle` is hidden the buoy says "Nell won't risk the fog yet." (the hidden-realm
   rule). The chart shows Kettle Isle as a fog bank until S-232a.
8. **Views**: densest `{x: -20, z: 112, yaw: 80, pitch: 4}` (the terraces east along the basins); landmark `{x: 60,
   z: 45, yaw: 300, pitch: 14}` (the Kettle and its spout); town `{x: -185, z: -25, yaw: 70, pitch: 0}` (Forgefall from
   the jetty); widest `{x: 112, z: -15, yaw: 220, pitch: -6}` (from the Spout Mouth over the isle).
9. **Prints** `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/kettle.json` (heights FNV, regions, land m², walk count, reach count,
   expected coverage land cells 80-92). S-223a-S-228 may refresh it; frozen after S-232a.

#### Assets
None (terrain falls back to home layers; S-224 generates the real ones).

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except "Frozen home island" and the precache line.
- `generateRealm('kettle')` is deterministic and equals the fixture; land 0.11-0.13 km²; `W.unreachable` ⊆ the lid;
  every spawn and anchor walkable (the lid spawn is on the lid disc); the switchbacks' slope ≤ 0.45 everywhere; the
  shoulder tube's `over` roof at the listed height; the five basins are wadeable water 0.5 m deep.
- `W.genMs` ≤ 1.2 s desktop; realm suite: no leak; 4 views within budget in both cameras.
- `REALMS.kettle.hidden === true`; the buoy refuses with the fog line.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test tests/realms/kettle.test.js` (new: determinism, prints, land area, reachability, the
  unreachable set, sample heights (summit 45 ± 0.5, Mouth floor 26, terraces 6/10/14/18/22 ± 0.2), region samples);
  `bun test`; `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`.

#### Out of scope
- Props and landmarks (S-223a/b), art and music (S-224), people (S-225), enemies (S-226), finds (S-228).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: carving a smooth switchback band into a steep cone makes stair-step artefacts; the band is graded along its
spline before the slope check. Rollback: revert; hidden realm.

<a id="s-223a"></a>
### S-223a · feat: Kettle Isle prop kit: forge houses, basins, vents, pipes and the shared pieces
Milestone M26 · Depends on: S-222, S-082 · Size: M (about 420 lines) · Save: none · Flag: realm still hidden ·
Bible: [atlas-far#kettle-kit](../bible/atlas-far.md#kettle-kit), [atlas-far#kettle-states](../bible/atlas-far.md#kettle-states),
[atlas-isles#shared](../bible/atlas-isles.md#shared), [art-audio#kettle](../bible/art-audio.md#kettle)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M26 · Kettle Isle: the forge, the Belly and Starsteel**. The isle's small furniture: a forge town that
is dark until the stone comes out, bath basins that swing between cold and scalding, steam vents and copper pipes.
(Split from S-223: the five landmark meshes are S-223b.)

#### What
1. **Kit** `js/props-kettle.js` (code-built, toon materials; ≤ 24 instanced types per realm counting the shared ones;
   new material programs ≤ 6: basalt, black sand, copper, steam (soft additive), forge glow, wet tile):

   | Type | Build (tris) | States |
   |---|---|---|
   | `kettle_forge_house` | stone shed 7 × 6 × 6 m `#5a4a48`, roof `#c2563f`, wide chimney, open front, anvil, trough (500) | `dark` / `lit` (emissive hearth `#e66f4f`, spark particles) |
   | `kettle_basin_rim` | worn stone rim `#d8cfc0` round a water disc `#7fd3d0`, a step (120; scaled 4-8 m) | steam colour `cold` `#e8f4ff` / `warm` `#f6f0ea` / `hot` `#ffd9c0`, ripple speed |
   | `kettle_vent` | rock crack with pale crust `#f4d1a6` on `#3b3540` (60) | puffs steam every 2.4 s (the Steam Hop lift of S-237b reads this phase) |
   | `kettle_brazier` | iron brazier on three legs, spiral bowl (120) | `cold` ash / `lit` flame sprite; use the shared cold-brazier gate model if S-082/atlas-isles defined one |
   | `kettle_pipe` | 2 m copper pipe segment `#8fc7a0` (40) | - |
   | `kettle_towel_line` | rope between posts, three towels `#f4d1a6` `#e66f4f` `#8fc7a0` (80) | sway |
   | `kettle_black_rock` | rounded black rocks 1-3 m (60) | - |
   | `kettle_fern` | curling fern 1 m (80) | curls when its nearest vent is not puffing |
   | `kettle_tube_mouth` | stone hood 6 m wide, dark inside (300) | - |
   | `kettle_sword_rack` | rack of six plain swords 1.5 m (120) | - |
   | `kettle_deckchair` | striped deck chair 1 m (80) | - |

   Shared kit pieces used here (S-082): bench, root-gate knot, Hollow door, vista post, lectern, soft-earth mound,
   hookbloom, ivy lattice, thornglass, bottle.
2. **Placement** (realm pass `placeProps` inside the recipe's `scatter` stage on its own stream `new RNG(40261 * 37 +
   13)`, which prints include; this is still before the realm is frozen): Forgefall's row of four forge houses along
   (−170, −55) → (−140, −30) facing the stream; the tea stall at (−130, −30); sword racks at (−158, −60) and (−146,
   −62); towel lines on terraces 1, 3 and 5; basin rims on the five ss-40 basins and one per terrace end (8 in all);
   eight Steam Hop vents at (−50, 110), (−35, 100), (−20, 92), (−5, 86), (10, 82), (25, 80), (40, 84), (52, 92) plus
   twelve dressing vents on the spout flank and Ash Beach; copper pipe runs from the terraces to the bathhouse site;
   three deck chairs on Ash Beach (140, 80), (150, 70), (160, 60); black rocks and ferns by scatter density (Ash Beach
   rocks 1 per 150 m², ferns on the spout flank 1 per 60 m² in cells within 10 m of a vent). Tube mouths on the three
   lava tubes of S-222.
3. **Unsettled state** (before `stoneHeld:kettle`, atlas-far#kettle-states): forge houses `dark`, no sparks; basins
   swing `cold` ↔ `hot` every 40 s (steam colour and ripple speed lerp over 3 s); the Kettle grumbles every 20-40 s (a
   low rumble `sfx.kettleGrumble` and a dust-fall particle burst on the switchbacks, cosmetic). The settled state (warm
   basins, lit forges with clinks) is a flag-driven state switch that S-232a turns on with `stoneHeld:kettle`; build
   both states here and test them via `G.debug.flags.set('stoneHeld:kettle', true)`.

#### Assets
- All kit pieces code-built per the table; ART BRIEFs verbatim from [atlas-far#kettle-kit](../bible/atlas-far.md#kettle-kit)
  (`kettle_forge_house`, `kettle_basin_rim`, `kettle_vent`, `kettle_brazier`, and the shared small-props sheet).
- One generated props reference sheet (docs only, `docs/art/ref/kettle-props.png`, not shipped): preamble + "volcanic
  spa island props: copper pipes, a towel line, black rocks, curled ferns, a lava-tube mouth, a sword rack, a striped
  deck chair, isolated" (atlas-far). Logged in `docs/art/prompts.md`.
- `kettle_card_steam` (6-frame strip 512×1024 webp ≤ 90 KB, realm `kettle`): preamble + `card` + "a soft column of
  white steam rising and curling, six frames"; `process_strip.py`; fallback white soft quads. Used by vents, basins
  and chimneys.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except "Frozen home island".
- `bun test`: prints refreshed once (this PR); ≤ 24 instanced types; ≤ 12 new material programs; Steam Hop vent
  positions equal the atlas list.
- In-page: the town view ≤ 200 main draw calls and ≤ 1.3 M tris in both cameras with forges lit; basins cycle cold/hot
  every 40 s unsettled and stay warm settled; no prop blocks the switchbacks or a basin (reachability test).

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test`; `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`; `node tools/build-pages.mjs && bun run test:pwa`.

#### Out of scope
- The Kettle's spout and plume, the Handle, the Hammerwheel, the bathhouse, the Teacup (S-223b).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: steam sprites over many basins cost fill-rate; steam uses one shared instanced card per kind and is culled
beyond 120 m. Rollback: revert; hidden realm.

<a id="s-223b"></a>
### S-223b · feat: Kettle Isle landmarks: the Kettle's spout and plume, the Handle, the Hammerwheel, the bathhouse and the Teacup
Milestone M26 · Depends on: S-223a · Size: M (about 380 lines) · Save: none · Flag: realm still hidden ·
Bible: [atlas-far#kettle-kit](../bible/atlas-far.md#kettle-kit), [atlas-far#kettle-regions](../bible/atlas-far.md#kettle-regions),
[atlas-far#kettle-states](../bible/atlas-far.md#kettle-states)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M26 · Kettle Isle: the forge, the Belly and Starsteel**. One landmark per region, each readable from
its region's entry in first person: the Kettle's plume, the Hammerwheel, the copper-domed bathhouse and the Teacup.

#### What
1. **Single meshes** (code-built, ART BRIEFs verbatim from atlas-far#kettle-kit; tris = code budget):
   - `kettle_spout` (600): a thick round stone lip at the Spout Mouth (115, −15), 10 m across, soot-dark inside
     `#2a2228`, basalt `#3b3540`; a **plume** billboard 8 × 40 m (uv scroll 0.3/s) rising from the Mouth. Unsettled:
     the plume puffs unevenly (scale noise 0.7-1.2). Settled (`stoneHeld:kettle`): steady; **the hourly whistle**: on
     every in-game hour a 2 s puff at 2× size and two notes (`sfx.kettleWhistle`, positional, heard across the isle,
     the track's last two notes); after `mainEnding` three notes. Collider: the lip as a ring (Pip can stand on its
     rim; c10 sits there).
   - `kettle_handle` (900): a smooth rock arch from (−85, −45) to (−85, 25), apex y 26, opening 18 m high and 20 m wide
     beneath (walkable underpass), ferns at its feet, spirals carved at the base (curio 12 sits there), a hookbloom on
     its crown (S-228's c11 and the vista use it); colliders: the two legs as capsules and a walkable `deck` along the
     top 3 m wide (reachable only by Hook; the solver proves it).
   - `kettle_hammerwheel` (1,200): a 14 m timber water wheel `#8a5a3c` at (−125, −65) under the Forge Ledge falls,
     its axle driving three trip hammers over anvils under a little roof; dark/still unsettled; settled: turns 6 rpm,
     hammers drop in turn (clang every 4 s, sparks; the anvil glow `#e66f4f`).
   - `kettle_bathhouse` (1,400): a round timber bathhouse `#c98b4b` 16 m across, green-copper dome `#8fc7a0` to 12 m at
     (−10, 95), wide eaves, roof steam vent, a bench out front; door at (−10, 85) (interior is S-236's if needed).
   - `kettle_teacup` (700): a squat basalt sea stack 16 m at (185, 115), top hollowed like a cup with seawater
     `#7fd3d0`, a rock loop "handle" on its seaward side, rim moss `#8fc7a0`; the rim is a walkable ring `deck` at
     y 16 (glided to from Spoon Point; S-228's c07, log-3 inside the hollow (a walkable floor at y 12 reached from the
     rim) and the vista).
2. **First-person reads**: each landmark's top is visible from its region entry (atlas-far#kettle-regions "Entry"):
   the realm suite's landmark view checks the plume's top is inside the frustum from the jetty view; the Teacup from
   the beach path (60, 120); the Hammerwheel from the jetty; the bathhouse dome from the Forgefall road (−70, 60).
3. Refresh `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/kettle.json` (props only; terrain unchanged).

#### Assets
- Five code-built landmarks above; ART BRIEFs verbatim from atlas-far (silhouettes, sizes, palettes, image prompts
  with the PREAMBLE, 3D prompts: "toon stone spout mouth 10 m, sooty inside", "toon rock arch 70 m span 26 m apex",
  "toon water wheel 14 m with three trip hammers", "toon round bathhouse 16 m wide, copper dome 12 m", "toon basalt sea
  stack 16 m shaped like a teacup"), each with a commented-out `models` slot `kettle_<name>`.
- `kettle_plume` sprite: preamble + `fx` + "a soft white steam plume sprite, transparent, 512 × 2048, 8-frame strip"
  (`process_strip.py`), ≤ 120 KB; fallback canvas soft plume.
- Concept reference (docs only): preamble + "a volcano shaped like a teakettle on a small island, a spout ridge letting
  off a gentle steam plume, a rock arch handle on its side, hot-spring terraces, black sand beach" → `docs/art/ref/`
  (also the loading card's reference in S-224).

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except "Frozen home island".
- `bun test`: the Handle top deck is unreachable on foot and reachable with the Hook model; the Teacup rim is reachable
  by a glide from Spoon Point (glide model 3 × drop − 1 m, atlas-home §1.1 rule 9) and not on foot; the Spout lip ring
  is reachable from the ridge path.
- In-page: the hourly whistle fires on the hour in the settled state only (debug clock); landmark views in budget
  (main ≤ 200, total ≤ 300 draw calls, ≤ 1.3 M tris) in both cameras.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test`; `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`; `node tools/build-pages.mjs && bun run test:pwa`.

#### Out of scope
- People (S-225), the Belly door's portal (S-229), finds on these landmarks (S-228).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: the 70 m arch mesh and its colliders crossing the stream's spline; the stream passes west of the legs (checked
by a test sampling water cells under the arch). Rollback: revert; hidden realm.

<a id="s-224"></a>
### S-224 · art: Kettle Isle look, loading card, map art and music
Milestone M26 · Depends on: S-222, S-029b · Size: M (about 260 lines of code and data, plus generated images) · Save: none ·
Flag: realm still hidden · Bible: [art-audio#kettle](../bible/art-audio.md#kettle), [art-audio#preamble](../bible/art-audio.md#preamble),
[art-audio#kinds](../bible/art-audio.md#kinds), [art-audio#pipeline](../bible/art-audio.md#pipeline),
[art-audio#bundles](../bible/art-audio.md#bundles), [art-audio#track-data](../bible/art-audio.md#track-data),
[art-audio#ambience](../bible/art-audio.md#ambience)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M26 · Kettle Isle: the forge, the Belly and Starsteel**. The isle's painted surfaces, its loading card
and map, and its brass-and-steam music: warm and steamy, never threatening (no lava glow, only ember-warm light).

#### What
1. **Terrain layers** (realm `kettle`, bundle `kettle`): eight seamless textures, each generated by a GPT Sol subagent
   or a Gemini model with the fixed preamble + `tile` kind + subject, processed `python3 -I tools/make_seamless.py <in>
   <out> --blend 64`, exported 1024 (hd ≤ 110 KB) and 512 (lo ≤ 40 KB) webp:
   `kettle_fernturf` "Short bright fern turf on warm volcanic soil. Palette: #7aa86a #9cc88a #8fc7a0 #3b3540." ·
   `kettle_ashwood` "Woodland floor of grey ash, fern fronds and small twigs. Palette: #8a8a88 #6a8a5a #c9b89a
   #3b3540." · `kettle_heath` "Low heath with tiny yellow and orange flowers on dark soil. Palette: #e7c45a #e66f4f
   #6a8a5a #3b3540." · `kettle_blacksand` "Smooth warm black sand with a few glossy pebbles. Palette: #3b3540 #4a4450
   #f4d1a6 #2a2430." · `kettle_ash` "Soft pale grey ash drifts with tiny glints. Palette: #c8c4c0 #e0dcd8 #a8a4a0
   #f4d1a6." · `kettle_cinder` "A path of rounded red cinder gravel. Palette: #b5674c #8a4a3a #e66f4f #3b3540." ·
   `kettle_tuff` "Rounded purple-brown volcanic tuff with small holes. Palette: #6a5060 #5a4450 #8a6a70 #3b3540." ·
   cliff `kettle_cliff` "A terraced warm volcanic cliff with soft orange and purple bands and fern ledges. Palette:
   #6a5060 #b5674c #8fc7a0 #3b3540." Fallback: the home layer in the same slot tinted by the swatch.
2. **Billboard cards**: `kettle_card_ground` (`sheet-items` N = 4, 1024×512: "a curled bright fern, an orange heath
   flower clump, a little yellow mineral crust, a round teal spring weed. Palette: #9cc88a #e66f4f #e7c45a #8fc7a0.";
   cells `fern`, `heathflower`, `sulphurpuff`, `kettleweed`; `slice_sheet.py --parts 4`), `kettle_card_sparks` (256²
   4-frame strip "a small burst of warm orange sparks, four frames"), `kettle_card_bathbubbles` (128² alpha "a few
   round soft bubbles"), `kettle_card_ember` (128² alpha "a small round glowing ember seed with a soft orange glow",
   also the Sling projectile of S-227a). (`kettle_card_steam` shipped in S-223a.)
3. **Loading card** `load_kettle` (1600×900 ≤ 200 KB + 960×540 lo): preamble + `loading` + "A round volcanic island
   shaped like a teakettle with a spout-shaped peak puffing soft steam, terraced hot-spring baths with towels drying, a
   little forge town with glowing windows, warm black sand. Palette: #3b3540 #f4d1a6 #e66f4f #8fc7a0." **Map**
   `map_kettle` (1024² ≤ 200 KB): preamble + `map` + "A round volcanic cone island with a spout peak, terraced baths on
   one flank, a forge town, black sand beaches and lava-tube cave mouths. Palette: #3b3540 #f4d1a6 #e66f4f #8fc7a0."
   Fallbacks: canvas card in the swatch; the map card's canvas renderer.
4. **Sail card** `card_sail_far_kettle` (if S-200's far-sail card set did not ship it): preamble + `loading` + "the
   little Dandelion sailboat crossing clear turquoise water toward a far volcano shaped like a teakettle with a soft
   steam plume, buoys along the way".
5. **Music** `kettle` (`js/audio.js` track table; data verbatim from [art-audio#track-data](../bible/art-audio.md#track-data)
   `kettle: { bpm: 104, … }`, F major, lead `pluck`, lead2 `brass` at layer 1, the bass/comp/perc rows and the 16-bar
   melody). Layers per [atlas-far#kettle-music](../bible/atlas-far.md#kettle-music): `forge` (anvil clinks beats 2 and 4,
   only after the forges relight), `bath`, `beach`, `spout`; before Fumarole is calmed every layer gets a slightly sour
   pitch wobble on long notes (± 15 cents LFO 0.3 Hz), removed when `stoneHeld:kettle` (the "dim" mechanism of
   art-audio decision 12: a missing voice and a wobble, never a grey palette). The whistle's two notes end each loop
   when settled. If the `brass` voice is not shipped yet, add it from art-audio#voices' recipe.
6. **Ambience**: `kettle_forge` `{ drone: [70, 0.03, 320], wind: [500, 0.6, 0.03, 0.02], ev: [['ambAnvil', 0.08, 0],
   ['ambSteam', 0.1, -1]], dim: { ev: 0.4 } }` (Forgefall), `kettle_baths` `{ drone: [58, 0.04, 260], ev:
   [['ambSteam', 0.33, -1], ['ambBubble', 0.25, -1]] }` (terraces, the Spout), `kettle_coast` `{ waves: [500, 0.11],
   wind: [550, 0.7, 0.04, 0.03], ev: [['ambGull', 0.06, 0], ['ambSteam', 0.05, -1]] }` (Ash Beach).
7. **Horizon card** (home): `horizon_isle_e` at 65° ([art-audio#horizon](../bible/art-audio.md#horizon)) shipped with
   home's horizon cards; this step only swaps its steam plume to the generated `kettle_plume` sprite if the card used a canvas plume.

#### Assets
As listed (all generated with logged prompts; raw generations never committed). Bundle `kettle` ≤ 2.5 MB without GLBs
(budget row ≈ 2,435 KB: terrain 1,050, cards 5 × 90, portraits 4 × 55, beast cards 4 × 35, loading 260, map 180,
sketches 3 × 45).

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except "Frozen home island". `?noassets=all` renders Kettle Isle with fallbacks.
- `tests/assets.test.js`: every new key has a fallback and `realm: 'kettle'`; size limits hold; the bundle ≤ 2.5 MB.
- In-page: the track plays with the `forge` layer off before the stone and on after (debug flag); the sour wobble is
  present unsettled and absent settled.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test`; `node tools/build-pages.mjs && bun run test:pwa`; `node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (views
  rendered with textures; screenshots attached to the PR for the four views in both cameras).

#### Out of scope
- Portraits (S-225), bestiary cards (S-226), Fumarole's card (S-231).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: bundle over budget; drop one ground card or export terrain lo at q75 (charter#budgets-over order). Rollback:
revert; fallbacks render.

<a id="s-225"></a>
### S-225 · feat: Forgefall's people: Master Scoria, Pitch and Soakley
Milestone M26 · Depends on: S-223b, S-068a · Size: M (about 450 lines) · Save: none · Flag: realm still hidden ·
Bible: [cast#master-scoria](../bible/cast.md#master-scoria), [cast#pitch](../bible/cast.md#pitch),
[cast#soakley](../bible/cast.md#soakley), [atlas-far#kettle-people](../bible/atlas-far.md#kettle-people),
[atlas-far#kettle-interiors](../bible/atlas-far.md#kettle-interiors), [systems#portal-table](../bible/systems.md#portal-table),
[charter#reserved-markers](../bible/charter.md#reserved-markers)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M26 · Kettle Isle: the forge, the Belly and Starsteel**. Kettle Isle's three people carry its story: a
quiet smith whose forges are cold, his eager little apprentice, and a bath-keeper too calm to be alarmed by anything.

#### What
1. **Looks** (`js/content/looks.js` `NPC_LOOKS`, read by one `npcDef` case per rig family; add the `bear` and `newt`
   families if earlier steps did not, per [cast#rig-families](../bible/cast.md#rig-families)):
   - `scoria`: rig `bear`, h 1.9; fur honey `#d9a441`, cream muzzle `#f4d1a6`, nose `#3a2a20`, leather apron `#6b4a2e`,
     goggles up on the brow `#c99a4a` with smoked lenses `#3b3540`, hammer head `#8f97a3` in the right paw, soot smudge
     on the nose.
   - `pitch`: rig `newt`, h 0.5; skin `#e66f4f`, yellow spots `#f2c94c`, belly `#f4d1a6`, oversized green-lensed goggles
     `#8fc7a0`, mini apron `#6b4a2e`, soot `#3b3540`, tongs taller than she is.
   - `soakley`: rig `rodent`, `tail: none`, body lathe × 1.4 wide, square snout `rbox`; fur `#9b7a56`, muzzle
     `#7a5a3e`, folded towel on the head `#ffffff` with a teal stripe `#8fc7a0`, robe `#f4d1a6`, half-closed eyes.
   - Two forge hands `kettle.smith-1/2` (bear rig recolours, h 1.6, names "Ferrick" and "Anvila"; they are minor and say
     one line each).
2. **Spots and schedules** (atlas-far#kettle-people, cast schedules): `kettle.scoria` (−152, −50) anvil 07:00-18:00,
   the tea stall (−130, −30) after (cast: `[[6,'kettle.forge_door','work'],[20,'kettle.forge_door','indoors']]` maps
   to these spots); `kettle.pitch` (−148, −46) beside him 07:00-19:00, the forge interior at night; `kettle.soakley`
   (−10, 82) at the bathhouse door all day, soaking in the great basin (10, 100) 18:00-21:00; smiths at (−160, −40),
   (−140, −58). Spots are a realm pass `placeSideMarks`-style record set on `new RNG(40261 * 107 + 19)` with no draws.
3. **The forge interior** `scoria_forge` (systems id; atlas-far `int_scoria_forge`), door (−155, −58), portal
   `kettle:forge` (`[E] Enter · The forge`, always), 64 m grid, two rooms: forge hall (anvil, hearth `t` lamps, Pitch's
   corner with a little shelf (M27's ss-39 uses it), a wall rack) and the cooling room (sword racks, the empty
   starsteel mould on the wall for ss-38). Tile map (ENGINE legend + the home interior letters):
   ```
   r1 forge hall          r2 cooling room (at [9, 0])
   ###w#w###              #######
   #t..O..t#              #O...O#
   #.......#              #.....#
   #...@...D              D.....#
   #O.....O#              #O...O#
   #.......#              #######
   ####X####
   ```
   Hearth glow and the anvil read `dark` until `stoneHeld:kettle`, `lit` after. Prints fixture for the interior.
4. **Dialogue** (`js/content/dialogue/kettle.js`, `js/text/en/ke.js`; voice tics: Scoria one-word verdicts and calls
   Pip "smallsmith"; Pitch "Hot hot hot!" and "Pip-pip"; Soakley "No rush." and "lovely"; reserve Pitch's "Hot hot
   hot!" and Soakley's "No rush." as their markers in the charter's reserved-markers table via the PR body). Priority 20
   idles and the first-meeting lines (the story's people-step lines are S-232a's):
   - Scoria: `ke.scoria.idle.1` "Cold forge. Cold hands. Cold tea. ...Hm." · `.idle.2` "Every sword Miso sells, I made.
     She says 'hand-picked'. I picked them up, so." · after stone `.idle.3` "Hot forge. Good." · `.idle.4` (night)
     "Tea. Strong. Sit."
   - Pitch: `ke.pitch.idle.1` "Hot hot hot! Well. Warm warm warm. The forge is sulking, Pip-pip." · `.idle.2` "One
     day I'll forge a star. A real one. Master says 'one day'. I say 'which day'." · after stone `.idle.3` "HOT hot
     hot! It's back! Stand back! Not that far back."
   - Soakley: `ke.soakley.idle.1` "The baths go cold, then scalding, then cold. No rush. It's lovely either way, if
     you're patient." · `.idle.2` "Towels provided. Hurry not provided." · after stone `.idle.3` "Warm, steady,
     lovely. I'd stand up and cheer, but I'm sitting down."
   - Smiths: `ke.smith1` "Ferrick. I hold things. Master hits them." · `ke.smith2` "Anvila. Mind the sparks. There
     aren't any. That's the problem."
5. **Miso supply hook**: a flag `kettleSupply` set on first talking to Scoria; Miso's home shop gains one line while
   it is set and ss-41 is not started: `miso.kettle.1` "Forgefall? Oh, I get my swords from a supplier there. Very
   ordinary supplier. Do NOT go and look~" (ss-41 in M27 picks it up).
6. **Portraits**: `portrait_scoria`, `portrait_pitch`, `portrait_soakley` (realm `kettle`).

#### Assets
- Portraits: preamble + `portrait` + the cast subjects verbatim (Scoria "A big, quiet honey-coloured bear blacksmith
  with a cream muzzle and soot on his nose, a scorched leather apron, brass goggles pushed up on his brow, a hammer in
  one paw, warm forge light on one side of his face."; Pitch "A tiny eager orange-red newt girl with yellow spots,
  oversized green-lensed welding goggles, a little leather apron, soot smudges on her cheeks, holding tongs taller than
  herself, beaming."; Soakley "A round, utterly calm capybara with half-closed contented eyes, a folded white towel with
  a teal stripe on his head, a cream bath robe, steam drifting past, the most relaxed smile in the world."); 512²
  webp ≤ 60 KB with alpha via `tools/export_sprites.py`; fallback canvas initial portraits.
- ART BRIEFs for the owner (batch 12, written in S-242): `npc_scoria` (≤ 6,000 tris, 3D "[3D preamble] a big
  honey-coloured bear blacksmith in a leather apron, goggles on his brow, holding a hammer, 1.9 m tall."), `npc_pitch`
  (≤ 2,500), `npc_soakley` (≤ 4,000), commented-out `models` slots now.
- Forge interior furniture from the shared interior kit (anvil, hearth, racks); the empty mould is a canvas decal.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except "Frozen home island". Forgefall spawns no enemies (town rule; S-226 asserts it too).
- `bun test`: looks validate per rig; content lint (lines ≤ 220, tics, `~` only in Miso's line); schedules resolve to
  walkable spots; the interior's prints.
- In-page (`realm`/`side`): talk to all five in the settled and unsettled states (debug flag), enter and leave the forge
  with no leak (≤ 60 draw calls inside), portraits show.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test`; `node tests/inpage.mjs side` (case `kettle-people`); `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`;
  `node tools/build-pages.mjs && bun run test:pwa`.

#### Out of scope
- The people steps of the main chain and their lines (S-232a); ss-38 (S-233a); ss-39-41 (M27); the bathhouse interior
  (with ss-40 in M27 if it needs one).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: a new rig family pushes the step past 500 lines; if both `bear` and `newt` are missing, ship `bear` here and
`newt` as S-225b (Pitch then uses the `rodent` rig with a newt look until it lands). Rollback: revert; hidden realm.
