<a id="s-226"></a>
### S-226 · feat: Kettle enemies: Soot Snuffle, Steam Puff and Ash Moth
Milestone M26 · Depends on: S-042, S-223b · Size: M (about 380 lines) · Save: none · Flag: realm still hidden ·
Bible: [creatures#enemy-soot-snuffle](../bible/creatures.md#enemy-soot-snuffle), [creatures#enemy-steam-puff](../bible/creatures.md#enemy-steam-puff),
[creatures#enemy-ash-moth](../bible/creatures.md#enemy-ash-moth), [creatures#spawn-tables](../bible/creatures.md#spawn-tables),
[atlas-far#kettle-enemies](../bible/atlas-far.md#kettle-enemies), [creatures#bestiary](../bible/creatures.md#bestiary)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M26 · Kettle Isle: the forge, the Belly and Starsteel**. Three tier-2 species that read the isle's
theme (soot, steam, ash) and each teach one twist on an archetype the player knows: a charger that turns once, a
turret that fires twice, a swooper whose line bends.

#### What
1. **Species** (`ENEMY[id]`, archetype modules of S-040-S-042; models `enemyDef('<id>')` in `js/models.js`, code-built):

   | id | Archetype | HP | r | Move | Tell (≥ 0.55 s, sound + body + ground) | Params | Gems |
   |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
   | `soot_snuffle` | charger, tier 2 | 6 | 0.46, ground, emerge `ground` (out of ash) | charge 7.5 m/s for 0.9 s, **turns once mid-charge up to 45°**; a wall or rock stops it sneezing for 1.2 s (gold) | snuffles, sparks fizz from its snout for **0.70 s**; stripe drawn; the new leg of the stripe is drawn **0.55 s** before the turn | `{ windup: 0.70, speed: 7.5, dur: 0.9, reach: 7.0, guard: false, bonkOpen: 1.2, tier: 2, turnMax: 45 }` | 2 |
   | `steam_puff` | turret, tier 2, stationary on a vent | 5 | 0.44, emerge `still` | two steam jets 0.35 s apart down the same stripe (moving puffs at 10 m/s); reload 1.2 s (gold, lid open) | its lid rattles and a coral stripe draws for **0.80 s** | `{ turn: 90, aim: 0.80, lock: 0.30, shot: 10, burst: 2, burstGap: 0.35, reload: 1.2, range: 12, tier: 2 }` | 2 |
   | `ash_moth` | swooper, tier 2 (as the Drip Bat: bending stripe) | 4 | 0.36, flying at 2.0 m, emerge `sky` | swoop 8.5 m/s for 0.55 s along a stripe that bends 35° | a dusty wing-rustle and its eyespots glow for **0.60 s** | `{ rise: 0.60, swoopSpeed: 8.5, swoopT: 0.55, reach: 5.3, alt: 2.0, tier: 2, bend: 35 }` | 2 |

   All damage 1 HP; poof colours `#d8d0c8` (snuffle, moth), `#f4f0ea` (puff). Ash Moths are wild animals: no clothes,
   no face mask, no lamp (they must never look like the Wicklings).
2. **Models** (code-built, ≤ 800 tris each): Soot Snuffle a round charcoal fluff ball `#3b3540` (fuzz shell), pink-orange
   snout `#e66f4f`, cream eye whites `#fff3d8`, pupils `#2a2440`, emissive spark flecks `#f4d1a6`; Steam Puff a small
   round cloud `#f4f0ea` shaped like a kettle with a lid knob `#8fc7a0` and rosy cheeks `#e66f4f`, sitting on a vent
   ring `#3b3540` (lid rattle and puff recoil by transform); Ash Moth a fat fuzzy grey body `#d8d0c8`, broad soft wings
   `#9a9098` with two orange eyespots `#e66f4f` (emissive on the tell), eyes `#2a2440`.
3. **Spawn tables** (`kettle`, verbatim from creatures#spawn-tables / atlas-far#kettle-enemies), replacing S-222's
   inert rows:

   | Region | Rolled | Placed | max |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | Forgefall | none (town) | - | 0 |
   | Bathhouse Terraces (outer terraces only) | `ash_moth 2` | `steam_puff` × 5 at (−70, 125), (65, 115), (−60, 80), (70, 95), (45, 60) | 4 |
   | Ash Beach | `soot_snuffle 4, ash_moth 2` (+3 `ash_moth` at night) | - | 6 |
   | The Spout | `soot_snuffle 3, ash_moth 3` | `steam_puff` × 4 at (60, −40), (85, −60), (100, 0), (40, −75), each ≥ 8 m off the switchback path's centre | 6 |

   Never spawn on the inner terraces (bearings 175°-205°), on the five ss-40 basins, on the Steam Hop vent line, within
   12 m of Forgefall's edge, or within 6 m of the Belly mouth. Placed Steam Puffs sit on vents and respawn on realm
   re-entry only.
4. **Bestiary** (cards realm `kettle`): Soot Snuffle — Hoot "Soot Snuffles sneeze sparks. Bless them." / Clover "It
   turns once! Watch the new line." · Steam Puff — Hoot "Steam Puffs are the Kettle's hiccups." / Clover "Two puffs,
   then it rests. Go!" · Ash Moth — Hoot "Ash Moths are no relation to the Wicklings, who would like that written
   down." / Clover "Its line bends. Step out late."
5. **Sound twins** (first person): a snuffle-and-fizz loop before the charge (positional, 15 m); a lid rattle for the
   Steam Puff's aim; a wing rustle for the moth's rise. Off-screen attacks within 10 m behind Pip get the shared
   edge-of-screen arrow (S-040's FP rule).

#### Assets
- Cards `card_soot_snuffle`, `card_steam_puff`, `card_ash_moth` (realm `kettle`): preamble + `card-beast` + the
  creatures card subjects verbatim ("a round fluffy charcoal-grey soot creature with a pink snout, sneezing a few tiny
  sparks, on warm black sand." / "a little round cloud shaped like a teakettle with a lid knob and rosy cheeks, sitting
  on a steaming vent, puffing out a jet of steam." / "a fat fuzzy grey moth with two soft orange eyespots on its wings,
  fluttering over warm ash with a volcano behind."); 384² webp ≤ 35 KB each (bundle budget row); canvas silhouette
  fallbacks.
- ART BRIEFs (owner batch 12) verbatim from creatures (silhouette, size 0.6 / 0.7 / 0.35 m body 0.9 m span, palettes,
  toon fuzz / soft / fuzz, animations, poly 800/2,500, 700/2,500, 700/2,500), each with a 3D prompt: "stylised low-poly
  fluffy soot-ball creature with a pink snout and spark flecks, under 2,500 triangles, 0.6 m"; "stylised low-poly
  little cloud shaped like a teakettle with a lid knob and rosy cheeks on a vent ring, under 2,500 triangles, 0.7 m";
  "stylised low-poly fat fuzzy grey moth with orange eyespots, wings spread, under 2,500 triangles, 0.9 m span".

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except "Frozen home island". Tone: every move's wind-up ≥ 0.55 s and damage 1 (the unit test
  over all `ENEMY` moves); Forgefall spawns nothing; the turn's second stripe leg is drawn ≥ 0.55 s before the turn.
- `bun test`: species defs validate against their archetype schemas; spawn tables parse; placed anchors are on vents
  and off the path by ≥ 8 m.
- In-page (`species`, one case per species): tell precedes damage by ≥ the listed time; poof; gold opening on a wall
  bonk (snuffle) and on reload (puff); the bend (moth).
- Kettle views with full spawns stay within budget in both cameras.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test`; `node tests/inpage.mjs species`; `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`;
  `node tools/build-pages.mjs && bun run test:pwa`.

#### Out of scope
- The Belly's waves (S-229, S-230 use these species).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: the mid-charge turn surprises players in first person; the second leg draws 0.55 s ahead and a sound cue plays
at the turn. Rollback: revert; Kettle spawns nothing.

<a id="s-227a"></a>
### S-227a · feat: the Ember Sling: ranged ember shots for bell targets, braziers, ice walls and wax plugs
Milestone M26 · Depends on: S-048, S-222 · Size: M (about 470 lines) · Save: none · Flag: none ·
Bible: [systems#items-tools](../bible/systems.md#items-tools), [systems#tool-sling](../bible/systems.md#tool-sling),
[systems#gates](../bible/systems.md#gates), [dungeons#entities](../bible/dungeons.md#entities), [systems#brief-tools](../bible/systems.md#brief-tools)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M26 · Kettle Isle: the forge, the Belly and Starsteel**. The eighth tool is the game's first aimed
shot: it reaches things a sword cannot (high bells, far valves, braziers on walls) and reopens finds in every earlier
land. (Split from S-227: the home bells, the Bell Hollow and the ice wall are S-227b.)

#### What
1. **Item and ability**: `sling: { name: 'Ember Sling', kind: 'tool', icon: 'sling', desc: 'Flings warm ember seeds.
   Lights braziers, melts wax and ice, rings bells.' }`; ability `sling`; tool-ring slot 7.
2. **Firing** `js/tools/sling.js`: **hold** `tool` to draw (**0.6 s**; 0.3 s with the `quick_draw` upgrade), release to
   fire; releasing early (under the draw time) cancels with no shot. While drawing Pip walks at **2.2 m/s** and cannot
   roll or swing. **Aim**: FP the crosshair; TP the view centre (a small reticle appears while drawing, charter
   decision 11: the Sling shows a reticle, not a pill); with lock-on (the shipped lock action), the shot aims at the
   locked target. **Projectile**: an ember seed sprite (`kettle_card_ember`, additive, one instanced draw for all live
   seeds) at 30 m/s with gravity 4 m/s² (a gentle arc), **range 25 m** (the seed fizzles out at 25 m path length),
   **cooldown 0.4 s**, endless seeds. It stops at the first collider, terrain cell or target. **Damage 1** to enemies
   (never staggers a boss unless the boss def has `slingOpen`), with an ember spark poof.
3. **Hits as events**: every impact emits `EV.EMBER {x, y, z, targetId}`; gate objects and dungeon entities subscribe:

   | Target | Rule | Look and sound |
   |---|---|---|
   | `bell_target` (gate) / `q` (dungeon) | one ember hit rings it for good (overworld: `gate:<realm>:<id>`); a dungeon `q` acts as a switch | brass bell on a 2 m post with a red ribbon; it swings 20° for 1.5 s and rings its pitch; ding-swings in the wind before (FP cue) |
   | `cold_brazier` (gate) / `a` (dungeon) | one ember hit lights it for good; the Lantern's Kindle within 1 tile also lights a dungeon `a` | iron bowl of grey ash "Cold as a toad." (Look); lit: flame sprite + warm `glow` (no point light) |
   | `ice_wall` (gate) / `A` (dungeon) | **3 ember hits**: each shrinks it a third with a steam puff; melts in **1.5 s** after the third, for good | blue glassy slab with frost feathers; creak within 10 m; "Brr. Something warm might help." (Swing/Look) |
   | `wax_plug` `f` (dungeon) | one ember hit melts it in **1.0 s**, for good | round creamy wax seal `#f4e3b8` with a stamped spiral, drips |
   | crystal switch `s` (dungeon, S-053) | an ember hit toggles it (as a sword hit) | - |
   | steam valve `v` (dungeon, S-229) | an ember hit turns it | - |

   Register `bell_target`, `cold_brazier`, `ice_wall` in `js/gates.js` with `needs: 'ability:sling'`; the solver gains
   `sling` (reach 25 m with line of sight).
4. **View-model**: FP: a Y-shaped wooden sling in Pip's left hand (bottom-left), the band stretching back while
   drawing; the ember glows in the pouch. TP: on the belt until drawn; drawn pose by transform (Pip's GLB untouched).
5. **Sandbox tests**: a test realm (S-022 sandbox) with one of each target at 10, 20 and 26 m.
6. **Debug**: `G.debug.sling.fire({x, y, z})` aims at a point and fires through the real path; `G.debug.give('sling')`.

#### Assets
- `held_sling` (code-built, ≤ 160 tris): a Y-shaped wood frame `#8a5a3c` with a leather pouch `#6b4a2e` and a band
  `#c2563f`. ART BRIEF per [systems#brief-tools](../bible/systems.md#brief-tools) (Sling row); image prompt preamble +
  `icon` + "a small wooden Y-shaped sling with a red band and a leather pouch holding a glowing ember seed"; 3D prompt
  "low-poly wooden slingshot with red band and leather pouch, 160 triangles, 0.25 m". Icon cell `sling` (128²).
- `gate_bell_target`, `gate_cold_brazier`, `gate_ice_wall` (code-built; ART BRIEFs from [systems#brief-gates](../bible/systems.md#brief-gates);
  `landmark_bell` is S-227b's). Fallbacks: `lathe` bell on a `cyl` post with a red ribbon decal; `cyl` bowl on three
  thin `cyl` legs; transparent blue `box`.
- `kettle_card_ember` (S-224) for the seed; fallback an orange additive dot.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all.
- `bun test`: draw/cooldown/range numbers; the projectile's 25 m cut-off; `EV.EMBER` routing; the solver models
  `sling` and its report lists what it opens per land.
- In-page (`tools`, case `sling`): in both cameras, aimed shots at 10 and 20 m hit; 26 m fizzles; bell target rings
  once and stays rung after reload; brazier lights; ice wall melts on the third hit; wax plug melts; walking speed
  while drawing is 2.2 m/s; a lock-on shot hits a moving dummy.
- HUD: the reticle passes `hud.audit` at 1080p and phone width.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test`; `node tests/inpage.mjs tools`; `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope
- Earning the Sling (the Belly's r5 chest, S-229). Home targets (S-227b). Kinako's quick-draw (economy step).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: hold-to-draw on `tool` collides with the tool ring's hold-0.35 s gesture; while the Sling is equipped, holding
`tool` draws, and the ring opens on a hold of the ring action only when the Sling is not equipped (or via a double-tap
of `tool`, documented in Controls). Pick one in the PR and test it. Rollback: revert; no save field.

<a id="s-227b"></a>
### S-227b · feat: four landmark bells, the Bell Hollow and the Snowglobe's ice wall on home
Milestone M26 · Depends on: S-227a, S-074 · Size: M (about 360 lines) · Save: none · Flag: none ·
Bible: [atlas-home#bell-targets](../bible/atlas-home.md#bell-targets), [atlas-home#passes](../bible/atlas-home.md#passes),
[systems#realm-registry](../bible/systems.md#realm-registry), [systems#economy](../bible/systems.md#economy)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M26 · Kettle Isle: the forge, the Belly and Starsteel**. The Sling's home finds (pacing rule 5): a bell
on every landmark, a small puzzle room that answers all four, and an old ice wall with something big snoring behind it
(the Snowglobe, M27).

#### What
1. **Pass row 21** `placeBellTargets` on `new RNG(7151 * 163 + 97)` (atlas-home), writing `bellTargets`, `bellHollow`,
   `snowglobeWall`; all rules of atlas-home §1.1 (own stream, rule-4 margins, world test seeds 1-40):
   - **Four landmark bells**: a small bronze Lamplighter bell on an iron bracket on each landmark at Y = base + 0.45H,
     on the side facing that region's path. Pitches: Woods (Grumble's Crown) **C4**, Peaks (the Cairn) **G4**, Shores
     (the lighthouse) **E4**, Meadow (the windmill) **D4**. The Cairn's bell must be within 25 m and in sight of a
     reachable cell (seed 7151: the east foot). An ember hit rings it for good (`gate:home:bell-woods|peaks|shores|
     meadow`, bible reconciliation R2). When all four are rung (any order): a deep chord across the isle and the toast
     "✿ Four bells! Something in the Meadow answers."
   - **The Bell Mound**: a grassy mound 7 m wide, 2.5 m tall, `solid: { r: 3.2 }`, at `near((−84, −20), 0, 12)` in the
     Meadow region, with a round spiral-carved door on the side facing the Meadow path. Closed until the four bells
     ring (Look: "A little round door in a hill. It hums when a bell rings, somewhere."), then `[E] Enter · The Bell
     Hollow` → interior `bell_hollow` (systems id; a one-room interior with a find, not a Hollow: no petal), card "The
     Bell Hollow" / "Four bells, one tune":
     ```
     ####N####
     #.......#
     #.......#
     #.......#
     W...o...E
     #.......#
     #...c...#
     #...@...#
     ####X#S##
     ```
     Four bells hang on the walls (`N` Woods carving, `E` Peaks, `S` Shores, `W` Meadow), each with its landmark
     carved above it and the same pitch as its landmark bell; `o` is a plaque stand: `[E] Read · Plaque` "Ring us as
     the sun walks." Solution **E, S, W, N** (sunrise east, south at noon, sunset west, north is the night). Bells are
     rung by sword (`[E] Ring · Bell` too) or ember. A wrong bell chimes and all four reset after 2 s (no harm). Solved:
     the chest `c` rises (**60 gems**, `S.chests['home:bell-hollow']`) and the Lore tab gains "The Bell Signal" (text
     from lore.md if it defines one; otherwise `lore.bellsignal` "When a Lamplighter boat came home safe, the four
     landmark bells were rung east, south, west and north, the way the sun walks, so everyone on the isle knew: a boat
     is home."). Prints fixture for the interior; solver: chest reachable.
   - **The Snowglobe ice wall**: an ice slab 3.2 × 2.6 m sealing a cave mouth in a rock arch on a Peaks slope face:
     `near(arena (114, −19), 26-44 m ring, any bearing)` in the Peaks region, a cell whose uphill neighbour within 2 m is
     ≥ 3 m higher, facing a reachable approach, ≥ 5 m outside the arena ring. **3 ember hits** melt it (each shrinks it
     a third, steam puffs; flag `gate:home:icewall`). Look before: "A wall of very old ice. Something big is snoring
     behind it." After melting, the cave mouth shows a dark 3 m alcove with a sign: "Snowglobe. Please shake gently.
     — F." The portal into the dungeon is M27's S-238 (a record on this item).
2. **Next free row** stays `(229, 163)` after rows 27-29 (this row 21 was reserved).
3. **Economy**: +60 (Bell Hollow chest). Completion: the Bell Hollow chest counts in home's chests row; regenerate
   `js/realms/stats.js`.

#### Assets
- `landmark_bell` (code-built, ≤ 220 tris): ART BRIEF verbatim from atlas-home#bell-targets (a small bronze bell with a
  spiral on its waist on a curly iron bracket; 0.6 m bell, 1.0 m bracket; `#c9a14a` `#8a6a30`, iron `#3a3a3a`; swings
  20° for 1.5 s when rung). M27 reuses it (Sling Gallery, Steam Hop bell).
- `bell_mound` (code-built, ≤ 900 tris) and `ice_wall` (≤ 600 tris): ART BRIEFs verbatim from atlas-home (prompts
  included). Bell Hollow interior furniture from the shared interior kit; four wall bells reuse `landmark_bell`.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all. Home prints byte-identical; row 21 changes nothing else on seeds 1-40 (test (a)); the Cairn's
  bell has a reachable firing cell within 25 m with line of sight on every seed 1-40 (or the pass moves it round the
  landmark until true).
- `bun test`: the Bell Hollow solver; the E-S-W-N rule rejects other orders; economy ledger ≥ 105 %.
- In-page (`tools`, case `home-bells`): ring all four bells with `G.debug.sling.fire`, the toast, the mound opens,
  solve E-S-W-N, a wrong order resets in 2 s, the chest pays 60 once; melt the ice wall with three shots.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test` (`tests/world.test.js` row 21); `node tests/inpage.mjs tools`; `bun run test:smoke`;
  `bun run test:playthrough` (Act I unchanged).

#### Out of scope
- The Snowglobe dungeon and its portal (S-238, M27).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: a bell at 0.45H on the Cairn's needle may sit above 25 m from every reachable cell on some seed; the pass turns
the bell round the needle in 15° steps until the firing-cell test passes. Rollback: revert; bell flags in a save are
ignored.

<a id="s-228"></a>
### S-228 · feat: Kettle Isle finds: chests, keepsakes, acorns, vistas, Lamp Logs, a bottle, Hollow doors, benches
Milestone M26 · Depends on: S-223b, S-074, S-086b · Size: M (about 430 lines, mostly data) · Save: none · Flag: realm still hidden ·
Bible: [atlas-far#kettle-pois](../bible/atlas-far.md#kettle-pois), [atlas-far#kettle-keepsakes](../bible/atlas-far.md#kettle-keepsakes),
[atlas-far#kettle-curios](../bible/atlas-far.md#kettle-curios), [atlas-far#kettle-arrival](../bible/atlas-far.md#kettle-arrival),
[atlas-far#kettle-coverage](../bible/atlas-far.md#kettle-coverage), [atlas-far#poi-kinds](../bible/atlas-far.md#poi-kinds),
[lore#bottle-table](../bible/lore.md#bottle-table), [lore#lamp-logs](../bible/lore.md#lamp-logs)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M26 · Kettle Isle: the forge, the Belly and Starsteel**. The isle's collectibles, every one with an
anchor and a gate, so that every 40 × 40 m cell of land holds something to find, see or walk to.

#### What
All by the far pass `placeFinds` on `new RNG(40261 * 71 + 3)` (atlas-far#far-passes), each at the nearest valid cell
within 3 m of its anchor (y given = on that ledge/prop), additive, prints unchanged after this step's refresh:
1. **Chests** `kettle:c01` … `c12`, 30 gems each, exactly the atlas table: c01 (−130, −72) the Hammerwheel's pit
   behind the paddles · c02 (−165, −55) y 12 a hatch on the shelf above the forge row, opens when **both cold braziers
   on the Forge Ledge** (two `cold_brazier` gates at (−125, −58) and (−115, −58), y 8) are lit from below (Ember Sling)
   · c03 (−40, 120) bottom terrace basin rim · c04 (15, 80) behind the bathhouse boiler · c05 (55, 35) halfway along
   the dark shoulder tube (Lantern; a `dark_mouth` at each tube end) · c06 (120, 110) tide-pool cove · c07 (185, 115)
   y 16 the Teacup's rim (Leaf Glider from Spoon Point (162, 92), R18) · c08 (100, 150) buried (Digging Paws soft
   earth, the `placeBuried` pass `new RNG(40261 * 73 + 5)`) · c09 (100, −50) third-switchback alcove · c10 (112, −22)
   y 28 the Spout Mouth's lip (steam puffs push 0.5 m every 6 s, harmless) · c11 (−85, −10) y 26 on top of the Handle
   (hookbloom on its crown) · c12 (60, −140) y 12 a north-coast cliff shelf up an **ivy lattice** (Ivy Gloves, S-248:
   placed now with the `ivy` gate, unreachable until the Gloves; the solver marks it "Act III return").
2. **Keepsakes** (4, LOCKED pattern, `S.secrets` ids, the shipped twinkle within 6 m): `kettle:k-mould` (−112, −78)
   "Something warm" · Lamp-cage mould; `kettle:k-towel` (−10, 80) "Something folded" · Very old towel; `kettle:k-spoon`
   (195, 125) "Something shiny" · Tiny tin spoon; `kettle:k-pebble` (20, −120) "Something glassy" · Star-scorched
   pebble. Two-line texts verbatim from [atlas-far#kettle-keepsakes](../bible/atlas-far.md#kettle-keepsakes) (each
   ≤ 220 characters); the land's **fifth line** when all four are found: "✿ Kettle Isle's keepsakes: lamps were born
   here, baths were saved, and something bright fell into the Kettle on the night of the Storm." Keepsake Hollow shelf
   "Kettle Isle" (east wall, 4 niches, S-056 order).
3. **Sleeping Acorns** `kettle:acorn-1` … `9` at (−140, −20), (−110, 40), (−30, 100), (30, 110), (130, 40), (160, 130),
   (80, −70), (−20, −110), (60, 20) y 18 (a cone-flank ledge reached from the shoulder tube's mouth).
4. **Lamp Logs** (texts and numbers are lore.md's; positions the atlas's, bible reconciliation R19): `kettle:17`
   "Forgefall" at the Lampwright's Bench (−158, −45) · `kettle:18` "The Kettle Breathes" in a niche on the spout ridge
   (70, −5) y 28 · `kettle:16` "Wardens" inside the Teacup's hollow (185, 115) y 12 (lore.md says "a niche at the
   Bathhouse Terraces"; the atlas's Teacup spot is used and the log keeps its number and text).
5. **Vistas** (Hoot's posts, sketches in S-224's `sketch_kettle_1-3` or canvas fallback): `kettle:vista-spout` "The
   Spout Lets Off Steam" (110, −15) y 28 · `kettle:vista-handle` "Through the Handle" on the Handle's top (Hook) ·
   `kettle:vista-teacup` "Tea for the Sea" on the Teacup's rim (Glider). Hoot's captions: "Kettle Isle, at a simmer.
   I could smell the toast." / "A handle on a mountain. Lift with your legs." / "One lump or two? The sea declined."
6. **Bottle** `kettle:9` (Mallow Thistledown's letter, lore#bottle-table) on the Ash Beach tideline (190, 40).
7. **Doors and benches**: `belly_mouth` (90, 5) y 24 (dungeon door anchor; its portal is S-229's) ·
   `hollow_kettle_sling` (130, 140) Ash Beach tube · `hollow_kettle_steam` (60, 75) a steam grotto under the top
   terrace (Hollow door anchors; S-234 builds them) · benches `kettle:bench-forge` (−150, −30), `-terrace` (−5, 70),
   `-beach` (150, 60), `-switchback` (95, −40), `-handle` (−95, 30) · root-gate `kettle:gate` (−80, −10) at the
   Handle's west foot (sealed stub; Paws open it, S-182's rule) · `kettle:lid-ladder` (record, hidden until
   `stoneHeld:kettle`; S-232a shows it).
8. **Curios** (14, `[E] Look · <pill>`) verbatim from [atlas-far#kettle-curios](../bible/atlas-far.md#kettle-curios)
   (anchors and lines as listed: Forge rota, Sword rack, Warm waterfall, Towel line, Bath rules, Copper pipes,
   Footprints, Steam fern, Lava ripples, Plume, Scorch line, Handle carving, Tea stall sign, Sea glass).
9. **Fish spot** `kettle:fish-1` Forgefall pier (−210, −50), code `k1` ("warm water"), on the far pass `placeFishSpots`
   `new RNG(40261 * 83 + 9)`: Warmwater Loach becomes catchable (22 of 24 species).
10. **Coverage**: `js/realms/coverage.js` over 13 × 13 cells of 40 m (last row/column 32 m), land cells ≥ 200 m²
    (expected 80-92); every land cell passes on a find, a view or a path (atlas-far#poi-kinds); fill pass ≤ 12 %.
    POI total must reach **96** (target 120,000 / 1,250); the test prints the count (atlas budget 98 with S-237's
    activity starts; without them ≥ 96 is still required: count the Steam Hop vents only if S-223a shipped them as
    `activity` POIs, which it did).
11. **Completion**: Kettle Isle's row group (chests 12, keepsakes 4, acorns 9, vistas 3, Hollows 2, regions 4, benches
    5, Lamp Logs 3, bottle 1); regenerate `js/realms/stats.js`. Economy: 12 × 30 gems in the ledger.

**Prize chests** (CANON §10.4; rows already in `js/content/prizes.js`, opened by S-086a's chest path): this land's
three prize chests give their item instead of 30 gems: `c04` Copper Kettle Stove, `c10` Ember Rust trim, `c07` "Teacup" sticker. Add one unit test that each listed chest resolves to its
prize, and keep `STATS` chest counts unchanged.

#### Assets
- All finds use shared kit models (chest, keepsake twinkle, acorn, vista post, log niche, bottle, bench, root-gate
  knot, Hollow door, curio pill). The two Forge Ledge braziers use `gate_cold_brazier` (S-227a). The hatch for c02 is
  code-built (≤ 80 tris).

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except "Frozen home island". Kettle prints refreshed in this PR only.
- `bun test` (`tests/realms/kettle.test.js`): every listed find exists at its anchor ± 3 m, on walkable ground or its
  ledge; gates resolve to abilities; the solver reaches every find in the final state and reports which need which
  tool (c02 sling, c05 lantern, c07 glide, c08 dig, c11 hook, c12 climb); coverage passes; POI ≥ 96; stats match.
- Keepsake and log texts ≤ 220 / 560 characters; the fifth line shows when all four keepsakes are found (in-page).

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test`; `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`; `node tests/inpage.mjs side` (case `kettle-finds`: open c01,
  read log 17, find a keepsake, catch a Warmwater Loach with a forced roll).

#### Out of scope
- Thornglass (S-233b), Hollows and the Dash (S-234), the Belly (S-229).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: coverage fails on the steep cone cells; those cells are only counted where walkable (the ridge path), as the
atlas notes. Rollback: revert; hidden realm.

<a id="s-229"></a>
### S-229 · feat: the Kettle's Belly rooms 1-7: valves raise and lower platforms
Milestone M26 · Depends on: S-227a, S-053 · Size: M (about 480 lines with map data) · Save: none · Flag: realm
`kettles_belly` hidden until S-232a · Bible: [dungeons#d8](../bible/dungeons.md#d8), [dungeons#entities](../bible/dungeons.md#entities),
[dungeons#state](../bible/dungeons.md#state), [dungeons#rules](../bible/dungeons.md#rules), [systems#portal-table](../bible/systems.md#portal-table)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M26 · Kettle Isle: the forge, the Belly and Starsteel**. Dungeon 8 is a dungeon of **steam**: valves
that raise brass platforms two seconds after they turn (so Pip can step on), then, from room 5, the Ember Sling turns
valves from afar, lights braziers and melts wax. This step builds rooms 1-7 including the Sling's chest (the bible's
step split; INDEX's S-230 row also mentions the room-5 chest: it ships here).

#### What
1. **Data** `js/realms/dungeons/kettles_belly.js` (realm id `kettles_belly`, bible reconciliation R11): copy
   **verbatim** from [dungeons#d8](../bible/dungeons.md#d8) the whole map `kettle_belly L1` (41 rows; the map key in the
   file uses the realm id), legend extension (`v` steam valve, `z` steam platform (this dungeon's meaning), `a` cold
   brazier, `f` wax plug as a door glyph), the platforms table, room table and entities. Fields: `name: "The Kettle's
   Belly"`, `kind: 'dungeon'`, grid 98 m, `palette: 'kettles_belly'` (stone `#3b3540`, brass `#c79a4a`, ember
   `#e66f4f`, steam `#f4f1ea`, mint `#8fc7a0`, dark tint `#221c24`), `light: { ambient: 0.6, torches: true }` plus warm
   orange underglow from vents (glow materials, no point lights), `music: 'kettles_belly'` (variant `{ from: 'depths',
   transpose: 5, voices: { lead: 'brass' }, add: { perc: [{ pat: 'k . . . p . a .', vol: 0.35 }] } }`), `parent:
   'kettle'`, `door: 'belly_mouth'`, `needsOnEntry: ['lantern']`. Name card line 2 "inside the Kettle". Rooms r8-r13
   compile as `stub: true` (no entities) in this step; the door (10,12) r7 → r8 opens onto the stub.
2. **Entities** (`js/dungeon-mod.js`):
   - **Steam valve** `v`: a red-and-brass hand wheel on a pipe post. `[E] Use · Valve` or an ember hit (`EV.EMBER`)
     toggles its listed platforms; the wheel spins one turn and puffs steam at once; **the platforms move 2.0 s later,
     over 1.2 s**, carrying Pip (a moving `deck`; Pip rides with it, never pushed through a ceiling: a platform whose
     rise would crush Pip against the ceiling stops 0.2 m short). State `state['r2:v1'] = true|false`.
   - **Steam platform** `z`: a 2 × 2 m riveted brass plate on a fat piston, `lo → hi` heights in digits (0.3 m each;
     `pit` = −4 m). Steam curls from the base while moving; a soft hiss.
   - **Wax plug** `f` (door glyph): a round creamy wax seal `#f4e3b8` with a stamped spiral filling the doorway; one
     ember hit melts it in 1.0 s, for good (`open['r5:f1']`). Swing on it: "Wax. It wants warming."
   - **Cold brazier** `a`: lit by an ember hit or the Lantern's Kindle within 1 tile, lit for good (`lit['r6:a1']`);
     doors with `lit:[…]` open when all listed are lit (the S-059 rule).
3. **Rooms r1-r7** exactly as the bible: r1 sign `?`(3,3) in Scoria's square letters "Valves: turn, wait, ride.
   Kettle: do not poke. — S." (`ke.belly.sign.r1`), `X` (0,4) to the Spout (spawn `belly`); r2 `v1` (13,3) raises
   `z1-z3` (14-16,4) pit → 0 into a walkway, Steam Puff ×1 at (18,1), map `&` (19,7), door (20,4) `always`; r3 (ceiling
   7) `v1` (24,3) raises `z1` (25,3) 0 → 9 (2.7 m), walk round to it within the 2 s, ride to the ledge, gem chest
   `r3:c1` (25,1); door (30,4) `always`; r4 wave Soot Snuffle ×3 at (33,2), (37,2), (35,6), clear → small key `r4:k1`
   (35,4), locked door (35,8); r5 treasure `$` (35,12) the **Ember Sling** with item-get (systems controls, R6-style
   rewrite of the bible line): "You got the Ember Sling! Hold {tool} to draw and let go to fling a warm ember seed. It
   lights braziers, melts wax and rings bell targets." (effects `item:sling`, `ability:sling`, equipped); the only way on
   is wax door (30,12) west; the south door (35,16) opens from r12 (S-230); r6 four braziers `r6:a1-a4` on stumps inside
   the pit at (24,11), (26,11), (24,13), (26,13): light all four with embers → door (20,12) opens (`lit`); wave Ash Moth
   ×2 swooping over the pit; Chime Stone `%` (25,15); r7 (ceiling 7) `v1` (19,11) → `z1` (18,11) 0 → 9 to the east ledge;
   from there an ember at `v2` (15,15) raises `z2-z3` (14-15,9) pit → 9 to ledge height; cross to the west ledge, small
   key `r7:k1` (11,9); wave Soot Snuffle ×2 at (13,13), (17,13); door (10,12) `always`.
4. **Registry and portal**: `kettles_belly` (`hidden: true`, bundle `kettle`, parent `kettle`). Portal `kettle:belly`
   on S-228's `belly_mouth` anchor (90, 5) y 24: a `dark_mouth` gate (Lantern lit to walk in) and `[E] Enter · The
   Kettle's Belly`, `needs: 'flag:kettleDepths'` (systems' `quest:mq-kettle>=3` written as the flag the people step
   sets; S-232a sets it). Until then: Look "A dark lava tube. Warm air breathes out of it. Not yet."
5. **Solver**: model the valve delay (a platform is boardable when Pip can walk from the valve to it within 2.0 s,
   at run speed 4.4 m/s: ≤ 8.8 m path, and the solver checks each hand-turned valve has its platform within 2 tiles
   of walking as the bible says), ember-turned valves (Pip may stand on the platform and shoot the valve), lit doors,
   wax doors needing `sling`. Target for this step: r1-r7 — prove `&`, `%`, `$`, `r3:c1`, `r4:k1`, `r7:k1` reachable,
   no soft-lock (a fixture with `r3:v1` moved 5 tiles from `z1` must fail with "r3:z1 unboardable").
6. **Hints** `ke.belly.hint.1` verbatim ("Valves are slow on purpose." / "Turn the valve, then hop straight onto the
   brass platform. It waits two seconds before it moves."); 2 and 3 in S-230.
7. **Prints** `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/kettles_belly.json`.

#### Assets
- `tex_kettle_rock` (realm `kettle`): preamble + `tile` + "Seamless texture of warm dark volcanic rock with smooth
  rounded lumps, faint orange glow in the cracks and little brass pipe brackets, tileable. Palette: #3b3540 #e66f4f
  #c79a4a #221c24."; `make_seamless.py --blend 64`; 1024/512 webp; fallback `#3b3540` noise with `#e66f4f` cracks.
- Code-built: `int_valve` (0.8 m red-and-brass wheel on a post; fallback red torus on a post), `int_steam_platform`
  (ART BRIEF verbatim from dungeons#d8: riveted brass plate 2 × 2 m on a piston 1.2 m wide, travel up to 2.7 m, brass
  `#c79a4a`, iron `#5b5e66`, gauge face `#f4f1ea`, 600 / 150 tris, image prompt "<style line> A friendly riveted brass
  lift platform on a steam piston inside a cosy volcano workshop, steam curling, warm orange glow.", 3D prompt
  "Stylised low-poly riveted brass platform on a steam piston with a small gauge, hand-painted, game-ready, under 600
  triangles, 2 m square."), `int_wax_plug` (cream disc with a stamped spiral; melts with drips), `int_brazier` (kit).
- `fx_steam` (generated sheet 4 × 2 frames of 128² soft white steam puffs, transparent; `slice_sheet.py --parts 8`,
  webp ≤ 40 KB; fallback white quads) if S-223a's `kettle_card_steam` cannot serve as the puff.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except "Frozen home island". Kettle prints equal (portal is a record).
- `bun test`: map test equal; compiler; solver r1-r7 targets and the unboardable fixture.
- In-page (`dungeons`, case `kettles_belly_1`): valve → platform moves after 2.0 s over 1.2 s carrying Pip; r3 ride
  reaches the chest; r5 gives the Sling once; the wax door melts on one ember; four braziers open r6's door; r7's
  ember-at-valve from the platform works; state survives reload; dizzy wakes at `@` with puzzle state kept.
- Realm suite: room views ≤ 120 draw calls and ≤ 150 k tris in both cameras; generation ≤ 0.15 s. Hidden.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test`; `node tests/inpage.mjs dungeons` (add `G.debug.dungeon.use(id)` if missing: it calls the
  valve's real use handler); `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`; `bun run test:smoke`; `node tools/build-pages.mjs && bun run test:pwa`.

#### Out of scope
- r8-r13, high braziers, the boss door (S-230); Lady Fumarole (S-231); going live (S-232a).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: riding moving decks is new for dungeons; it reuses the moving-deck carry of the Glassway boat/rafts if S-198
built one, else a deck whose `h` lerps with Pip snapped to it while standing. Rollback: revert; hidden realm.

<a id="s-230"></a>
### S-230 · feat: the Kettle's Belly rooms 8-13: braziers at range and wax plugs
Milestone M26 · Depends on: S-229 · Size: M (about 380 lines) · Save: none · Flag: realm still hidden ·
Bible: [dungeons#d8](../bible/dungeons.md#d8), [dungeons#entities](../bible/dungeons.md#entities)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M26 · Kettle Isle: the forge, the Belly and Starsteel**. The Sling's half of the Belly: valves turned
from the platform they move, braziers hung too high for hands, a shortcut back to the Sling room, and the door to Lady
Fumarole's spout.

#### What
1. **High wall brazier** variant (`a` with `high: true`, `int_brazier_wall` on an iron arm at 3 m): lit only by an
   ember (the Lantern's Kindle cannot reach). Brazier-driven platforms: `r11:z1` (26,18) 0 → 2 and `r11:z2` (27,18)
   0 → 4 **rise for good** when both `r11:a1` (22,17) and `r11:a2` (28,23) (both high) are lit.
2. **Rooms r8-r13** (remove `stub: true`), entities exactly as the bible: r8 The Wax Works (ceiling 7): valve `v1`
   (8,14) far from platform `z1` (3,10) 0 → 9 — stand on the platform and fling an ember at the valve, ride up, gem
   chest `r8:c3` (1,9); wave Steam Puff ×1 at (3,13); wax door (5,16) south; r9 The Ash Loft (ceiling 8): wave Ash Moth
   ×3 at (3,18), (7,18), (5,21), clear opens door (10,20); gem chest `r9:c2` (1,23); r10 The Boiler Room: the great
   boiler (pillars 14-16 × 19-21) hisses (a looping steam card on top), wave Steam Puff ×2 at (12,22), (18,22); locked
   door (20,20) east; boss door `B` (15,24) south; r11 The Gauge Room (ceiling 7): Root Key chest `K` (29,17) on a
   ledge (digit 6), the two high braziers raise the steps; wave Soot Snuffle ×1 at (23,22); r12 The Pressure Room: wave
   Steam Puff ×2 at (33,19), (37,19); crystal switch `r12:s1` (35,17) opens door (35,16) to r5 for good (the
   shortcut); gem chest `r12:c4` (38,23); r13 Lady Fumarole's Spout (ceiling 16): arena 19 × 15 tiles, the spout lid
   dais (digit 2, 3 × 3) at the centre with her bath (S-231 fills it), boss spawn `M` (20,30), `X` (20,40) back to the
   Spout (spawn `belly`; S-232a switches it to `lid` once the stone is out). Gem chests: c1 (S-229) + c2, c3, c4 at 40
   = 160 gems.
3. **Hints** `ke.belly.hint.2-3` verbatim ("Some valves are easier to reach from the platform." / "In the Wax Works,
   stand on the brass platform and fling an ember at the valve across the room. Up you go." · "Those braziers in the
   Gauge Room are hung too high for hands." / "Fling an ember at each of the two high braziers. The brass steps rise up
   to the Root Key.").
4. **Solver** — whole dungeon: prove the bible's solver path (r1 → r2 → r3 → r4 → unlock → r5 `$` → melt → r6 → r7 →
   r8 (stand on `z1`, ember `v1`, ride, `c3`; melt (5,16)) → r9 → r10 → unlock (20,20) → r11 (`a1`, `a2`, climb, `K`)
   → r12 (`s1`, `c4`) → r11 → r10 → B → r13 → `X`), every chest and key, the boss door, no soft-lock; replay it as a
   script test.
5. **Ledger**: `r4:k1` → L (35,8); `r7:k1` → L (20,20); `K` → B (15,24). Map r2, Chime Stone r6, Sling r5.
6. Prints refreshed (frozen after S-232a).

#### Assets
- `int_brazier_wall` (code-built, ≤ 140 tris): the kit brazier on an iron wall arm at 3 m. `int_boiler` (code-built,
  ≤ 400 tris): a riveted copper boiler `#c79a4a` / `#8fc7a0` filling the 3 × 3 pillar block, gauges, a steam card on
  top. Fallbacks: kit brazier; `cyl`.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except "Frozen home island".
- `bun test`: map test; full solver path; every chest (`&`, `%`, `K`, `$`, c1-c4) and both keys; no soft-lock.
- In-page (`dungeons`, case `kettles_belly_2`): r8's ride by ember; r11's high braziers raise the steps for good; the r12
  switch opens the r5 shortcut; the boss door opens with the Root Key; r13 is reachable and empty (S-231 adds her).
- Realm suite: r8-r13 views ≤ 120 draw calls (r13 ≤ 150) and ≤ 150 k tris.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test`; `node tests/inpage.mjs dungeons`; `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`; `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope
- Lady Fumarole (S-231); going live (S-232a); the ss-38 starsteel shard in `r12:c4` (S-233a adds it to the chest).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: an ember fired from a moving platform at a valve that lowers that platform strands Pip; r8's valve only raises
(`0 → 9` and back only by another turn while Pip is on the ledge), and the solver proves no strand. Rollback: revert.

<a id="s-230b"></a>

### S-230b · feat: The Kettle's Belly grows the Whistle Room, the Pressure Cock and the Gauge Cupboard: the steam slows down (depth rule R14)

Milestone M26 · Depends on: S-230, S-060b, S-104b (shared annex pieces and the latch) · Size: M (200-500; most of it map data and entity tables) · Save: none · Flag: the dungeon realm stays `hidden: true` · Bible: [../bible/dungeons.md#d8-depth](../bible/dungeons.md#d8-depth), [../bible/dungeons.md#depth-common](../bible/dungeons.md#depth-common), [../bible/dungeons.md#depth-counts](../bible/dungeons.md#depth-counts), [../bible/dungeons.md#rules](../bible/dungeons.md#rules), [../bible/dungeons.md#d8](../bible/dungeons.md#d8)

Added by the playtime review (dungeons.md R14, CANON §11.1 A2; see [Index changes](#index-changes)). Filed after
S-230 and before S-231, which now depends on it.

#### Why
Roadmap item **M26 · Kettle Isle: the forge, the Belly and Starsteel**. Every story dungeon had about one simple beat per room and almost no room where two
mechanics meet, so The Kettle's Belly played in well under its budget and taught one idea without building on it. Rule R14
asks for ≥ 1.6 beats per counted room, ≥ 4 compound rooms, one mid-dungeon state flip that sends the player back
through rooms already solved, and a last room before the boss that uses everything. This step builds
The Kettle's Belly's annex, flip, latch and synthesis from the bible, so the dungeon meets the rule before its boss lands.

#### What
1. **Annex levels.** Copy the map blocks of [dungeons.md#d8-depth](../bible/dungeons.md#d8-depth) verbatim
   into the dungeon's data module (`js/realms/dungeons/<id>.js`, the file its room steps created): `kettle_belly LA` (`at` [43,0]): r14 The Whistle Room, r15 The Pressure Cock; `kettle_belly LB` (`at` [43,10]): r16 The Gauge Cupboard; extent 64 × 41, realm grid 144 m. Room
   table rows, entity rows and the room-by-room conditions exactly as the bible gives them. Annex levels join the §5
   rooms only through stair warps.
2. **Stairs.** r14 `^`(1,7) ↔ r10 (12,18), a hatch that opens on `lit:[r10:a1]`; r16 `^`(1,7) ↔ r7 (18,9) on the ledge, sealed until the flip.
3. **Tile edits to the §5 map** (apply to the bible's map block and to the data in this PR; the map test compares
   against the edited block): r10 (12,18) `.` → `^`; r10 (18,18) `.` → `a` (`r10:a1`, high); r10 (14,22) `.` → `v` (`r10:v1`); r7 (18,9) `9` → `^`. Update the bible's room table, keys-and-chests ledger and solver notes to
   match, in the same PR.
4. **The flip.** **the Pressure Cock** `r15:s1` (19,1), flag `kb.cock`, line `dg.kb.flip` "Pipes knock all through the Belly. Somewhere the steam runs slower now.". From then on: **Q = r8** and **P = r7**: every valve in r7 and r8 waits 4.0 s instead of 2.0 s, and each platform's rest and raised heights swap (`lo ↔ hi`); in r7 ride a platform down and back up, turning its valve with an ember from the platform, to reach the ledge stair (18,9). Saved as `S.dungeons[<id>].state.flip = true` (v8
   container, no bump); the dungeon compiler applies the listed entity-state changes when it builds Q and P; the first
   entry into each after the flip plays the 0.4 s result cue (R7).
5. **The latch.** `B` (15,24) opens on `key:K && latch:r16:s1 && clear:r10`. Hitting the latch plays the toast `dg.latch` and a positional clunk at `B`.
6. **Synthesis in the room before the boss** (R14 item 4): r10: turn the new valve `r10:v1` (14,22) to lift the boiler's steam caps so the new high brazier `r10:a1` (18,18) is in sight, then light it with an ember to open the hatch; the wave is `clear` for `B`.
7. **Ask the Tree** gains two hint pairs for this dungeon (systems#hints): "The Boiler Room has a brazier hiding in the steam." / "Turn the new valve in the Boiler Room, fling an ember at the brazier it uncovers, go down the hatch and work your way to the Pressure Cock." · "The valves are slow now, and up is down." /
   "In the Steam Lift, stand on the platform and fling an ember at its valve; wait for the slow steam to carry you to the ledge stair." (text ids `dg.kettle_belly.hint.4`, `.5`).
8. **Solver and depth test.** The flip is one more state bit in the lock-and-key graph (S-049); the solver proves the
   whole path (§5 rooms → annex → flip → Q and P re-solved → latch → `B`), every chest, and no soft-lock whether the
   player flips early or late. Extend `tests/dungeon-depth.test.js` (S-060b) with this dungeon's row: 16 rooms, 14 counted, ≥ 23 beats (planned 27), ≥ 4 compound rooms (planned 7); the test prints the per-room table
   for the PR body.
9. **Two waves from Act III** (dungeons.md R14; CANON §10.3): every combat room of this dungeon gets a second wave of mixed archetypes at the land's pattern tier (creatures.md#pattern-tiers), spawning 1.5 s after the first is calmed, at most 2 attackers at once; each room's `clear` waits for both. List the second waves in each room's entry in `dungeons.md`.

#### Assets
- The annex rooms reuse this dungeon's kit, textures and palette (no new generated images; the annex rooms are within
  the realm bundle already budgeted).
- **`int_pressure_cock`** (code-built, ≤ 140 tris; the flip prop): a copper pipe with a big star-shaped tap and a round pressure gauge with a red needle. ART BRIEF: silhouette as described;
  size 1.2 m; palette `#b87333` `#e66f4f` `#f4ead2`; toon materials; 140 / 600 tris (code / painted GLB); pose static, it
  turns or swings once when used; image prompt "<style line> a copper pipe with a big star-shaped tap and a round pressure gauge with a red needle, cosy storybook dungeon prop, three-quarter
  view"; 3D prompt "stylised low-poly a copper pipe with a big star-shaped tap and a round pressure gauge with a red needle, hand-painted texture, game-ready, under 600
  triangles, 1.2 m". Manifest key `int_pressure_cock` (commented-out GLB slot). Fallback: the code-built model.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep *Frozen realms* (this dungeon's prints are refreshed in this PR, inside its own build milestone,
  before the land is unhidden), *Hidden until whole*, *One save slot* (no bump), *Budgets* (R11: every annex room view
  ≤ 120 draw calls and ≤ 150 k triangles), *Tone*, *Assets*, *Machine*.
- `tests/dungeon-maps.test.js` passes with the annex blocks and the edited §5 block; data equals the bible blocks.
- The solver proves the full path, every chest, the flip in both orders, and no soft-lock; the depth test row passes
  the thresholds above.
- In page: hitting the flip changes Q and P exactly as listed; `B` stays shut until the latch (or d1's plate) and the
  Root Key; the hints show in Ask the Tree.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `agentctl heavy --job <job> -- bun test tests/dungeon-maps.test.js tests/dungeon-depth.test.js` and the
  dungeon's solver test.
- `agentctl heavy --job <job> -- node tests/inpage.mjs dungeons` (new case `kettle_belly_depth`: `G.debug.realm('<id>')`,
  play the intended path from the annex stair with `G.debug.dungeon.solve('<id>', { from: 'annex' })`, assert the flip's
  changes in Q and P, the latch, and `B`).
- `agentctl heavy --job <job> -- node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (prints refreshed, budgets).

#### Out of scope
- The boss (S-231); any new chest, key or reward (the 48-chest ledger is fixed); the land overworld.

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: the flip makes a room unsolvable from the side the player returns by; the solver run in both flip orders is the
guard, and the bible's fallback (§5A.1) adds a block and plate. Rollback: revert the PR; the §5 rooms work alone
(stair warps to a missing annex show as sealed hatches) and the realm stays hidden.

<a id="s-231"></a>
### S-231 · feat: Lady Fumarole, the fire-newt in a bath towel on the spout lid
Milestone M26 · Depends on: S-230b, S-045 · Size: M (about 500 lines with P3; ship the balance test as S-231b if over) · Save: none · Flag: realm still hidden ·
Bible: [creatures#boss-fumarole](../bible/creatures.md#boss-fumarole), [creatures#boss-def](../bible/creatures.md#boss-def),
[creatures#boss-grammar](../bible/creatures.md#boss-grammar), [creatures#hp-formula](../bible/creatures.md#hp-formula),
[creatures#balance-targets](../bible/creatures.md#balance-targets), [cast#lady-fumarole](../bible/cast.md#lady-fumarole),
[story#ch15-17](../bible/story.md#ch15-17)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M26 · Kettle Isle: the forge, the Belly and Starsteel**. The isle's Warden: a glamorous fire-newt in a
bath towel who will not let anyone touch the stone in the spout until the heat has somewhere to go. Her opening is her
own towel; the new Sling can make it come sooner.

#### What
1. **Def** `js/boss/fumarole.js`, `BOSS_DEFS['kettle.fumarole']`: `save: 'kettles_belly:fumarole'` (M27's S-236 reads
   this key), name "Lady Fumarole", title "Warden of the Kettle (in a Towel)", `kind: 'bout'`, `model: 'boss_fumarole'`,
   `height: 3.4` (sitting; 4.4 standing per cast), `r: 1.8`, `keepOut: 3.0`, `room: 3.6`, **`hp: 136`**, `enrageAt: 0.5`,
   **`enrageAt2: 0.33`, `arenaChange: 'bath'`** (the runner hook from S-164), `cool: [1.4, 2.4]` (P2 `[1.2, 2.0]`, P3
   `[1.0, 1.7]`), `slingOpen: true`, `arena: { realm: 'kettles_belly', anchor: 'r13:M', r: 13,
   shape: 'round', seal: 'bossDoor' }`. **Arena**: her bath (r 4 m, rim 0.6 m) on the spout lid dais at the centre;
   **six steam vents** in the floor at radius 8 m (bearings 30°, 90°, … 330°); four cold **braziers** against the wall
   at 45°, 135°, 225°, 315° (the Sling lights them: a warm glow, cosmetic). Moves (creatures table):

   | Move | Tell | Shape | Wind | Active | Opening | Weights P1 / P2 |
   |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
   | `flop` | she rises out of the bath with a sizzle and a falling whistle; light pillar | disc r 3.0 on Pip | 1.0 | lands 0.2 | she must re-tie her towel: **1.8 s gold** | 40 / 35 |
   | `pebbles` | stirs the bath, pebbles glow | fan × 5 (7 in P2) of warm pebbles at 8 m/s | 0.6 | - | - | 30 / 25 |
   | `steam` | three vents bubble and hiss | circles × 3 r 1.3 at vents (5 in P2, one at the vent nearest Pip) | 1.0 | pillars 1.2 | - | 30 / 25 |
   | `doubleflop` (P2) | two rises | two flops, the second disc drawn as the first lands | 1.0 + 0.8 | - | towel slips right off: **2.4 s gold** | 0 / 15 |

   **The Sling**: an ember that hits her while she is up for a `flop` makes her drop early, flustered, towel slipping:
   **2.2 s gold**. P2 at 50 %: `speedK` 1.4. All damage 1 HP; ≤ 2 threats; shrug; passive contact never hurts.
   **P3 at 33 %, arena change `bath`** (creatures#boss-fumarole): line `ke.fumarole.p3` "Ooh, too hot! More water,
   darling!"; a teal edge line is drawn at r 8 m for 2 s, then the bath water rises one step and the floor from the
   bath rim to r 8 m floods 0.3 m for the rest of the fight (wading × 0.62, `wadeK`); the three vents inside the flood
   only bubble, so `steam` uses the three outer-ring vents (plus the vent nearest Pip if he is outside the flood); `flop`
   lands with a splash disc r 4.5 (wind stays 1.0 s); each lit brazier keeps a dry circle r 2 m. P3 table `flop` 40,
   `pebbles` 25, `steam` 20, `doubleflop` 15. A dizzy reset drains the flood.
   Intro line after the name card: `ke.fumarole.i1` "Hffff. A visitor. In MY bath. Darling, there is a queue."
2. **Handover** (creatures): `ke.fumarole.h1` "Ooh! Ooh, that's chilly. Is a window open? Fetch me my other towel,
   darling." · `ke.fumarole.h2` "That stone fell straight into the spout and keeps the Kettle at a simmer. Pull it out
   with nowhere for the heat to go, and the whole isle boils over." Then scene **`stone-give-kettle`** (25 s,
   `js/content/scenes/kettle.js`, `replay: true`): `two` shot on her; `ke.fumarole.s1` (story) "Fine. FINE. The forges
   are hot again, the heat has somewhere to go. Take your rock." → `fx.stoneLift` from the spout (the stone rises out
   of the steam, glowing ember `#e66f4f`) → `ke.fumarole.s2` (story) "Mind, it's warm. Everything here is warm. That's
   rather the point of us." → `emit item stone_kettle` "You got a Stair Stone! One of the three keystones of the old
   Starstair. Set it at the Summit Crystal Cairn." + `flag stoneHeld:kettle` (creatures' third handover line overlaps
   the story's and is dropped: bible reconciliation R13 applies here too). `after: ['boss:kettles_belly:fumarole',
   'quest:mq-kettle.advance']`. Item `stone_kettle` in `js/items.js` (same shape as `stone_bell`, warm `#e66f4f` glow).
3. **After**: she rests at `kettle.fumarole` (55, 108), the hottest basin, 14:00-18:00 (cast), in her bath on the lid
   otherwise (Wake for the rematch is offered at the basin); Talk `ke.fumarole.after` "Hotter, darling. No, hotter.
   Perfect." **Plushie** `plush_fumarole` · 100 · desc "Warm to hold. Towel included." · got "A Lady Fumarole plushie!
   Still a little toasty~" (Miso's stock rule). **Rematch** 170 HP (enraged from 75 % and 40 %), 60 gems, `verb: 'Wake'`. **Dream** `dream_fumarole`
   (rising bath water, S-295). Bestiary: Hoot "Lady Fumarole has never been cold. She says so, often, while cold." ·
   Clover "After she flops she has to fix her towel. Go!"
4. **Balance test**: a careful bot wins ≥ 90 % of 20 seeded runs in **120-180 s** losing ≤ 3 hearts (8 hearts, Shiny,
   Sling: creatures' balance targets for an Act III Warden), reaching P3 in every win; wooden sword ≤ 300 s; a
   never-dodging bot wins ≤ 50 %; a Sling-using bot's mean fight time is shorter than a sword-only bot's (the Sling
   opening works).
5. Music `warden_fumarole` (`{ from: 'warden', voices: { lead: 'brass' } }`).

#### Assets
- `boss_fumarole` (code-built per cast#lady-fumarole, R8: `newt` rig scaled to 4.4 m standing, ≤ 3,000 tris): skin
  ember red `#e66f4f` with glowing specks `#ffcf7a` (emissive 0.3 via `colorFn`), belly `#f4d1a6`, a pink-and-cream
  striped bath towel (`lathe`, `#e88a8a` / `#f4d1a6`), a mint towel turban `#8fc7a0`, a yellow rubber duck `#f2c94c`
  under one arm, a soft wet specular band on the skin; transform animations lounge, rise, flop, towel re-tie (the
  opening), stir. Her bath: a `lathe` tub with a steam card. ART BRIEF (owner GLB, batch 12, written in S-242): cast's
  brief verbatim (≤ 12,000 tris, emissive specks in alpha or a second emissive map; 3D prompt "[3D preamble] a huge
  glamorous fire-newt in a striped bath towel and towel turban, glowing specks, rubber duck under one arm, 4.4 m tall.")
  + creatures' animation list. Commented-out `models` slot.
- `portrait_fumarole` (realm `kettle`): preamble + `portrait` + "A huge glamorous ember-red fire-newt lady with glowing
  golden specks, wrapped in a pink-and-cream striped bath towel with a mint towel turban, a yellow rubber duck under one
  arm, steam rising, a theatrical sigh." 512² ≤ 60 KB. `card_boss_fumarole`: preamble + `card-beast` + "a big round
  orange fire-newt lounging in a steaming bath, wearing a striped towel and a towel turban, looking pleased with herself,
  in a warm volcanic cave." 384² ≤ 35 KB. Canvas fallbacks.
- `plush_fumarole` (the S-045 plushie recipe at 0.12 scale).

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except "Frozen home island". Every move: wind-up ≥ 0.55 s (pebbles 0.6 s), damage 1 (unit test).
- `bun test`: def validates; HP 136 / rematch 170; P2 table at 50 %, P3 table and the `bath` change at 33 %; the Sling
  opening only during `flop`'s rise.
- In-page: at 33 % the flood edge is drawn 2 s before the water rises; inner vents never steam in P3; ≤ 2 threats at
  every sampled frame; a dizzy reset drains the flood.
- Balance test passes (numbers in the PR body).
- In-page (`bosses`, case `fumarole`): bout, handover, the stone scene (skippable: item, flag and `after` applied),
  `S.bosses['kettles_belly:fumarole']`, plushie stocked, Fumarole at her basin afterwards, Wake rematch at 170 HP.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test`; `node tests/inpage.mjs bosses`; `node tests/inpage.mjs dungeons`; `bun run test:smoke`;
  `node tools/build-pages.mjs && bun run test:pwa`.

#### Out of scope
- The main chain, the isle's state switch and unhiding (S-232a); her Dream (S-295); ss-39/40 (M27).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: an emissive speck pattern via `colorFn` adds a material program; reuse the shipped toon emissive path (counts
toward the realm's 12). Rollback: revert; hidden dungeon.

<a id="s-232a"></a>
### S-232a · feat: Kettle Isle's main chain: the forges relight and a Stair Stone is set
Milestone M26 · Depends on: S-231, S-225, S-212a, S-030, S-033a · Size: M (about 440 lines) · Save: none · Flag: unhides `kettle`
and `kettles_belly` · Bible: [story#ch15-17](../bible/story.md#ch15-17), [story#stone-beats](../bible/story.md#stone-beats),
[atlas-far#kettle-states](../bible/atlas-far.md#kettle-states), [atlas-far#buoys](../bible/atlas-far.md#buoys),
[atlas-far#chart](../bible/atlas-far.md#chart), [systems#quests](../bible/systems.md#quests), [systems#test-harness](../bible/systems.md#test-harness)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M26 · Kettle Isle: the forge, the Belly and Starsteel**. Kettle Isle becomes playable from the
Glassway's red buoy to the stone at the Cairn. Its stone opens whichever memory group the count says: memories 4-6 if
it is the first stone, 7-9 (twist T5) if it is the second. (Split from S-232: memories 7-9 are S-232b.)

#### What
1. **Quest `mq-kettle`** (`js/content/quests/kettle.js`, main, `n: 16`, starts on `flag:ch14`):

   | # | id | Journal | Done when | Target | Hint nudge / answer |
   |---|---|---|---|---|---|
   | 0 | `forgefall` | "Find out why the forges went out." | Talk with Scoria (`TALK_END`) | `npc:scoria` | "Someone in Forgefall knows forges." / "Sail through the Glassway's red buoy to Kettle Isle and talk to Master Scoria at the anvil." |
   | 1 | `terraces` | "Visit the bathhouse." | Talk with Soakley | `npc:soakley` | "Follow the warm stream to the steam." / "Soakley keeps the bathhouse on the terraces south of the Kettle." |
   | 2 | `ashbeach` | "Go into the Kettle." | `flag:kettleDepths` (Pip walks into the Belly mouth with the Lantern lit) | `w:bellyMouth` | "A dark tube breathes warm air." / "Climb the switchbacks from Ash Beach; the lava-tube mouth on the spout's south side is dark. Light the Lantern and walk in." |
   | 3 | `depths` | "See what's inside the Kettle." | `item:sling` | `kettles_belly` | "Valves are slow on purpose." / "Turn a valve, then step on its brass platform before it moves." |
   | 4 | `fumarole` | "Ask the lady in the bath to move." | `boss:kettles_belly:fumarole` | r13 | "She has to fix her towel after a flop." / "Dodge her flop, then hit her while she re-ties her towel. An ember while she's up makes her drop early." |
   | 5 | `place` | "Set the Stair Stone at the Cairn." | `flag:stone:kettle` | `w:cairn.socket` | "The stone wants to go home, high up." / "Take the Stair Stone to the stone ring at the foot of the Summit Crystal Cairn." |

   Lines (`js/text/en/ke.js`, priority 70, the people steps; story's quoted phrases kept):
   - Scoria (step 0): `ke.scoria.q1` "Forges are out. Three weeks. The Kettle won't boil. It just sulks." · `ke.scoria.q2`
     "Something's stuck in the spout. Smallsmith, go and ask at the baths. Soakley notices things. Slowly."
   - Pitch (step 0, if talked to): `ke.pitch.q1` "Hot hot... no. Not hot. Nothing's hot! Even my tea's sulking, Pip-pip."
   - Soakley (step 1): `ke.soakley.q1` "The baths go cold, then scalding, then cold. No rush. It's quite lovely, in its way."
     · `ke.soakley.q2` "Lady Fumarole sits on the spout lid and won't be moved, dear, not even for towels. The way up is
     through the Kettle. The dark tube, past the switchbacks."
2. **People state**: `kettleDepths` is set by walking into the dark Belly mouth with the Lantern lit while `mq-kettle`
   is at step 2 (before that the mouth's Look says "A dark lava tube. Warm air breathes out of it. Ask around first.").
3. **The isle settles** (`stoneHeld:kettle`, set by S-231's scene; within 10 s, atlas-far#kettle-states): the plume
   steadies; the four forge houses relight one by one (0.8 s apart; glow, sparks, hammer sfx); the Hammerwheel turns 6
   rpm; the baths settle to steady warm steam; the music's `forge` layer and the settled no-wobble state turn on
   (S-224); the **lid ladder** `kettle:lid-ladder` appears (`[E] Climb · Ladder` from the lid down to the spout ridge
   (70, −5)); r13's `X` now leads to spawn `lid`; **the Kettle whistles** two notes every in-game hour (S-223b). Story
   beat: "the Kettle boils over gently into a steam plume and Forgefall's chimneys light" (the plume's 2 s burst at 2×
   size plays once at the moment of handover).
4. **Unhide**: `REALMS.kettle.hidden = false`, `REALMS.kettles_belly.hidden = false`; refresh and freeze both prints
   fixtures. The Glassway's red buoy now sails to Kettle Isle (card `card_sail_far_kettle`); after the first arrival the
   chart's Kettle button lights (atlas-far#chart: first reached only through the buoy) and benches/root-gate travel work
   as for every land. The chart's Kettle fog bank clears from `ch14` per S-203 (already) and the node becomes active on
   `realm:kettle.visited`.
5. **Stone beat**: placing `stone_kettle` runs S-212a's `stone-set` scene with `<site> = kettle`; the count decides
   the flight, the crown lantern (L6 ember `#ff9a6a` in the source-keyed scheme) and the memory group; nothing here is
   Kettle-specific. Compass while `stoneHeld:kettle`: "Set the Stair Stone · the Cairn".
6. **Reactions** (priority 30, `js/content/dialogue/home.js` and `kettle.js`): Pitch becomes the plaza visitor for
   Kettle Isle (cast: 09:00-12:00 at Miso's counter "studying the merchandise", from `stone:kettle`): `ke.pitch.plaza`
   "Miso's shop! So many swords. Master made all of them. I made... the shine. On one. A bit."
7. **Chapter tests** (S-030 format), two runs of the same spec `tests/fixtures/chapters/ch-kettle.json`:
   - **as 1st stone** — start `tests/fixtures/saves/ch-kettle-start.json` (`ch14` done, no stones; Act II tools, Paws,
     Lantern; Pip at the Glassway's red buoy aboard): sail to Kettle → people steps → enter the Belly →
     `G.debug.dungeon.solve('kettles_belly')` → `G.debug.killBoss('kettles_belly:fumarole')` or the balance bot →
     scene skip → assert the isle settled within 10 s (forge houses `lit`) → warp home `w:cairn.socket` → place →
     assert `stone:kettle = 1`, flight 1, knots 4-6 open. Writes `tests/fixtures/saves/ch-kettle-end.json` (M27's
     S-236 starts from it).
   - **as 2nd stone** — start from `ch-bell-end.json` (+ the same tools): same script → assert `stone:kettle = 2`,
     flight 2, Wenna's line 2 ("Two flights. I can almost see the top…"), knots 7-9 open, `mq-remember` offers `m789`.
     Writes `tests/fixtures/saves/ch-kettle2-end.json`.
   CI: chapter jobs `ch-kettle` and `ch-kettle-2`; the chain check (S-030) accepts both orders.
8. **Solver**: pacing rule 6 — `mq-kettle`'s critical path needs only Act II tools, the Lantern, the Paws and its own
   tool (the Sling, earned inside); no Starsong, no Gloves.

#### Assets
- `kettle_lid_ladder` (code-built, ≤ 200 tris): an iron rung ladder bolted down the cone from the lid to the ridge.
  Fallback: two `cyl` rails with `box` rungs. `card_sail_far_kettle` if not shipped (S-224 item 4).

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except "Frozen home island" (home: only reactions and the Pitch visitor spot, a record).
- `bun test`: quest lint; solver pacing rule 6; prints frozen.
- Both chapter runs pass; the end fixtures are written; the chain check accepts Bell-then-Kettle and Kettle-first.
- In-page: the settled state switch within 10 s of the handover; the hourly whistle; the lid ladder works both ways.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test`; `node tests/chapter.mjs ch-kettle` then `node tests/chapter.mjs ch-kettle-2` (one at a time);
  `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`; `bun run test:smoke`; `bun run test:playthrough`.

#### Out of scope
- Memories 7-9 and T5 (S-232b). Starsteel (S-233a). Kettle stories (M27).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: the "2nd stone" run depends on `ch-bell-end.json` staying valid; the chapter harness migrates fixtures through
`normalize` on load. Rollback: revert re-hides Kettle Isle and the Belly; a save inside them Continues at the parent's
spawn (the Glassway's Kettle buoy for `kettle`).

<a id="s-232b"></a>
### S-232b · feat: memories 7-9: he comes down, come home, and who would they wake up next to (twist T5)
Milestone M26 · Depends on: S-232a, S-212b, S-190c · Size: M (about 480 lines, mostly scene and echo data; if it passes
500 the vignette moves to S-232d, see [Index changes](#index-changes)) · Save: none · Flag: none ·
Bible: [lore#mem-07](../bible/lore.md#mem-07), [lore#mem-08](../bible/lore.md#mem-08), [lore#mem-09](../bible/lore.md#mem-09),
[lore#twists](../bible/lore.md#twists), [lore#memory-rules](../bible/lore.md#memory-rules), [story#stone-beats](../bible/story.md#stone-beats),
[story#act-III-reactions](../bible/story.md#act-III-reactions), [cast#mote](../bible/cast.md#mote),
[lore#vignette-down-the-ladder](../bible/lore.md#vignette-down-the-ladder), [lore#memory-echoes](../bible/lore.md#memory-echoes)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M26 · Kettle Isle: the forge, the Belly and Starsteel**. "Brother and sister": the second stone (Bell or
Kettle, whichever comes second) shows Mote coming down the Ladder, asking his sister to come home, and Wenna choosing
to stay: **T5**, the twist that turns Mote's whole plan into a rescue nobody asked for. The group is played, not only
watched: Pip walks down the mountain beneath Mote's light as it steps lamp to lamp to the plaza (vignette 3, Down the
Ladder), and nine echoes spread the three nights across the isle (lore#memory-hours).

#### What
1. **Data** in `js/content/memories.js`, group 3 (opens `stones>=2`, knot glow lilac `#c9b6ec`, music box very slow
   solo), three entries with every line, label, staging note and Look line copied **verbatim** from lore.md:

   | Memory | Card | When / tod | Spawn · face | Cast | Notes |
   |---|---|---|---|---|---|
   | 7 | "Memory 7 · He Comes Down" | night 6, 22:30 | spawn near (95, −52), the needle's foot, facing up the stair (the vignette); `sceneAt` near (4, 10), plaza south-east, facing east along the path to the Peaks bridge | Mote (memory variant: `npc_mote` slightly brighter, cloak patches hidden), Wenna, Keeper Tallow, five Lamplighters, four villagers at the plaza edge | the whole Ladder lit (`stair`, `landing`, `path`); the scene opens as the vignette's light stops on path lamp 142 beside the pedestal and blooms into Mote (0.4 s); `shot two at npc:mote`; line 5 Tallow leads everyone off the plaza (`go: true`) |
   | 8 | "Memory 8 · Come Home" | night 9, 22:00 | near (4, 12), facing the pedestal | Mote sitting on a lamp on the pedestal step, Wenna | plaza empty; Wenna's face `tired` at line 6 |
   | 9 | "Memory 9 · Who Would They Wake Up Next To?" | night 12, 23:00 | near (4, 12), facing the pedestal | Mote, Wenna | a few cottage windows lit, one duck asleep on the pedestal ring (`[E] Look · A duck` "Zzz."); line 5 `shot look at w:greatTree.face`; line 7 face `sad`; line 8 Mote steps onto the lamp line, walks east, and his light climbs the Starstair to the sky isle (`go: true`, then a sky streak) |

   Labels: "Mote", "Wenna", "Keeper Tallow", "A Lamplighter", "A villager". Look lines: 7 Mote "Don't look at my feet.
   I'm standing on a lamp. It's very undignified." / A Lamplighter "Not ours to hear. ...I'm not listening. I'm just
   walking very slowly."; 8 Mote "Night four. Not that I'm counting."; 9 none (the duck).
2. **Return lines** (`rd.wenna.mem07-09`, once, `wenna` frame): 7 "He came all the way down. Nobody had ever done that
   for me. Not in all the long time." · 8 "He wasn't wrong, you know. I was tired. I still am, a little. He was only
   wrong about what would help." · 9 (the myth complicated once) "The old song says I put down roots so I wouldn't fall
   any further. That was true, once. I keep them now for a different reason. You're one of the reasons, Pip."
   After all three: the one-time Taproot line `rd.wenna.trip3` "I chose. I remember now. I'm sorry I forgot. It was the
   most important thing I ever did." Sets `mem:7`-`mem:9`; `mq-remember` advances to `m101112` (which waits for
   `stones>=3`).
3. **T5 bookkeeping**: flag `t5` set on the first watch of memory 9 (the spoiler ladder's class C opens: later lore
   texts with class C may show from here, lore#spoiler-ladder). The Lore tab's Myth section gains its "complicated
   once" footnote (Wenna's return line 9) as an entry under The Myth.
4. **Reactions**: the s2 column of [story#act-III-reactions](../bible/story.md#act-III-reactions) for every row,
   verbatim, priority 30; Elder Shellby's s2 line ("She chose us, you know. I always hoped she had.") keyed to `mem:9`
   watched instead of `stones>=2` (story: "Shellby's three columns key to memories 6, 9 and 12 watched instead").
5. **Spoiler rule check**: memories 7-9 open only at `stones>=2`; a content test asserts no text with class C (T5)
   is reachable before `stones>=2` (lore#spoiler-ladder placement check).
6. **Vignette 3 · Down the Ladder** (lore#vignette-down-the-ladder, verbatim; S-190c's runner and its `lead.light`
   variant, no new mode): `vignette: { id: 'downTheLadder', mode: 'lead', when: 'before', light: { sprite:
   'fx_mote_light', set: 'path', from: 1, to: 142, ahead: [4, 10], wait: 14, vmax: 5 }, end: { x: 4, z: 10, r: 10 } }`
   with a scripted intro: a 3 s `shot look` at stair step 66, then the light comes down `stair` 66 → 1 and the four
   `landing` lamps in 10 s (each lamp it passes brightens to 1.4 for 0.6 s; `sfx.lampOn` pitched up 3 semitones, at
   most 8 per second) while Pip stands at the foot; then control returns and the light walks the path just ahead of
   him. Overheard lines (`rm.mem07.v.1-3`, once each, positional): path lamp 20 A Lamplighter (from the Peaks bridge,
   (45, 4.5)) "He's coming! He's actually coming! Somebody tell the Keeper! ...Keeper, he's coming!" · path lamp 84 Mote
   (the light itself) "A hundred and fifty-four. A hundred and fifty-five. Don't look down. There isn't a down." · path
   lamp 120 Keeper Tallow (from the plaza edge, (12, 6)) "Lamps steady, everyone. Let him come." When the light reaches
   lamp 142 with Pip within 10 m, the scene starts where Pip stands (the first-person rule is measured from `sceneAt`;
   the speakers are all within 8 m of (4, 10) or shot first). Skip: Pip placed near (4, 10) facing east, the light on
   lamp 142.
7. **Echoes for memories 7-9** (S-190b's runtime), verbatim from the **Echoes** tables under lore#mem-07, #mem-08 and
   #mem-09 (text ids `rm.mem0N.echo.<k>`): 7: A Lamplighter near (57, 2) "Two hundred and twelve lamps. He stepped on
   every one. Not one cracked. Good lamps."; A villager near (−22.5, −4) at a window; Young Wick near (96, −48) "He
   walked down on my lamp. My lamp. Hm. Hm." · 8: Keeper Tallow near (95, −47) with a slate of sums; A Lamplighter near
   (−104, −2) yawning; A villager near (−13, 21) feeding ducks · 9: A villager asleep on cottage 3's sill near (−15.9,
   10); Young Pinch near (9, 115) hanging a lamp on the lighthouse door; A Lamplighter near (96, −48) turning the bottom
   lamp down low. Memory 9's echoes carry no class-C text (they only show the night around the scene); the content
   test covers them.

#### Assets
- Memory 7's lamp line uses S-190c's `stair`, `landing` and `path` sets of instanced Ladder lamps. Mote's memory
  variant: `npc_mote` with `emissive × 1.15` and the patch decal hidden (if the patches are a separate node; if baked
  in, leave them: lore#memory-extras allows it).
- `fx_mote_light` (the light walking down the Ladder): **generate** a 256² alpha image, prompt: art-audio preamble +
  `fx` suffix + "a small warm gold star-light, a soft round glow with a tiny bright core and a faint trailing shimmer,
  soft painterly storybook style, transparent background. Palette: #fff1c4 #ffd27a #ffe9a8."; process with
  `tools/export_sprites.py` to webp ≤ 14 KB; manifest key `fx_mote_light` (`fx`, `remembered`, lazy). Fallback: canvas
  radial gradient `#fff1c4` → `#ffd27a` → transparent with a 2 px white core. Original art.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all.
- `bun test`: lint over `rm.mem07-09.*` (≤ 220 characters, allowed labels, no "Shellby" in memory lines, Mote and Wenna
  only as labelled); every scene ≤ 90 s of script time; the FP speaker rule (within 8 m and the view cone, or a shot
  first) over the scene data; the class-C placement test.
- In-page (`stones`, case `memories-789`): from `ch-kettle2-end.json`, touch knots 7, 8, 9 (any order), each plays,
  skips cleanly and returns with its line; the triple line plays once; `t5` set; `mq-remember` at `m101112`.
- In-page (`stones`, case `down-the-ladder`): memory 7 spawns Pip at (95, −52); the light comes down 66 stair and 4
  landing lamps in 10 s ± 1; with the bot warping along the path in 3 m steps, the light stays 4-10 m ahead, stops
  when the bot stands still more than 14 m behind it, and never exceeds 5 m/s; the three overheard lines fire once
  each in order; at lamp 142 the scene starts; skip places Pip near (4, 10).
- Each of memories 7-9 shows its three echoes; Look shows the lore line verbatim and sets `seen:echo:mNN.k`; echo lint
  and placement pass for the nine rows.
- Budget: the view from the needle's foot down the lit Ladder toward the village (212 lamps, the light) stays ≤ 350
  draw calls and ≤ 1.3 M tris.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test`; `node tests/inpage.mjs stones`; `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`; `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope
- Memories 10-12 (S-254b, M28) and vignette 4 (S-254d). New vignette modes (S-190c's `lead` is enough).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: memory 9's final walk (Mote climbing the Starstair) must read from the plaza in first person; the scene adds a
`look` shot at the sky isle for 2 s before control returns. Rollback: revert; knots 7-9 say "still tying itself" and
the `mem:*` flags stay valid.
