<a id="s-232c"></a>

### S-232c · feat: The Boil-Over: after Fumarole, Pip relights Forgefall's three forges with the Ember Sling through the steam

Milestone M26 · Depends on: S-232a, S-231, S-227a, S-091b · Size: M (about 420 lines) · Save: none · Flag: none ·
Bible: [../bible/story.md#set-pieces](../bible/story.md#set-pieces), [../bible/story.md#loop-variations](../bible/story.md#loop-variations),
[../bible/story.md#ch15-17](../bible/story.md#ch15-17), [../bible/atlas-far.md#kettle](../bible/atlas-far.md#kettle),
[../bible/systems.md#items-tools](../bible/systems.md#items-tools)

Added in review (see [Index changes](#index-changes)). Filed **after** S-232a (Kettle Isle is open), so it changes
the handover's timing and adds a `before` sub-objective to `mq-kettle`'s step `place` instead of inserting a step.

#### Why

Part of **Kettle Isle: the forge, the Belly and Starsteel**. Kettle Isle's set piece and its loop variation in one:
the Warden no longer hands the stone over at the end of the dungeon. Lady Fumarole warned that pulling the stone with
nowhere for the heat to go would boil the isle over, and it does: steam fills Forgefall, and Pip must relight three
cold forges with the Ember Sling, out in the overworld, after the dungeon, before she hands the stone over at the
bathhouse. 15-25 minutes, no fail.

#### What

1. **Handover change** (S-231's `stone-give-kettle`): after `ke.fumarole.h1`/`h2`, the fight's end plays a new scene
   `kettle-boil` (16 s) instead: `ke.fumarole.b1` "And now you've cooled me down, the heat's got nowhere to go at all.
   Hear that? That's the Kettle, darling. Boiling over." · `fx.plumeBurst` (the spout plume at 2× for 3 s, S-223's
   burst) · `ke.fumarole.b2` "Light the forges in Forgefall. Three of them. Give the heat somewhere to go, then come
   and find me at the baths." `after: ['boss:kettles_belly:fumarole', 'set:ke:boil', 'quest:mq-kettle.advance']`. The
   stone is **not** given here. `stone-give-kettle` (unchanged lines; "The forges are hot again…" now fits literally)
   plays at the bathhouse instead (item 5).
2. **Data** (`mq-kettle`, step `place`): `before: [{ id: 'forges', until: 'flag:ke:forgesLit', minutes: 15, text:
   'ke.q.forges', short: 'ke.q.forges.short', target: 'w:kettle.forge-next', hint: ['ke.q.forges.n', 'ke.q.forges.a']
   }, { id: 'baths', until: 'item:stone_kettle', minutes: 4, text: 'ke.q.baths', short: 'ke.q.baths.short', target:
   'npc:fumarole', hint: ['ke.q.baths.n', 'ke.q.baths.a'] }]`. Texts: "Relight Forgefall's forges ({n}/3)." · "Relight
   the forges" · "The forge mouths are shuttered against the steam." / "Swing at a forge's shutter lever, then shoot an
   ember into its mouth with {tool} before the shutter drops." · "See Lady Fumarole at the baths." · "Lady Fumarole" ·
   "She said she'd be at the baths." / "Soakley's Bathhouse is on the terraces south of the Kettle."
3. **The boil-over state** (`ke:boil` and not `ke:forgesLit`; Kettle realm only): Forgefall's fog tightens to near 10 m
   / far 50 m (`#f2ece4` steam); 12 street vents (Kettle post-pass `placeSetPiece`, `new RNG(40261 * 109 + 23)`,
   rows `kettle:boilvent-1..12` on Forgefall's walkable street cells, ≥ 6 m apart, none within 3 m of a door) puff on
   timers: a 0.8 s drawn ring on the ground, then a 1.2 s column that pushes Pip 2 m outward and does **no damage**.
   The smiths stand in doorways coughing politely; Scoria: "Shutters down on all of them. Steam gets in a cold forge
   and it'll never draw again. Ember through the mouth, quick, then the shutter drops." The Hammerwheel stops.
4. **Three forges** (S-223's forge houses, `grep -rn "forgehouse\|forge_house" js/realms/`; the three that are not
   Scoria's at `w:forge` (−152, −50)): each gets a shutter lever beside its door (`[E] Use` does nothing; a Swing
   throws it) and a forge mouth 2.4 m up behind an iron shutter. Throwing the lever lifts the shutter for **4 s**
   (a ticking `sfx.ratchet` and the shutter's chain visibly unwinding); an ember from the Sling (S-227a's `brazier`
   target kind) through the open mouth lights the forge: a roar, sparks out of the chimney, the forge's glow, the
   smith inside cheers, `set:ke:forge<k>`, `inc:ke:forges`. Each forge's firing spot is across the street, so the shot
   crosses a vent's lane (time the puff). Forge 3 is reached by the stair to its loft balcony, the only clear line to
   its mouth. A missed shot just bounces (no penalty).
5. **All three** (scene `kettle-forges`, 12 s): the camera rises over Forgefall; the steam drains up the three
   chimneys; the Hammerwheel turns again; the fog lifts; `set:ke:forgesLit`. Fumarole moves to the bathhouse door
   (−10, 82) and Talk there plays S-231's `stone-give-kettle` (gives `stone_kettle`, `stoneHeld:kettle`). S-232a's
   "isle settles" item (forge houses relight one by one, the fourth, Scoria's, last) now runs on `ke:forgesLit` for the
   three and on `stoneHeld:kettle` for Scoria's.
6. **Saves**: a save already holding or having set the stone (`stoneHeld:kettle` or `stone:kettle`) loads with
   `ke:boil` and `ke:forgesLit` set; nothing is taken back.
7. **Chapter tests** `ch-kettle` and `ch-kettle-2` (S-232a's spec): after the boss, `G.debug.ke.lightForges()` (lever
   and Sling shot per forge with the real interactions) then `talk fumarole` at the bathhouse; expect `ke:forgesLit`
   and `stoneHeld:kettle` before the Cairn. **Debug:** `G.debug.ke.lightForges()`, `G.debug.ke.boil(on)`.
8. **Flags**: `ke:boil`, `ke:forge1..3`, `ke:forges` (counter), `ke:forgesLit` (story).

#### Assets

- Shutter and lever: code-built (`box` iron shutter `#4a4a52`, lever `cyl` + `sphere` knob `#c2563f`), ≤ 120 tris each,
  added to S-223's forge-house record. Vent ring: canvas decal; steam column: the shipped steam card.
- No generated art.

#### Acceptance criteria

- Calming Fumarole no longer gives the stone; it starts the boil-over (steam, vents, stopped wheel). Three
  lever-then-Sling beats relight three forges; the fog lifts; the stone is given at the bathhouse; the Cairn step
  follows unchanged.
- Vents never damage, always draw their ring 0.8 s ahead, and never trap Pip (in-page check: a bot walking the street
  for 60 s is never pushed off a walkable cell).
- A save past the stone loads unchanged. `ch-kettle` and `ch-kettle-2` pass.
- Invariants: *Frozen realms* (post-pass rows only), *Tone* (no fail; nothing earned is taken back), *Budgets per
  view*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

```sh
bun test tests/quests.test.js tests/content.test.js tests/realm-kettle.test.js && bun test
free -h && node tests/inpage.mjs kettle
node tests/chapter.mjs ch-kettle && node tests/chapter.mjs ch-kettle-2
```

#### Out of scope

Starsteel (S-233a), the Belly itself (S-229, S-230), Kettle's Hollows and Dash (S-234).

#### Risk & rollback

Changes a shipped handover's timing. Rollback: revert; S-231's scene gives the stone at the lid again.

<a id="s-233a"></a>
### S-233a · feat: side story 38 Starsteel: sword level 3, forged by Master Scoria; save v11
Milestone M26 · Depends on: S-232a, S-013, S-199 · Size: M (about 430 lines) · Save: **v10 → v11** · Flag: none ·
Bible: [side-content#ss-38](../bible/side-content.md#ss-38), [systems#items-tools](../bible/systems.md#items-tools)
(Swords), [systems#save-v11](../bible/systems.md#save-v11), [systems#save-plan](../bible/systems.md#save-plan),
[art-audio#hood-trims](../bible/art-audio.md#hood-trims), [lore#lamp-logs](../bible/lore.md#lamp-logs)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M26 · Kettle Isle: the forge, the Belly and Starsteel**. The last sword: Master Scoria, who forged every
sword Miso sells and never one for a hero, forges Starsteel from three shards of star-metal that fell in the Storm.
It is the game's biggest one-time gem sink (600) and the key to thornglass in every land (S-233b). (Split from S-233.)

#### What
1. **Save v11** (`js/save.js`): `SAVE_VERSION = 11`; `MIGRATIONS[10] = (s) => ({ ...s })` (identity on data: no field
   moves or changes meaning); `normalize`: `out.swordLevel = clampInt(s.swordLevel, 1, 3, 1)` replacing today's
   `>= 2 ? 2 : 1` (a v10 save with level 2 stays 2; a missing or garbage value becomes 1; 3 is now kept). The `-bak`
   copy before migration is kept (S-003's backup rule). Tests in `tests/save.test.js`: v6, v7, v8, v9 and v10 fixtures
   migrate to v11 with every field equal except `v`; `swordLevel` 3 round-trips; 4 clamps to 3; 0 and `"x"` to 1; the
   v6 Continue fixture in `test:smoke` still loads.
2. **Combat and player** (`js/combat.js`, `js/player.js`, `js/data.js`): damage by level from a table `SWORD = { 1:
   { ab: 1, c: 2, reach: CFG.reachWood }, 2: { ab: 2, c: 3, reach: CFG.reachShiny }, 3: { ab: 3, c: 4, reach: 2.2 } }`
   (systems: 1/1/2 1.6 m, 2/2/3 2.0 m, 3/3/4 2.2 m; `resolveSword` reads `SWORD[level].ab + (C ? TC.dmgC : 0)` so level
   1 and 2 stay byte-identical in behaviour: verify `TC.dmgC === 1`); `player.setSword(3)`: the Shiny Sword mesh stays
   visible (level ≥ 2) with the **Starsteel look**: a material tint `#cfe8ff` on the blade and an emissive edge
   `#8fd8ff` at 0.6 on the sword mesh only (Pip's `hero.glb` untouched; the art-audio hood-trim rule: tints in third
   person apply to the sword node only); the trail colour `#bfe8ff`; a faint hum (`sfx.starsteelHum`, 2 s loop at −28 dB
   while drawn in FP). `CFG.reachStar = 2.2`. The shop's "sword" item never downgrades: buying the Shiny Sword is only
   offered while `swordLevel < 2` (today's rule).
3. **ss-38 Starsteel** (`js/content/quests/kettle.js`, `n: 138`, owner `scoria`, unlock `flag:stone:kettle` (side
   content's `stone.kettle`), reward the Starsteel Sword; cost 600 gems):
   - `ask` "Master Scoria's offer": `scoria.1` "I forged every sword Miso sells. Never forged one for a hero. Bring me
     starsteel and I'll put that right."
   - `shards` "Find three starsteel shards (n/3)" (key items `starsteel_shard_a|b|c`, kind `delivery`, a blue-white
     sliver icon), parallel:
     (a) **buried on Ash Beach**: a soft-earth mound with a blue sparkle at (145, 85) (Kettle `placeSideMarks` `new
     RNG(40261 * 107 + 19)`, no draws; `[T] Dig · Soft earth`, Paws);
     (b) **stuck high on the Spout's rim**: a glinting shard on the Mouth's north lip at (114, −22), y 30.5 (a
     `bell_target`-style ember target; an ember hit knocks it down to the lip, `[E] Pick up · Starsteel shard`);
     (c) **the Kettle's Belly's `r12:c4` chest**: it gives the shard with its 40 gems (dungeons#d8's "starsteel" chest;
     the gem-chest count and ledger are unchanged). Because the chest may have been opened before ss-38 existed, shard
     (c) counts as found when `S.dungeons.kettles_belly.chests['r12:c4']` is set, and the item is granted on the first
     Talk with Scoria after `ask` ("Found that in the Belly, did you? Good.").
   - `pay` "Pay for the forging (600 gems)": Talk → the shop-style confirm (reuse the shop UI's confirm row): "Forge the
     Starsteel Sword? 600 gems." With fewer than 600: `scoria.2` "Six hundred gems. For the coal. I'd do it for free,
     but the coal won't." (no charge). With 600: the gems are taken.
   - `forge` "Watch the Starsteel Sword forged": scene `ss38-forge` (25 s, in the forge interior `scoria_forge`,
     `replay: true`): hammer rhythm on beats (the music cue: the `kettle` track's `forge` layer with 8 anvil hits), Pitch
     on the bellows (`ke.pitch.ss38` "Hot hot HOT!"), a blue-white flash; item-get "The Starsteel Sword! Cuts thornglass,
     reaches further, hums a little." → `S.swordLevel = 3`; `scoria.3` "There. Starsteel. It'll cut thornglass and it'll
     hum when it's happy. Mind you keep it happy."
   - World marks: the forge window flashes blue-white once each night (atlas-far#kettle-states `ss-38 done`); Scoria's
     cooling room shows the empty starsteel mould on its wall. Miso (home, after): `miso.ss38` "Mrrow~ Is that a Scoria?
     A STARSTEEL Scoria? ...I'm not jealous. I'm a shopkeeper. It's different~". Journal lines verbatim.
4. **Economy**: the 600 gem sink in the ledger's one-time sinks; the ledger test stays ≥ 105 %. Lamp Log 17 "Forgefall"
   (lore) already foreshadows the star-splinter forge; no text change.
5. **Bestiary / HUD**: the Items tab's sword row shows "Starsteel Sword · 3 / 3 / 4 · reach 2.2 m · cuts thornglass".

#### Assets
- `starsteel_shard` (code-built, ≤ 40 tris): a blue-white crystal sliver `#e8f6ff` with an emissive core `#8fd8ff`;
  icon cell `starsteel_shard` (preamble + `icon` + "a small sliver of blue-white star-metal glowing faintly at its
  core"; fallback canvas sliver).
- Starsteel look: a material change only (no new mesh). ART BRIEF note for the owner's later sword GLB (none needed:
  the LOCKED Shiny Sword shape is kept).

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all, including **one save slot**: `SAVE_VERSION` 11, `MIGRATIONS[10]`, tests from every older
  version, the `-bak` copy; no v6 field moves or changes meaning (only `swordLevel`'s allowed range widens).
- `bun test`: save tests above; combat damage per level 1/1/2, 2/2/3, 3/3/4 (unit test drives `resolveSword` with a
  stub enemy); reach 2.2 at level 3; the shop never sells a downgrade; ledger.
- `bun run test:smoke` (the v6 Continue fixture) and `bun run test:playthrough` (Act I with level 1 and 2) unchanged.
- In-page (`side`, case `starsteel`): from `ch-kettle-end.json`, dig shard (a), shoot shard (b), shard (c) from the
  chest (and the retro grant when the chest was already open), refusal under 600, payment at 600, the scene, level 3
  in both cameras with the tint and hum.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test`; `bun run test:smoke`; `bun run test:playthrough`; `node tests/inpage.mjs side`;
  `node tests/inpage.mjs bosses` (no boss changed behaviour at level 1-2).

#### Out of scope
- Thornglass thickets (S-233b).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: the save bump is the one irreversible part: a v11 save opened by a reverted v10 build is rejected by `migrate`
(newer than `SAVE_VERSION`) and the `-bak` v10 copy is offered (S-003's rule). Rollback plan: do not revert alone;
ship a forward fix. Before merge, the PR body shows the v6-v10 → v11 test output.

<a id="s-233b"></a>
### S-233b · feat: thornglass thickets in every shipped land, cut only with Starsteel
Milestone M26 · Depends on: S-233a, S-074 · Size: M (about 380 lines) · Save: none · Flag: none ·
Bible: [systems#gates](../bible/systems.md#gates), [atlas-home#thornglass](../bible/atlas-home.md#thornglass),
[atlas-isles#kit-thornglass](../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-thornglass), [atlas-isles#passes](../bible/atlas-isles.md#passes),
[atlas-far#far-passes](../bible/atlas-far.md#far-passes), [systems#brief-gates](../bible/systems.md#brief-gates),
[systems#economy](../bible/systems.md#economy)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M26 · Kettle Isle: the forge, the Belly and Starsteel**. Starsteel reopens every land: glassy thickets
guard optional caches on home, the four isles, the Rootdeep, the Glassway and Kettle Isle (pacing rule 5: every tool
opens home finds and re-opens ≥ 3 finds in earlier lands).

#### What
1. **Gate** `thornglass` (`js/gates.js`, `needs: 'ability:sword3'`): a ring thicket like thornvine (`solid: { r }` =
   the thicket radius, 1.25-2.0 m) of glassy pale-teal thorn stems 1.4 m tall, 2.5-4 m across; **3 sword hits** at
   level 3 shatter it into soft sparkles that fade (12 sparkle quads) leaving a stump ring; flag `thorn:<realm>:<id>`.
   Lower swords: a glassy "tink" and "Too tough for this sword." FP cue: a glassy tinkle within 8 m. Opaque toon glass
   with rim light (no transparency sort).
2. **Placements** (each pass additive on its own stream; prints equal; nothing else moves):

   | Realm | Pass | Thickets → what each guards |
   |---|---|---|
   | home | row 22 `placeThornglass` `new RNG(7151 * 167 + 101)` | `home:thornglass-woods` `near((−20, −118), 0, 12)` Woods north beach behind the Hollow → 30-gem cache; `home:thornglass-meadow` `near((−118, 20), 0, 12)` the west coast → 30-gem cache |
   | duskwick | `placeIsleThornglass` `new RNG(40211 * 73 + 9)` | top terrace north end of Coral Steps (120, 4) r 8 → `duskwick:chest-10` (**swap** S-086's temporary coral-rock collider for the thicket) |
   | pinewhistle | same rule (40223) | Whistle Rise north slope (150, −170) r 8 → `pinewhistle:chest-14` |
   | archwater | same rule (40231) | NW lowland (−150, −70) r 8 → `archwater:acorn-8` |
   | threemills | same rule (40237) | a Long Lane hedge gap (−120, 40) r 8 → `threemills:chest-14` |
   | rootdeep | far `placeThornglass` `new RNG(40241 * 79 + 7)` | (−120, −120) Glowcap west, (90, −135) Glowcap east, (170, 40) the G2-G4 tunnel nook, (−215, −10) Burrowbottom's far west corner → four 30-gem caches |
   | glassway | far `placeThornglass` `new RNG(40253 * 79 + 7)` | Tern Tuft (−200, −140), Mabel Rock (250, −20), Bell Islet (−35, 200) → three 30-gem caches |
   | kettle | far `placeThornglass` `new RNG(40261 * 79 + 7)` | (−175, 10), (−5, 125), (170, 20), (40, −140) → four 30-gem caches |

   Caches are `S.chests['<realm>:thornglass-<n>']` (small wooden cache boxes, the S-013 cache model) and count in their
   land's chests row (systems decision 17); the isle finds named above already exist and only gain the thicket. If the
   isle's finds step placed its find behind an inert placeholder, replace the placeholder's `needs` with
   `thorn:<realm>:<id>` (as S-208 did for star doors). Atlas-far decision 31 leaves "what thornglass guards" to
   systems#gates, which names no prize: this step sets them as **30-gem caches** (home's amount).
3. **Economy**: home 60 + Rootdeep 120 + Glassway 90 + Kettle 120 = **390 gems** added to the fixed sources (if
   systems#economy's ledger already lists a thornglass amount per land, use its amounts and keep these positions);
   the ledger test stays ≥ 105 %.
4. **Solver**: `sword3` reopens 2 home finds and ≥ 3 earlier-land finds (Duskwick, Pinewhistle, Archwater,
   Threemills, Rootdeep, Glassway); the report table gains the `sword3` row.
5. **Completion**: regenerate `js/realms/stats.js` (chests rows grow per land).

#### Assets
- `kit_thornglass` (code-built, ≤ 700 tris; fallback a cluster of 14 thin cones `#9fd6cf`): palette stems `#9fd6cf`
  (core `#e8fbf7`), base `#5d7f7a`, sparkle `#ffffff`. ART BRIEF verbatim from
  [atlas-isles#kit-thornglass](../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-thornglass) with the image prompt from systems#brief-gates
  ("a bush made of pale teal crystal shards pointing up and out, gentle sparkle, storybook, transparent background")
  and 3D prompt "stylised thicket of glassy pale-teal thorny stems, 1.4 m tall, 3 m across, crystalline but soft, toon
  shading with rim light". Commented-out `models` slot `kit_thornglass`.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all. Home prints byte-identical; row 22 changes nothing else on seeds 1-40; each realm's prints
  equal (every pass's "changes nothing else" test).
- `bun test`: placements deterministic and reachable (the cache inside each ring is reachable only after the cut:
  solver); `sword3` report; ledger; stats.
- In-page (`tools`, case `thornglass`): at level 2 a thicket refuses with the line; at level 3 three hits shatter it,
  the flag persists after reload, the cache opens; Duskwick's chest-10 rock is gone and the thicket stands in its
  place.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test` (world test row 22; realm tests for the 7 other realms); `node tests/inpage.mjs tools`;
  `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`; `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope
- Thornglass on Saltglass Flats, Driftholm and later lands (their finds steps).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: swapping Duskwick's temporary rock changes a collider in a frozen realm; it is a post-pass record swap (the
rock was a post record of S-086), so prints are equal. Rollback: revert; thornglass flags and caches paid stay in the
save; the thickets vanish (the caches behind them become open, which only helps).

<a id="s-234"></a>
### S-234 · feat: Kettle Isle's two Hollows, its Dash course and Rook's race part 4
Milestone M26 · Depends on: S-228, S-073, S-154 · Size: M (about 340 lines) · Save: none · Flag: none ·
Bible: [dungeons#hollows](../bible/dungeons.md#hollows), [dungeons#h19](../bible/dungeons.md#h19), [dungeons#h20](../bible/dungeons.md#h20),
[side-content#act-dash](../bible/side-content.md#act-dash), [side-content#ss-49](../bible/side-content.md#ss-49),
[atlas-far#kettle-pois](../bible/atlas-far.md#kettle-pois), [cast#rook](../bible/cast.md#rook)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M26 · Kettle Isle: the forge, the Belly and Starsteel**. The isle's last finds and two timed runs: a
Sling puzzle (heart petal), a steam-platform puzzle (gem bag), Clover's eighth Dash course and Rook's fourth race.

#### What
1. **Hollow 19 "The Cinder Hollow"** `hollow_kettle_1`, door `hollow_kettle_sling` (130, 140) on Ash Beach (the lava
   tube of S-222). Map verbatim from H19: four **high** braziers (1,4), (9,4), (1,8), (9,8) and a high bell target
   `q` (5,6); light all four and ring the bell with embers → the hidden bridge `=` (5,2)-(5,3) rises to the **heart
   petal** `+` (5,1). Wave: Ash Moth ×2. Hint "Fire up high." / "Fling embers at the four high braziers and the little
   bell."
2. **Hollow 20 "The Kettle-Steam Hollow"** `hollow_kettle_2`, door `hollow_kettle_steam` (60, 75), a steam grotto under
   the top terrace. Map verbatim from H20: gem bag `*` (9,1) on a ledge (digit 9); platform `z` (8,3) rises 0 → 9 by
   valve `v` (2,8), too far to reach in 2 s: stand on the platform and fling an ember at the valve. Wave: Steam Puff ×2.
   Prize **60 gems**. Hint "Some valves are best turned from afar." / "Stand on the brass platform and fling an ember at
   the valve."
3. Both: `kind: 'hollow'`, grid 48 m, walls `tex_kettle_rock`, `collect.hollow['hollow_kettle_1'|'hollow_kettle_2']` (realm ids) and the H19
   petal `collect.petal['hollow_kettle_1:p1']`; solved doors
   glow gold; Completion "Hollows" for Kettle Isle = 2; solver per Hollow.
4. **Kettle Dash** (course 8, `collect.dash['dash:kettle']`, tenths of a second): start post at the jetty (−195, −20) → under the Handle
   (−85, −10) → the terraces' west stair (−60, 95) → the beach path (60, 120) → finish at the Teacup causeway
   (165, 105); about 430 m, **12 rings** placed every ~36 m along that line at ground + 1.2 m (one ring above the
   Steam Hop vent at (−50, 110) at + 4 m: the Leaf Glider over one vent is the fast line). Par `P` recorded by the
   careful bot; medals and tier gems as every course (bronze 15, silver 20, gold a ribbon `#e66f4f` on Clover's
   windowsill). Rings and posts by Kettle `placeSideMarks` (no draws).
5. **Rook's Races part 4** (ss-49 step `race4`): unlocks on `realm:kettle.visited & ss49.race3`. Rook at the Forgefall
   gate (the Forgefall road's east edge (−70, 60), spot `kettle.rook`); `[E] Talk · Rook` → 3-2-1 → Rook flies a fixed
   ghost spline (no collisions) from (−70, 60) up the terraces on the Steam Hop vents to the Spout Mouth's lip (115,
   −15) y 28 ("the Kettle's rim"); about 500 m. Rook's time = the careful-bot time × **1.15**; the best line glides on
   the steam vents (Leaf Glider). Win: 8 gems, a small flag at the finish "Pip was here first. —R. (grudgingly)", line
   "Steam vents?! I was going to use those! ...I was going to think of using those." Lose (cycles): "Ha! Rematch? Go
   on. I'll even pretend to stretch." / "Finders keepers! ...Again?" Timed runs pause the spawner.
6. **Economy**: Hollow gem bag 60 + Dash tiers 35 + Rook 8 in the ledger.

#### Assets
- Hollow walls `tex_kettle_rock`; braziers/bell targets/valve/platform reuse S-227a and S-229 models. Rook's flag:
  the S-113 flag prop. No new generated asset.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except "Frozen home island". Kettle prints equal.
- `bun test`: both Hollow maps equal the bible; each solver proves its prize and no soft-lock (H20: the platform is
  reachable and the valve is in line of sight from it within 25 m).
- In-page (`dungeons`: `hollow_kettle_1/2`; `activities`: `dash-kettle`, `rook-4`): solve both; the bot records `P`;
  Rook's ghost time = 1.15 × the bot time; a sprint-and-glide bot run beats Rook.
- Completion: Kettle's Hollows row 2; petals +1.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test`; `node tests/inpage.mjs dungeons`; `node tests/inpage.mjs activities`; `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope
- Steam Hop and the Sling Gallery (M27, S-237). Rook part 5 (Saltglass).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: the Steam Hop vents' puff phase (2.4 s) makes the Dash par vary; the bot waits for the puff, and the recorded par
includes that wait. Rollback: revert; collect flags ignored.

<a id="s-234b"></a>

### S-234b · feat: Kettle Isle's Hollows get their second stage, and The Kettle-Steam Hollow becomes a Deep Hollow

Milestone M26 · Depends on: S-234, S-090b · Size: M (200-500; mostly map data) · Save: none · Flag: the land's Hollow realms keep the land's hidden flag · Bible: [../bible/dungeons.md#hollows](../bible/dungeons.md#hollows), [../bible/dungeons.md#h20](../bible/dungeons.md#h20), [../bible/lore.md#lamp-logs](../bible/lore.md#lamp-logs), [../bible/story.md#motes-traces](../bible/story.md#motes-traces)

Added by the playtime review (dungeons.md §7 and R15, CANON §11.1 A3; see [Index changes](#index-changes)). Filed after S-234.

#### Why
Roadmap item **M26 · Kettle Isle: the forge, the Belly and Starsteel**. Every Hollow was a one-step room that played in 2-3 minutes and all 24 shared one
shape. Now every Hollow has two stages with the prize behind the second, and one Hollow per land is a three-room
**Deep Hollow** (15-20 min) that ends in a reading nook with a Lamp Log and a note from the Lamp Thief, so the
optional bucket of CANON §11 is real play and each land has one hidden place worth remembering.

#### What
1. **The reliquary** (if S-093b has not added it yet, this step does; later steps reuse it): entity `reliquary: { when:
   [...] }` on a Hollow's prize tile; a code-built cage of four curved roots `#6f5a44` with gold buds `#ffe9a8`, 1.0 m,
   ≤ 120 tris; it opens in 1.0 s with `chime_up` when its condition holds; its state lives in
   `S.dungeons['<hollow>'].stage` until the prize is taken, then is dropped (the shipped Hollow rule). The solver proves
   stage 1, then stage 2, then the prize.
2. **Stage 2 for H19 The Cinder Hollow** (copy each Hollow's **Stage 2** line from [dungeons.md#hollows](../bible/dungeons.md#hollows)):
   - **H19 The Cinder Hollow** (`hollow_kettle_1`): the petal sits under a soft-earth mound at (5,1); dig it with the Paws; condition `dug`.
3. **The Deep Hollow H20 The Kettle-Steam Hollow** (`hollow_kettle_2`): replace its one-room data with the 31 × 11 composite map
   from [dungeons.md#h20](../bible/dungeons.md#h20) (copy, never paraphrase; realm grid 78 m). Room 1 is the old
   puzzle (stage 1) with a door (10,1) where its prize stood. **Room 2 The Boiler Stair**: fling an ember at the valve (18,2) across the pit; 2.0 s later five platforms (13-17,5) rise into a bridge; cross; melt the wax-plug door `f` (20,9) with one ember. **Room 3 The Reading
   Nook**: the prize on its pedestal (25,1); **Lamp Log 24** on a page pinned to stone (23,3) (`Read · Lamp Log`;
   it moves here from its atlas slot in Kettle Isle: edit that land's finds data so the slot is skipped, update the
   atlas row and run `node tools/realm-stats.mjs`; land and game totals unchanged); a scrap jam-jar lamp (`prop_scrap_lamp`
   from S-090b, or its fallback) holding a folded note (27,3), `[E] Read · Note`: "Warm enough to sleep. Too warm to stay. Borrowed a little heat. Gave it back. — M" (text id
   `lore.hollow.kettle`, ≤ 220 characters; it never names him, the charter spoiler rule; reading it adds the Lore
   entry "A note in a Hollow" and sets `trace:hollow:kettle`; if story.md's traces define this land's note, its
   text wins); and its own exit `X` (25,10) back to the spiral door.
4. **Hints** (Ask the Tree): one pair per stage-2 line (nudge = the condition's object, plain = the line), and for the
   Deep Hollow's room 2: "The valve is on the far side. So is the door." / "Fling an ember at the far valve, cross the risen platforms and melt the wax door.".
5. `STATS` is regenerated (`node tools/realm-stats.mjs`): same Hollow ids, same prizes, the log's new position.

#### Assets
- `int_reliquary` (code-built as above; ART BRIEF: a small cage of four curved roots with gold buds over a stone
  pedestal; 1.0 m; palette `#6f5a44` `#8a5a3c` `#ffe9a8`; toon bark; 120 / 500 tris; it opens like a flower; image prompt
  "<style line> a small cage of four curved tree roots with tiny gold buds over a mossy stone pedestal, cosy"; 3D prompt
  "stylised low-poly root cage over a stone pedestal, hand-painted, under 500 triangles, 1.0 m"). Fallback: four bent
  cylinders. The Deep Hollow reuses the land's Hollow kit and the scrap lamp; no new images.

#### Acceptance criteria
- `tests/dungeon-maps.test.js` passes with the edited Hollow blocks (16 `+` and 8 `*` still counted across the Hollows);
  data equals the bible blocks.
- The solver proves each Hollow's two stages and the Deep Hollow's three rooms in order, with no soft-lock, using only
  the tools listed in the Hollow table.
- In page: a bot solves each stage-2 Hollow and the Deep Hollow; reading the note sets `trace:hollow:kettle`; the
  Lamp Log counts once; prizes and `collect.hollow` keys are unchanged.
- Content lint (≤ 220 characters, no spoiler name). Invariants: keep *Frozen realms* (Hollow realms only; the land's
  overworld prints are untouched except the log slot, refreshed in this PR before the land is unhidden or, if it is
  already unhidden, by a finds-data change that the prints test lists), *One save slot*, *Tone*, *Assets*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `agentctl heavy --job <job> -- bun test tests/dungeon-maps.test.js tests/stats.test.js tests/content.test.js`.
- `agentctl heavy --job <job> -- node tests/inpage.mjs dungeons` (new case `hollows_kettle`).

#### Out of scope
- New prizes or Hollows; the land's other finds.

#### Risk & rollback
Data changes inside Hollow realms plus one log slot. Revert the PR: the one-stage Hollows and the log's atlas slot come
back.
